# Main Guide v1

This is a self-contained route guide. It tells you what to do, when to do it, what to save before, and which important completion states change as you play. You should not need to consult the objective spreadsheet while playing; the spreadsheet is used behind the scenes to verify completeness.

Draft note: The route sections through `Final Reconciliation` are player-facing v1 text.

## Guide Conventions

* Keep Survival Mode on unless this guide explicitly records a user-approved exception.
* When the guide names a Survival Mode exception, do only the named action while Survival Mode is off, then turn Survival Mode back on immediately before travel, combat, sleep, crafting, or unrelated interaction.
* Keep Legendary difficulty on.
* Keep rotating manual saves throughout the run.
* Named saves are written as `HARD SAVE: HS-NAME`.
* Branches are played first from the named hard save, then the save is reloaded and the canonical main-route choice is made.
* Keep the completion checklist open while playing. Whenever the guide tells you to complete a quest, discover or clear a location, learn a spell or shout, read or take a tracked book, acquire a unique item or set, gain a power, unlock a follower, buy property, obtain a pet or mount, finish a favor, or perform another tracked completion action, update the matching checklist row yourself.
* The guide does not repeat routine checklist prompts for things the game makes obvious. If you did the named action and the game records or awards it, log it.
* Explicit route bookkeeping appears only when the state is not obvious from normal play: counter totals, branch-experienced rows, conditional availability, randomized radiant results, and similar route-control notes.
* Branch checklist rows should be recorded as branch-experienced, not kept as the final main-save world state.
* When the guide gives a counter total, record that total in your notes or checklist immediately. Treat the counter as complete when the route reaches the named target.
* Do not read skill books casually. Collect the selected copy when routed, but read skill books after Scholar's Insight is active unless this guide names a deliberate exception.
* Do not disenchant unique items to learn enchantments.
* Do not enter, start, claim, or report any leveled reward gate before the named level threshold.
* Sleep in a proper bed before planned level-ups, major crafting, hard fights, cold expeditions, and long dungeon chains.

**Route Discipline:** Follow the guide positively. Do only the actions it routes, and leave everything else alone until it is named. Do not ask NPCs for rumors, work, bounties, special jobs, unusual dialogue, property, pets, mounts, maps, horse armor, or Creation items unless the guide tells you to. Do not read notes, journals, books, notices, letters, or skill books unless the guide tells you to. Do not pick up treasure maps, unusual weapons, special items, AE notes, or quest-starting documents unless the guide tells you to. Do not enter or clear caves, mines, forts, towers, homes, dungeons, Creation locations, or bandit camps unless the guide tells you to. Courier letters, rumors, map markers, and Creation prompts are not permission to pursue content early.

## Route Start and Global Setup

### Setup and Save Baseline

1. Confirm you are playing Skyrim Special Edition / Anniversary Edition on PS4 with the official Anniversary Upgrade entitlement installed. Install the base game, Dawnguard, Hearthfire, Dragonborn, and the bundled Anniversary Edition Creation Club content.
2. Do not install mods, non-AE Creations, Verified Creator Program content outside the AE bundle, third-party Creations, PC-only content, console-command-only content, or anything that disables PlayStation trophies. Do not add or remove content mid-run.
3. Confirm the official AE bundle includes Creation content such as Survival Mode, Fishing, Camping, Rare Curios, Saints & Seducers, Farming, Tundra Homestead, Myrwatch, The Cause, Ghosts of the Tribunal, Bittercup, and the other official AE creations. This is only an installation check; the guide routes each Creation when it is safe.
4. Set the difficulty to Legendary before normal play begins, and leave it there unless this guide explicitly records a later exception.
5. Survival Mode is mandatory for the main route. The game offers the Survival Mode prompt after leaving Helgen; turn it on immediately after the escape.
6. Once manual saving is available, begin a rotating manual save set. Keep at least three rotating manual saves. When this guide names a hard save, create a separate save with the exact `HS-...` label and keep it until that branch or reward window is finished.
7. Start saving Fire Salts when practical. They are the key ingredient for hot food in Survival Mode, and hot food is how this route prepares for cold travel. Do not hoard every food ingredient if carry weight is tight; prioritize Fire Salts and common vegetables.

Useful early Survival recipes:

| Recipe | Ingredients |
| --- | --- |
| Hot Cabbage Soup | Fire Salts, Cabbage |
| Hot Potato Soup | Fire Salts, Potato |
| Hot Cabbage Potato Soup | Fire Salts, Cabbage, Leek, Potato |
| Hot Vegetable Soup | Fire Salts, Cabbage, Leek, Tomato, Potato |
| Hot Beef Stew | Fire Salts, Raw Beef, Carrot, Garlic |

8. Camping is available through the AE bundle, but do not depend on camps as your main survival plan. Inns and owned beds are safer for the route because they support planned rest and level-up timing. Later, when the guide tells you to craft Camping Supplies, use a forge or anvil with three firewood and one leather. Until then, keep those materials only when carry weight allows.
9. Before leaving setup, confirm the baseline: official AE bundle installed, no non-AE content installed, trophies preserved, Legendary difficulty selected, rotating-save policy understood, Survival Mode will be enabled at the post-Helgen prompt, and Route Discipline is understood.

## Early Warm-Core Stabilization

### Helgen, Riverwood, and First Survival Loop

1. Start the game normally. During `Unbound`, follow Hadvar as the route default. Near the bear exit, take the Black-Briar Mead and keep it for Falkreath. Leave Helgen with one usable weapon setup, one armor setup, lockpicks, gold, potions, and light food.
2. Finish the Helgen escape and leave the cave. Confirm the quest completes.
3. Discover Helgen before leaving the area.
4. After leaving Helgen, when the game offers the Survival Mode prompt, turn Survival Mode on immediately.
5. Make your first rotating manual save outside Helgen.
6. Follow the road to The Guardian Stones. Discover the location. Activate the Warrior Stone as the opening default.
7. Continue into Riverwood. Discover Riverwood.
8. Speak to Alvor for the `Before the Storm` Riverwood step. Accept his starter support, take the woodcutter's axe from the woodpile in Alvor's cellar if you do not already have one, and leave `Before the Storm` parked at the Whiterun/Jarl objective; it is not complete until the Whiterun turn-in.
9. At Alvor's forge, ask him to show you smithing work. Complete the tutorial sequence: make an iron dagger, improve the dagger, make leather, make a hide helmet, and improve the helmet.
10. Go to the Sleeping Giant Inn. Buy one inexpensive alcoholic drink from Orgnar if you do not already have one, give it to Embry. Keep the Black-Briar Mead from Helgen for Siddgeir.
11. Speak to Sven about Camilla and accept `Sven's Fake Letter from Faendal`.
12. Find Faendal near the mill or around Riverwood. Show him `Sven's Fake Letter from Faendal`, then accept `Faendal's Fake Letter from Sven`.
13. Go to the Riverwood Trader. Speak to Lucan or Camilla about the stolen claw and start `The Golden Claw`. Then speak to Camilla about the love triangle, give her `Faendal's Fake Letter from Sven`, and return to Faendal. Complete `A Lovely Letter`. Record Hero of the People at 2 of 50 finite miscellaneous objectives.
14. While at the Riverwood Trader, sell ordinary Helgen loot if you need food, arrows, lockpicks, or carry space.
15. Use the inn as the first Survival support stop: leave Riverwood with food for the Whiterun road, and rent a room first if Fatigue or a waiting level-up needs an indoor bed.
16. Check your inventory before leaving Riverwood. Keep gold, lockpicks, healing, food, one armor set, one main weapon setup, ammunition if you use it, unique items, quest items, and the woodcutter's axe for the first Whiterun inn loop.
17. Make another rotating manual save before starting the road to Whiterun.

### First Day in Whiterun

1. Walk from Riverwood to Whiterun on the main road.
2. Approach Whiterun Stables before going up to the city gate. Discover Whiterun Stables.
3. Walk up to the main gate. Discover Whiterun.
4. At the gate, use the Helgen/Riverwood dragon-warning dialogue to enter the city.
5. If you reach Whiterun after shops and daytime NPC schedules are over, rent a room at the Bannered Mare and start the city loop in the morning.
6. Speak to Adrianne Avenicci outside Warmaiden's and accept the delivery of Balgruuf's Greatsword to Proventus.
7. Go to Dragonsreach. Discover Dragonsreach.
8. Speak to Jarl Balgruuf. Report the Helgen dragon attack, complete `Before the Storm`. Accept the next main-quest handoff to Farengar for `Bleak Falls Barrow`.
9. Find Proventus Avenicci in Dragonsreach and give him Balgruuf's Greatsword. Complete `Greatsword for a Great Man`.
10. Speak to Farengar and receive the Dragonstone objective. Before leaving, ask whether he is the only wizard in Whiterun, accept Farengar's Frost Salts for Arcadia, and leave Dragonsreach.
11. Go to Arcadia's Cauldron during business hours and give Arcadia the frost salts. Complete `Salt for Arcadia`. Then ask Arcadia about using the alchemy lab, craft any valid potion at the shop's lab.
12. In the market district, speak to Carlotta Valentia and ask whether someone is giving her trouble. Accept her request to deal with Mikael.
13. Find Amren in the streets, market, or his house, ask about his family sword, and accept `Dungeon Delving (Amren's Family Sword)`. Completion waits for the assigned hideout later.
14. Give Brenuin one gold for his beggar favor and confirm `Gift of Charity` appears. Then ask Brenuin whether he is always drunk and accept `Argonian Ale Extraction`.
15. Find Lars Battle-Born between 8am and 8pm, agree to help with Braith, speak to Braith, then return to Lars. Complete `Bullying Braith`.
16. Go to the Hall of the Dead. If you are carrying any ordinary Amulets of Arkay, drop them on the Hall of the Dead floor before speaking to Andurs; pick them back up after the quest turn-in. Speak to Andurs, enter Whiterun Catacombs, take Andurs' Amulet of Arkay from the table, and take the Unusual Gem from the alcove near the lower stairs. Leave the `Death Blow of Abernanit` skill book closed. Return the amulet to Andurs.
17. Go to the Bannered Mare. Tell Mikael to leave Carlotta alone. Try the Persuade option first; if it succeeds, record Snake Tongue progress as Persuade 1 of 3. If it fails or the conversation becomes a brawl, fight barehanded with no follower, pet, summon, weapon, spell, poison, or shout. Return to Carlotta and complete `A Few Words with You`.
18. Use the wood chopping block behind the Bannered Mare. Chop at least one batch of firewood, then sell firewood to Hulda. Record Hard Worker progress as wood chopped 1 of 3.
19. In the Bannered Mare kitchen, take the Argonian Ale only when the prompt says `Take`. If it still says `Steal`, sell another batch of firewood to Hulda and check again. Give the Argonian Ale to Brenuin, complete `Argonian Ale Extraction`.
20. Make a rotating manual save before the brawl with Uthgerd. If you have less than 100 gold, sell ordinary duplicate loot first. Challenge Uthgerd the Unbroken, wager 100 gold, and fight barehanded with no follower, pet, summon, weapon, spell, poison, or shout. Speak to Uthgerd after she yields. Leave her available in Whiterun for now.
21. Record Hero of the People at 9 of 50 finite miscellaneous objectives: `Blacksmithing Tutorial`, `A Lovely Letter`, `Greatsword for a Great Man`, `Salt for Arcadia`, `Alchemy Tutorial`, `A Few Words with You`, `Bullying Braith`, `Andurs' Arkay Amulet`, and `Argonian Ale Extraction`.
22. If carry weight is tight, sell ordinary duplicate gear during business hours. Buy only food, arrows, lockpicks, or basic supplies you immediately need.
23. Before leaving Whiterun, make a rotating manual save.

### Bleak Falls Barrow and First Dragon

1. Before leaving Whiterun, carry food, healing, lockpicks, enough empty carry space, and a ranged option for the first dragon fight. Make a rotating manual save.
2. Return to Riverwood as the final warm stop before the dungeon. Use the inn or trader if you need food, rest, arrows, lockpicks, or carry space, then make a rotating manual save before climbing toward the barrow.
3. Take the northwestern trail from Riverwood to Bleak Falls Barrow. Discover Bleak Falls Barrow. Clear the exterior bandits, then enter Bleak Falls Temple.
4. In the first pillar puzzle room, set the pillars to Snake, Snake, Whale, then pull the lever and pass through the gate.
5. Leave the Pickpocket skill book `Thief` closed; this copy is reserved for the planned Scholar's Insight reading window.
6. Continue to the webbed chamber. Kill the wounded frostbite spider, speak to Arvel, cut him down, and recover the Golden Claw and Arvel's Journal from him when he dies. Read Arvel's Journal once for the door clue, then keep it until approved storage is available.
7. Continue through the draugr catacombs to the waterfall cavern. If Thomas's body is present on the ledge, loot `Note to Thomas`. If the body is not present, continue; the guide will handle the note later if needed.
8. Enter Bleak Falls Sanctum. For the claw door, set the rings from outer to inner as Bear, Moth, Owl, then activate the keyhole with the Golden Claw.
9. Before approaching the word wall, make a rotating manual save. Let the guardian fully emerge before killing it. Learn the first word of Unrelenting Force from the wall. Loot the Dragonstone from the guardian.
10. After the guardian is dead and the Dragonstone is in your inventory, use the exit handle and leave by the mountain shortcut.
11. Return to Riverwood before Whiterun. Give the Golden Claw back to Lucan at the Riverwood Trader, complete `The Golden Claw`. Record the Sideways counter at 1 of 10 qualifying side quests; do not close the Sideways trophy until the guide reaches the tenth qualifying side quest. Leave the Golden Claw on display until the final claw/display policy tells you otherwise. Camilla is now a non-default marriage option, but the route still uses Ysolda as the default spouse later.
12. Travel back to Whiterun and go straight to Dragonsreach. Make a rotating manual save before handing over the Dragonstone. Give the Dragonstone to Farengar, complete `Bleak Falls Barrow`.
13. Stay in Dragonsreach for the dragon report scene. Follow the Jarl's instructions for `Dragon Rising`. Breezehome is purchasable after this main-quest gate, but the route will not buy a house until the gold exists without grinding or exploits. If you later see the Alik'r warriors arguing with Whiterun guards at the gate, let the scene pass and stay with this route; their quest is handled in the next Whiterun block.
14. Before leaving the city with Irileth, confirm you have food, healing, and a ranged option. Make a rotating manual save. Follow Irileth and the guards west to the Western Watchtower. Discover Western Watchtower.
15. Defeat Mirmulnir and stay near the body until the soul absorption finishes. Start the Dragon Hunter counter at 1 of 20 absorbed dragon souls. Record absorbed souls, not dragon kills.
16. Equip Unrelenting Force after the soul is absorbed. If the optional objective to use the shout is still active, aim away from guards and civilians, use the shout once, then stop.
17. Return to Dragonsreach and report to Jarl Balgruuf. Complete `Dragon Rising`. Accept the Whiterun thaneship, the Axe of Whiterun, and Lydia's housecarl assignment. Leave Lydia available in Dragonsreach for now.
18. Make a rotating manual save before leaving Dragonsreach.

### Saadia, First Horse, Falkreath Mead, and Western Road Support

1. Start in Whiterun after reporting `Dragon Rising`. Sell ordinary duplicate loot, especially heavy dragon bones or scales if you need the money and carry space. Keep unique items, quest items, skill books, claws, stones, journals, named rewards, and the Black-Briar Mead from Helgen. Your immediate cash target is 1,500 gold for the first horse and one horse-armor purchase; house money comes later.
2. Leave Whiterun through the main gate. When the Alik'r warriors argue with the guards, let the scene finish, then speak to one of the warriors and agree to look for the Redguard woman.
3. Go to the Bannered Mare. Speak to Saadia, tell her the Alik'r are looking for her, and follow her upstairs. Hear her out and take her side as the route default.
4. Go to Dragonsreach Dungeon. Speak to the jailed Alik'r prisoner once, then pay his 100-gold fine to a Whiterun guard. Return to the prisoner only after the fine is paid, ask for Kematu's location, and receive the Swindler's Den objective.
5. Make a rotating manual save, then go to Whiterun Stables. Buy the Whiterun stable horse from Skulvar Sable-Hilt or Jervar for 1,000 gold. Ask the hostler to apply Elven Horse Armor for 500 gold. Steel Horse Armor waits until the later Whiterun property visit, when owned storage is available for the removed armor.
6. Before taking another sleep that could raise you to level 9, ride to Falkreath with the saved Black-Briar Mead. If the mead is missing, buy one from an innkeeper before speaking to Siddgeir.
7. Discover Falkreath.
8. Go to the Jarl's Longhouse, speak to Siddgeir, and use the work dialogue that asks whether he is the Jarl. Give him the Black-Briar Mead immediately, complete `Rare Gifts`. Record Hero of the People at 10 of 50 finite miscellaneous objectives. End the conversation after the mead reward; the Falkreath land job is routed later when the 5,000-gold land purchase is funded.
9. Return to Whiterun, rest if needed, sell ordinary duplicate loot if needed, and make a rotating manual save.
10. Ride west to Swindler's Den. Discover Swindler's Den at the cave entrance.
11. Enter Swindler's Den and fight through the bandits toward Kematu's chamber. Leave the `Thief` skill book closed when you pass its bedroll room; this copy is still reserved for the Scholar's Insight reading window.
12. Use the cooking pot in the bandit camp chamber to cook one food item from the ingredients available in the cave. Hard Worker tracking is now wood chopped and food cooked; mining waits for the first routed pickaxe and ore vein.
13. When Kematu stops you behind the waterfall, keep Saadia's side. End the conversation on the hostile path, kill Kematu and the Alik'r warriors directly, and use the cave shortcut back to the entrance.
14. Return to the Bannered Mare and tell Saadia that Kematu is dead. Complete `In My Time Of Need`, and record the Sideways counter at 2 of 10 qualifying side quests.
15. Sell ordinary Swindler's Den loot if you need food, arrows, lockpicks, or carry room. Keep the remaining gold liquid for the next section's Breezehome fund. Breezehome costs 5,000 gold before upgrades; Tundra Homestead costs 7,500 gold. Do not grind, exploit, or assume either house is available yet.
16. Make a rotating manual save before leaving Whiterun again.

### Rising Dead Early Activation

1. Run this section as soon as the courier gives you `Anonymous Letter` after level 5. If you already have the letter after the Saadia and Falkreath support loop, do this now before the Guardian Stones cache loop. If the letter has not arrived yet, skip to the next section and return here immediately when it does; do not enter Silent Moons Camp until this section is complete.
2. Read `Anonymous Letter`. Stock food, healing, and warm travel supplies from Whiterun or Falkreath, use an indoor bed if a level-up is waiting, and make a rotating manual save.
3. Ride toward Helgen, then to the ritual site in the mountains north of Southfringe Sanctum. Use this trip for the ritual site only; Southfringe Sanctum itself is a separate dungeon route. Make a rotating manual save before approaching the ritual circle.
4. Defeat the zombies at the ritual site. Loot at least one Mort Flesh and keep it for the later alchemy pass. Loot the marked necromancer for `Necromancer's Journal` and the leveled zombie spell tome.
5. Read `Necromancer's Journal` to complete `The Rising Dead` and activate Plague zombie horde world interactions. Before leaving the ritual site, find and read the zombie spell tomes there: `Spell Tome: Conjure Zombie`, `Spell Tome: Conjure Foul Zombie`, `Spell Tome: Conjure Putrid Zombie`, and `Spell Tome: Conjure Malignant Zombie`. If any one of those tomes is not present or cannot be found cleanly, leave only that missing tome for the later College/vendor spell pass.
6. Return to Whiterun, sell ordinary loot if needed, keep one Mort Flesh stored in your carried ingredients for now, and make a rotating manual save before the Guardian Stones cache loop.

### Guardian Stones Cache and Whiterun Farm Loop

1. Start in Whiterun after the Saadia turn-in. Sell ordinary duplicate loot, keep unique items and quest items, stock food for one warm-road loop, and make a rotating manual save.
2. Ride toward Riverwood, then go to the unmarked bandit camp just south of The Guardian Stones. Make a rotating manual save before the camp fight. Clear the camp and take `Treasure Map I`.
3. In the same camp, leave the `Night Falls on Sentinel` skill-book copy closed. The reading window still waits for Scholar's Insight.
4. Clear Anise's Cabin and Anise's Cellar while you are beside the Guardian Stones and Riverwood road. Leave the cellar books closed for the planned reading windows.
5. Ride to Riverwood and find Treasure Chest I inside the trunk of the fallen tree near town. Loot the cache. Treasure Maps tracking is now 1 of 13 resolved map-and-cache entries.
6. Go to the Riverwood Trader during business hours. Sell ordinary camp and cache loot, then buy `Spell Tome: Clairvoyance` from Lucan's standard stock. Read the tome immediately.
7. Use Riverwood as the warm support stop for this loop: buy food or rest at the inn before returning to Whiterun if Survival penalties would interfere with the ride back.
8. Ride back toward Whiterun Stables and reveal Pelagia Farm south of the stables. If the Companions giant scene is active, let it finish and accept Aela's Jorrvaskr lead if she speaks to you; the Companions join route waits for its own section.
9. Ride around Whiterun's eastern farm road and reveal Chillfurrow Farm.
10. Continue to Battle-Born Farm. Discover the farm.
11. Harvest the gourds in the Battle-Born fields. Speak to Alfhild Battle-Born and sell her the gourds through her crop-sale dialogue. This completes the representative crop-sale activity without using a merchant barter screen.
12. Return to Whiterun, sell ordinary loot from the loop, and keep the gold liquid for the Breezehome fund. Breezehome is still not bought here: the route has not yet created a reliable 5,000-gold house purchase without grinding.
13. Make a rotating manual save before moving on to the level-8 gate work.

### Halted Stream and Silent Moons Level Gate

1. Start in Whiterun. Rest if a level-up is pending, sell ordinary duplicate loot, stock food for a north-of-Whiterun loop, and make a rotating manual save.
2. Check the active marker for Amren's Family Sword. If it points to Halted Stream Camp, you will collect the sword during this section. If it points somewhere else, leave Amren's favor active for its assigned hideout later.
3. Ride north-northeast from Whiterun and reveal Whitewatch Tower from the road or courtyard, then continue north.
4. Ride to Halted Stream Camp. Discover the camp.
5. Clear the exterior bandits, enter the mine, and take the pickaxe from the crate near the first interior ore vein.
6. Mine one ore vein with the pickaxe. This completes the third Hard Worker action after the earlier wood-chopping and cooking steps. The paid ore-sale favor is a separate checklist item and waits for a routed ore buyer.
7. Take one Mammoth Tusk from the cart or shelves inside Halted Stream Camp for Ysolda.
8. Loot `Poacher's Axe` from the bandit carrying it inside the mine. Keep it; do not use it as an enchantment source.
9. In the boss chamber, read `Spell Tome: Transmute Mineral Ore`.
10. If Amren's Family Sword marker points to Halted Stream Camp, take Amren's Family Sword from the boss chest before leaving.
11. Kill the bandit leader, finish the mine.
12. Return to Whiterun. Find Ysolda, ask about the Mammoth Tusk, then give her the tusk immediately. Complete her `Rare Gifts` favor.
13. If you recovered Amren's Family Sword from Halted Stream Camp, return it to Amren now. If his marker pointed elsewhere, leave the quest open for that later route.
14. Record Hero of the People at 11 of 50 finite miscellaneous objectives, or 12 of 50 if Amren's sword was completed here too.
15. Check your character level. Silent Moons Camp is still locked until level 8.
16. If you are below level 8, stop here, rest or train only as the guide later routes it, and do not approach Silent Moons Camp yet. Return to this subsection as soon as the route brings you back at level 8+.
17. At level 8 or higher, start in Whiterun again. Rest if a level-up is pending, empty as much carry space as practical, stock food and lockpicks for the ride, and make a rotating manual save.
18. Ride northwest to Silent Moons Camp. Discover the camp.
19. Clear the exterior bandits and work through the camp. At the Lunar Forge, leave the `Light Armor Forging` skill-book copy closed for the Scholar's Insight reading window.
20. Take every weapon whose name begins `Lunar` from the camp: one on the Lunar Forge workbench, one on the grindstone, one from the shelves behind the locked interior double doors, and one from the upper tower table reached from the outer wall. Log the exact Lunar Iron or Lunar Steel mace, sword, and war axe forms you actually obtain.
21. Keep every Lunar weapon for now. Silent Moons Enchant is learned later, after the guide has set up storage and chosen the exact weapon to disenchant.
22. Clear the camp.
23. Return to Whiterun, sell ordinary loot, keep `Poacher's Axe` and all Lunar weapons, and make a rotating manual save.
24. Use Whiterun as support for the first central-plains clear. Discover Whiterun Military Camp, Whiterun Stormcloak Camp, Fort Greymoor, and The Ritual Stone. Clear White River Watch, Redoran's Retreat, Fort Greymoor, Guldun Rock, Hamvir's Rest, Greenspring Hollow, and Bleakwind Basin. Inside Redoran's Retreat, take `Treasure Map IV`; before returning through Whiterun, loot Treasure Chest IV near Pelagia Farm. Treasure Maps tracking is now 2 of 13 resolved map-and-cache entries. If Amren's Family Sword points to any of these places, take it from the marked chest before leaving. Leave `The Knights of the Nine` at Guldun Rock Cave closed for the Scholar's Insight reading window.
25. Return to Whiterun. If Amren's Family Sword was recovered in this loop, give it to Amren now and add his favor to Hero of the People tracking. Sell ordinary loot, rest if a level-up is waiting, and make a rotating manual save.
### Goldenhills Farm and Rorikstead

1. Start in Whiterun after the Silent Moons loop. Sell ordinary duplicate loot, rest if a level-up is pending, and set aside at least 1,300 gold for this pass: 500 gold for Goldenhills farmhands, 600 gold for livestock, and 200 gold for Hilda. Bring food, three Firewood, one set of Nails, and ten low-value plantable crops. Duplicates work; use common crops such as cabbage, potatoes, leeks, gourds, and wheat. If you are short on crops or Nails, buy or craft them before leaving Whiterun. Make a rotating manual save.
2. In the Bannered Mare, read the `For Sale` notice about the trained goat. This starts `Pets of Skyrim`; only Hilda is handled in this section.
3. Travel to Riverwood, recruit Faendal, and bring him with you toward Rorikstead. He is being brought for Goldenhills stewardship, not for combat.
4. Travel west to Rorikstead. Discover Rorikstead.
5. At Frostfruit Inn, speak to Erik about becoming an adventurer, then speak to Mralki. Use Persuade first. If Persuade fails, use Intimidate. If both fail, pay the gold option. Return to Erik and tell him his father changed his mind. Complete `Erik the Slayer`, and record Hero of the People at 12 of 50 finite miscellaneous objectives, or 13 of 50 if Amren's sword was completed earlier.
6. After completing Erik's favor, do not rent a room and sleep overnight at Frostfruit Inn during this visit. Use Goldenhills for rest after the farm is claimed. If a stranger offers a drinking contest in the inn, leave it for the Daedric route.
7. Start `The Unquiet Dead` by going to Goldenhills directly. Do not chase the optional `Bounty - Restless Spirits` innkeeper letter; the direct plantation start is the controlled route.
8. Before the first approach to Goldenhills, tell Faendal to wait in Rorikstead or on the road well away from the plantation, dismount, leave the horse away from the first fight, and make a rotating manual save. Approach Goldenhills on foot and alone.
9. Discover Goldenhills Plantation. Defeat the first ghost yourself and start `The Unquiet Dead`.
10. Take `Torn Page` beside Urval's body.
11. Enter the farmhouse. Upstairs, take and read `Urval's Journal`.
12. In Rin's room, take and read `Rin's Journal`.
13. In the basement, activate the hidden button to enter Jonquil's secret lab. Defeat Jonquil's ghost, take and read `Jonquil's Journal`. Leave the `Song of the Alchemists` skill book closed on the lab bookshelf; the selected copy for that title is routed later.
14. Go to the old well only after the journals point you there. Defeat the wolves, take Rin's Toy Sword from the child's skeleton, return to Rin's room, and place the toy sword where Rin asks.
15. Speak to Rin, lay the spirits to rest, and complete `The Unquiet Dead`.
16. Leaving the farmhouse starts `A Farmer's Life For Me`. Retrieve Faendal and bring him to Goldenhills. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-GOLDENHILLS-STEWARD` before assigning him.
17. Plant ten low-value crops in the fertile soil around the farm.
18. Ask Faendal to become the Goldenhills steward.
19. Ask Faendal to hire two farmhands for 500 gold.
20. At the Farmer's Workbench, build Animal Pens using three Firewood and Nails.
21. Ask Faendal to buy the Goldenhills cow for 300 gold, goats for 200 gold, and chickens for 100 gold.
22. Use the Goldenhills bed to pass a few days until farm income is available. Collect the income from Faendal. Open the Pantry Cupboard inside the farmhouse and take the first farm produce.
23. Complete `A Farmer's Life For Me`. Goldenhills is now the route's food, bed, and income base for this region. Use it for rest, pantry food, crops, and farm income; keep unique-item display and long-term collection storage for the storage base named later.
24. During this first farm setup, build only Animal Pens and buy only the livestock named above. The remaining Goldenhills projects wait for a later visit after the guide routes the materials and storage checks: Kitchen, Dining Area, Alchemy and enchanting lab, Loft, Master Bedroom, Child's Bedroom or Library/display choice, Apiary, Blacksmithing Workshop, Exterior Decoration, Farmhand Bunkhouse, Stable, Windmill, and the Goldenhills horse.
25. Return to Rorikstead. Find Halvar near Cowflop Farmhouse, buy Hilda for 200 gold. Read `Halvar's Journal` from Halvar's bedroll. Record `Pets of Skyrim` at 1 of 5 pets: Hilda acquired; Sweet Roll, Thistle, Arachnia, and Skritch remain for their later regions.
26. After the farm wait, visit Frostfruit Inn again and confirm Erik has become Erik the Slayer and is hireable. Leave him at the inn for now.
27. Use Goldenhills and Rorikstead as support for the western Whiterun road. Discover Crabber's Shanty, Gjukar's Monument, and Lund's Hut. Clear Orotheim, Talking Stone Camp, Sleeping Tree Camp, and Moldering Ruins. If Amren's Family Sword points to one of these places, take it from the marked chest before leaving.
28. Continue the same western loop only if food, weather, and carry weight are stable: discover Fort Sungard and Whiterun Imperial Camp, then clear Bleakwind Bluff and Sunderstone Gorge. At Sunderstone Gorge, learn the `Fire Breath` word `Toor` and take the Stone of Barenziah from the table in front of the word wall. Leave `King` at Bleakwind Bluff and `Guide to Better Thieving` at Fort Sungard Oubliette closed for the Scholar's Insight reading window.
29. Return to Goldenhills or Whiterun, sell ordinary loot, collect any farm income that has accrued, turn in Amren's Family Sword if this loop recovered it, and make a rotating manual save.
## Companions, Falkreath, and Southern Warm Expansion

### Companions Entry

1. Start in Whiterun after Goldenhills and Rorikstead. If Hilda is following you, send her to wait at Goldenhills before entering Jorrvaskr. Bring food, healing, lockpicks, one unenchanted weapon or bare fists for sparring, and enough carry space for a long ruin. Make a rotating manual save.
2. Go to Jorrvaskr and speak to Kodlak Whitemane in the living quarters. Ask to join the Companions. This first Jorrvaskr pass is for the faction quest only: Jorrvaskr's skill books, private-room books, Harbinger-room gem, Daedra Heart, and private-room ingredients are routed later.
3. Follow Vilkas to the training yard. Use an unenchanted weapon or bare fists only; do not use magic, a bound weapon, or an enchanted weapon for the sparring test. Stop as soon as Vilkas tells you to stop.
4. Take Vilkas's Sword directly to Eorlund Gray-Mane at the Skyforge. Do not place Vilkas's Sword on a weapon rack or plaque. At the forge, leave `The Armorer's Challenge` closed on the stone slab; the selected Smithing skill-book copy is routed later.
5. Give Vilkas's Sword to Eorlund, receive Aela's Shield, then take Aela's Shield to Aela in the living quarters. Follow Farkas when he shows you the sleeping area. Complete `Take Up Arms`.
6. Before taking your first Companions job, make `HARD SAVE: HS-COMPANIONS-FIRST-JOB`. Ask Farkas for work first. Record the quest title, giver, target NPC or target location, target hold, and completion result in your tracker.
7. Use the matching route for the first job you actually receive:
   * `Hired Muscle`: complete it immediately if the target is alive and reachable. Go to the marked NPC, start the brawl through dialogue, and fight barehanded with no follower, pet, summon, weapon, spell, poison, or shout. Return to Farkas.
   * `Trouble in Skyrim`: keep the contract only if the target location has not already been cleared or completed by this guide and is fully accessible now. Go to the marked location, kill the marked leader, record the exact target location, return to Farkas.
   * `Animal Extermination`: if Aela gives the house/shop/farm version, kill the marked invading beast and leave the building promptly after the objective updates. If she gives the den version, go to the marked den and kill the marked boss beast. Return to Aela.
   * `Family Heirloom`: go to the marked location, retrieve the heirloom from the boss chest, record the exact target location, return it to Skjor or Vilkas.
   * `Escaped Criminal`: follow the marker to the moving criminal, speak once if the dialogue is available, kill the criminal after they turn hostile, return to Skjor or Vilkas.
   * `Rescue Mission`: rescue the marked citizen, escort them back to the named home or hometown, return to Skjor or Vilkas.
8. If the first job points to an already handled, unreachable, or inaccessible target under the current route state, reload `HS-COMPANIONS-FIRST-JOB` and take work from a different available Companion leader instead. Do not restart the entire run to force `Hired Muscle`; if your save's first clean job is not `Hired Muscle`, record that and continue. After one clean Companions job is complete, record Companions radiant gates at 1 of 4.
9. Return to Jorrvaskr and ask for more work. When the leader tells you Skjor is looking for you, speak to Skjor and start `Proving Honor`.
10. Before speaking to Farkas for `Proving Honor`, send Hilda away if she has returned, do not bring any other follower, rest if a level-up is waiting, and make `HARD SAVE: HS-COMPANIONS-DUSTMANS-CAIRN`.
11. Speak to Farkas and have him follow you to Dustman's Cairn. Do not tell him to meet you there. Travel northwest of Whiterun, discover Dustman's Cairn.
12. Enter Dustman's Cairn with Farkas. In the first room, leave `Battle of Sancre Tor` closed; the selected copy for that skill-book title is routed later.
13. Continue until you reach the lever-trap room. Before pulling the lever, make a rotating manual save and make sure Farkas is in the room with you. Pull the lever, let the scripted Silver Hand scene finish, then continue after Farkas opens the way.
14. In Dustman's Crypt, take Dustman's Cairn Key from the chest in the large lower room, then unlock the north door and continue through the ruin.
15. In the final chamber, learn the first word of Fire Breath from the word wall. Take the Fragment of Wuuthrad from the altar, defeat the final draugr wave, use the shortcut back to the entrance.
16. Return to Whiterun through the main gate and go to Jorrvaskr. If Vilkas is waiting outside, speak to him and let him lead you to the initiation ceremony. If he is not outside, enter Jorrvaskr, find him, and speak to him before approaching the ceremony area.
17. Complete the ceremony and `Proving Honor`. Choose a Skyforge Steel Sword as the route-default free weapon from Eorlund unless your current build has a clear immediate need for one of the other offered Skyforge Steel weapons. Leave the Companions' next job for `Beast Blood And The Silver Hand`.
18. Make a rotating manual save before leaving Jorrvaskr.

### Beast Blood and the Silver Hand

1. Start in Whiterun after `Proving Honor`. Keep Hilda and any regular follower away from Jorrvaskr for this pass. Bring food, healing, lockpicks, and enough carry space for one Companion job and a Silver Hand fort. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-COMPANIONS-SECOND-JOB`.
2. Complete exactly one more clean Companion job before speaking to Skjor. If `Hired Muscle` / `Scare My Enemy` is still not recorded, ask Farkas first; otherwise ask any available Companion leader. Use the same job rules from the first Companions contract: record the title, giver, target NPC or location, hold, and result; brawls are barehanded; location jobs must have a fresh reachable target; rescue jobs must finish the escort and turn-in. If the marker points to Gallows Rock, or to an already handled, unreachable, or inaccessible target under the current route state, reload `HS-COMPANIONS-SECOND-JOB` and take a different clean job. After the turn-in, record the completed radiant title and record Companions radiant gates at 2 of 4.
3. Return to Jorrvaskr. When the leaders point you to Skjor, speak to him and start `The Silver Hand`.
4. Wait until night if needed, then meet Skjor outside the Underforge beneath the Skyforge. Before entering, make `HARD SAVE: HS-COMPANIONS-BLOOD-OATH`.
5. Enter the Underforge with Skjor. When Skjor and Aela are ready, activate the blood fountain and accept Beast Blood. After the transformation takes effect, the route has Beast Form.
6. During the forced Whiterun rampage, stay near the Underforge and let the sequence advance. This is not a werewolf-perk grind; preserve Whiterun residents and routed NPCs.
7. When you wake outside Whiterun with Aela, speak to her and re-equip your gear. You are now a werewolf. Keep sleeping in beds when the guide routes rest or level-ups, but do not expect Rested or Well Rested bonuses while Beast Blood remains active.
8. Follow Aela to Gallows Rock. Discover the fort.
9. Enter Gallows Rock with Aela. Pull the chain in the first room to open the spear gate, then clear the fort. Leave the skill books `The Importance of Where` and `Last Scabbard of Akrash` closed; those titles are read later.
10. In the final chamber, defeat Krev the Skinner and the Silver Hand. Speak to Aela near Skjor's body to complete `The Silver Hand`.
11. Return to Jorrvaskr and make `HARD SAVE: HS-COMPANIONS-AELA-JOBS` before asking Aela for revenge work.
12. Complete two Aela revenge jobs before starting the next Companions story quest. For each job, record the title, target location, hold, and result; return to Aela before taking the next one.
   * `Striking the Heart`: go to the marked Silver Hand camp or fort, kill the marked Silver Hand leader, return to Aela.
   * `Stealing Plans`: go to the marked Silver Hand hideout, take the Silver Hand Stratagem from the marked container, return it to Aela.
   * `Retrieval`: go to the marked Silver Hand hideout, take the Fragment of Wuuthrad from the boss chest, return it to Aela.
13. If an Aela job points somewhere inaccessible under the current route state, reload the nearest save from before accepting that job and take the clean assignment the game gives next. Do not chase a preferred pair of radiant titles.
14. After two Aela revenge jobs are complete, record Companions radiant gates at 4 of 4. If one of `Striking the Heart`, `Stealing Plans`, or `Retrieval` was not offered on this save, record that title as unavailable on this save. Do not ask Kodlak for the next Companions story assignment yet. Make a rotating manual save.

### Falkreath Land, Lakeview Foundation, and Glenmoril Coven

1. Start from Goldenhills or Whiterun after the two Aela revenge jobs. Collect Goldenhills income and sell ordinary duplicate loot until you have at least 5,000 gold for Lakeview Manor plus travel supplies. Bring food, healing, lockpicks, one cabbage, gourd, or potato for Mathies, and a woodcutter's axe or Poacher's Axe. Make a rotating manual save.
2. Return to Falkreath and go to the Jarl's Longhouse. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-FALKREATH-LAND-JOB` before asking Siddgeir for work.
3. Ask Siddgeir for more work, accept `Kill the Bandit Leader`, and record the assigned target before leaving the longhouse.
4. If the target is Bannermist Tower, ride northwest of Falkreath. Discover Bannermist Tower. Kill the marked bandit leader, clear the tower, leave `Advances in Lockpicking` closed.
5. If the target is Bilegulch Mine, ride to the mine northwest of Falkreath. Discover Bilegulch Mine. Kill the marked bandit leader, clear the mine, leave `The Armorer's Challenge` closed.
6. If the target is Cracked Tusk Keep, record that target, leave `Kill the Bandit Leader` active, and do not enter the keep yet. The guide will return to Cracked Tusk Keep when the level-20 Mehrunes Dagon route sends you there. For this save, do only steps 11-14, then continue from step 21. Siddgeir's turn-in, Falkreath thaneship, Lakeview Manor, Rayya, and The Woodsman's Friend wait for that later return.
7. If the target is Embershard Mine, first return to the Whiterun Guard Barracks inside the gate and read `Guard's Dossier: Yakhtu gra-Orkulg` to start `Smith 'N Slash`. Then ride to Embershard Mine, discover it. Clear the mine, kill Yakhtu gra-Orkulg, take and read `Yakhtu's Journal`, retrieve the enchanted Orcish Plate armor from the mine leader. Leave `Light Armor Forging` closed.
8. If the target is Knifepoint Ridge, treat the assignment as a bug-conflict result. Reload `HS-FALKREATH-LAND-JOB`, ask Siddgeir for work again, and take the next clean assignment the game gives. Do not enter Knifepoint Ridge and do not try to park this bounty for the later Boethiah route; clearing Knifepoint before Boethiah can break Boethiah, while the active Boethiah champion state can break the Jarl bounty.
9. If the assigned target was Bannermist Tower, Bilegulch Mine, or Embershard Mine, return to Siddgeir, report the bandit leader dead, complete `Kill the Bandit Leader`. Record Hero of the People at 13 of 50 finite miscellaneous objectives, or 14 of 50 if Amren's sword was completed earlier.
10. Speak to Siddgeir again and accept the Falkreath thaneship objective to assist three people in the hold.
11. Make a rotating manual save. Find Thadgeir in Falkreath, ask about the burial, and accept Berit's Ashes. Deliver Berit's Ashes to Runil, complete `Delivery`. Record Falkreath help at 1 of 3. If Siddgeir's bandit job was completed in this section, Hero of the People is now 14 of 50, or 15 of 50 if Amren's sword was completed earlier. If the bandit job is parked at Cracked Tusk Keep, Hero of the People is now 13 of 50, or 14 of 50 if Amren's sword was completed earlier.
12. Find Mathies at Corpselight Farm, in Falkreath, or at Dead Man's Drink. Sell him one cabbage, gourd, or potato through his crop-sale dialogue, and record Falkreath help at 2 of 3. Leave the Sinding/daughter conversation for the later Hircine route.
13. Ride to Half-Moon Mill. Discover the mill. Chop one batch of firewood if needed, sell firewood to Hert, and record Falkreath help at 3 of 3.
14. From Half-Moon Mill, go south-southwest into the woods to Sweet Roll's clearing. Loot the dead thief, read and take `Thief's Journal`, take one of the sweet rolls from the body, feed a sweet roll to Sweet Roll, and receive the Teleport Pet: Sweet Roll spell. Send Sweet Roll to Goldenhills if the pet dialogue offers that home option; otherwise tell the fox to wait safely at the clearing before you continue.
15. From Half-Moon Mill, ride to the Lake Ilinalta island with The Lady Stone. Discover The Lady Stone. Keep the Warrior Stone active for now.
16. Return to Siddgeir and report that you have helped the people of Falkreath. Accept the thaneship, the Blade of Falkreath, and Rayya's housecarl assignment. Leave Rayya at the Jarl's Longhouse for now.
17. Speak to Nenya in the Jarl's Longhouse and buy the Lakeview Manor plot for 5,000 gold.
18. Ride to Lakeview Manor. Discover the property.
19. At Lakeview, use the drafting table to choose `Small House Layout`. Use the nearby stone quarry for ten quarried stone, use the starting materials and log pile at the site, then build the foundation at the carpenter's workbench. Land Baron tracking is now 1 of 3 Hearthfire plots purchased.
20. Behind Lakeview, down the hill near the dead wood cutter, pick up The Woodsman's Friend. Preserve it; it is not a firewood axe.
21. Do not assign Rayya as steward yet. The permanent Lakeview steward assignment waits for the Main Hall and service-building pass.
22. If you are below level 14, keep `Blood's Honor` unstarted, make a rotating manual save, and continue into the next warm-route section; the guide will bring you back to step 23 at level 14+. At level 14 or higher, rest at a proper bed, send Hilda or any follower away, carry food and healing, and make a rotating manual save for Glenmoril Coven.
23. Before speaking to Kodlak about `Blood's Honor`, ride to Glenmoril Coven and clear it in its normal hag state. Discover Glenmoril Coven. Clear the cave, leave `A Tragedy in Black` and `Horror of Castle Xyr` closed. This clear happens before `Blood's Honor` so the cave can register its map clear state.
24. Return to Jorrvaskr, speak to Kodlak, and start `Blood's Honor`. Travel back to Glenmoril Coven when Kodlak sends you there.
25. In Glenmoril Coven during `Blood's Honor`, kill all five Glenmoril Witches and take all five Glenmoril Witch Heads. Keep the heads; they are needed for later cure options and do not add normal carry weight.
26. Return to Jorrvaskr. Let Vilkas confront you, complete `Blood's Honor`. `Purity of Revenge` starts immediately; leave it active for the next Companions story block.
27. Make a rotating manual save after `Purity of Revenge` starts.

### Southern Warm Corridor

1. Start from Whiterun, Goldenhills, Riverwood, or Falkreath after the Companions state from the previous section is settled as far as your level allows. Sleep in a proper bed if a level-up is waiting, collect Goldenhills income if you are passing the farm, sell ordinary duplicate loot, and carry food, healing, lockpicks, one standard Potion of Healing for Valdr, and a woodcutter's axe. Make a rotating manual save.
2. If `Blood's Honor` is still unstarted because you were below level 14 in the previous section, keep it unstarted for the moment. As soon as this section raises you to level 14 or higher, pause after the current turn-in, rest in a proper bed, go to Glenmoril Coven, clear it in its normal hag state, leave `A Tragedy in Black` and `Horror of Castle Xyr` closed, return to Jorrvaskr, start `Blood's Honor`, return to Glenmoril Coven for Kodlak, kill all five Glenmoril Witches, take all five Glenmoril Witch Heads, and complete `Blood's Honor`, then resume this section at your next uncompleted step.
3. Go to Falkreath. During business hours, stop at Grave Concoctions and buy one Deathbell from Zaria; keep it for Peryite and do not count it toward Ingun's 20 Deathbell turn-in. Then find Dengeir of Stuhn in Dengeir's House, Dead Man's Drink, or the Jarl's Longhouse. Ask about the plot against him and accept the letter theft.
4. Make a rotating manual save, enter Lod's House when you can do so cleanly, take the Private Letter from the bedside table, and return it to Dengeir.
5. Speak to Dengeir again and accept `Dark Ancestor`. Ride or walk to Bloodlet Throne, discover it.
6. Enter Bloodlet Throne, clear the vampire lair, kill Vighar, take one Vampire Dust from the vampires for Peryite, and leave the `Incident at Necrom` skill-book copy closed.
7. Return to Dengeir and complete `Dark Ancestor`.
8. Speak to Lod in Falkreath and start `A Daedra's Best Friend`. Meet Barbas outside Falkreath, let him travel to Haemar's Shame, then clear Haemar's Shame and Haemar's Cavern. Leave `Response to Bero's Speech` closed in the cavern, speak to Clavicus Vile at the shrine, and accept the Rueful Axe objective. Rimerock Burrow waits for the later Haafingar coast route.
9. Continue to Southfringe Sanctum. Leave `The Warrior's Charge` closed at the chest site north of the cave, clear Bashnag's spellswords and the spider tunnels, cut down Selveni Nethri, tell her when the path is clear, and let her escape to complete `The Savior of Selveni Nethri`.
10. Travel to Moss Mother Cavern northwest of Half-Moon Mill. Discover the cave.
11. Speak to Valdr outside the cave and give him the standard Potion of Healing. Clear Moss Mother Cavern, return to Valdr, and accept Valdr's Lucky Dagger. Preserve Valdr's Lucky Dagger.
12. If Lakeview Manor is available on your save, return there before Pinewatch. Use the site chest, drafting table, anvil, and carpenter's workbench to finish the Small House: wall framing, walls, floor, roof framing, roof, and door. If you spent any of the starting iron or corundum, buy replacement material before beginning the build.
13. If Lakeview Manor is available, make `HARD SAVE: HS-LAKEVIEW-RAYYA-STEWARD`. Once Rayya is at Lakeview after the Small House is built, ask her to become the Lakeview steward. This is the permanent Lakeview steward choice.
14. If Rayya is steward, use normal loot from this section to keep 500 gold spare after food and rest money, then ask her to hire the Lakeview carriage driver. If you do not have 500 spare gold yet, return after the Pinewatch sell-off in step 19 and hire the driver before leaving for Ivarstead. Do not buy steward furnishings, bard service, livestock, or a homestead horse yet.
15. Go to Pinewatch. Discover the shack. Enter the house, take `Note to Rhorlak`.
16. Use the hidden button by the basement bookcase to enter the bandit hideout. Leave `The Red Kitchen Reader` skill-book copy closed.
17. Clear Pinewatch and its Bandit's Sanctuary. Take `Father's Missive`, the Unusual Gem in the locked treasure room, and the Curious Silver Mold from the treasure room.
18. After Rigel Strong-Arm and the Pinewatch bandits are dealt with, preserve the Curious Silver Mold for the later Markarth Thieves Guild special job.
19. Sell ordinary Falkreath and Pinewatch loot at Falkreath, Riverwood, or another warm support stop. Keep unique items, quest items, skill books, journals, stones, claws, and named rewards. Rest if a level-up is waiting.
20. Complete the wider Falkreath pine-forest loop while this warm support route is active. Discover Hunter's Rest, Falkreath Imperial Camp, Falkreath Stormcloak Camp, Fort Neugrad, North Shriekwind Bastion, South Shriekwind Bastion, North Skybound Watch, and South Skybound Watch. At Fort Neugrad, climb the top tower, take `Fort Neugrad Treasure Map`, then loot the Fort Neugrad treasure chest east of the fort near the lakeshore and snowy mountain nook. Treasure Maps tracking is now 3 of 13 resolved map-and-cache entries. Clear Ancient's Ascent, Bonechill Passage, Evergreen Grove, Greywater Grotto, Peak's Shade Tower, Secunda's Kiss, Shriekwind Bastion, and Skybound Watch Pass. Leave Ilinalta's Deep for `The Black Star` after the quest points there; Ilinalta's Deluge is quest-gated. Leave `Purloined Shadows` in the hut northwest of Bonechill Passage, `Mannimarco, King of Worms` at Evergreen Grove, `De Rerum Dirennis` north of Evergreen Grove, and `Father Of The Niben` at Hunter's Rest closed for the Scholar's Insight reading window.
21. Travel to Ivarstead. Discover Ivarstead. Use Vilemyr Inn as the local bed and food endpoint.
22. At the Ivarstead mill, chop one batch of firewood and sell it to Temba Wide-Arm. Ask about the bear problem and accept `Grin and Bear It`; from now on, keep bear pelts, cave bear pelts, and snow bear pelts from routed caves until the guide gives exactly ten to Temba. Temba is now a non-default marriage option; the route default remains Ysolda.
23. At Fellstar Farm, harvest crops with a `Take` prompt and sell them to Boti through her crop-sale dialogue.
24. Find Narfi near the ruined house by the river. Give him one gold.
25. Speak to Narfi about Reyda. Then ask Wilhelm at Vilemyr Inn about Reyda, recover Reyda's Necklace from the river east of Ivarstead's southern bridge, and return it to Narfi. Complete `The Straw that Broke`.
26. Speak to Wilhelm at Vilemyr Inn about Shroud Hearth Barrow and accept `Lifting the Shroud`.
27. Enter Shroud Hearth Barrow, discover it. In the first lever room, use the two levers closest to the door. Leave `Before the Ages of Man` closed.
28. Reach Wyndelius Gatharian's room. Do not use Unrelenting Force or destructive area effects near the table with his journal. Defeat Wyndelius, take Wyndelius's Journal, and return it to Wilhelm. Complete `Lifting the Shroud`. Wilhelm is now a non-default marriage option.
29. Return to Shroud Hearth Barrow and use the Sapphire Dragon Claw on the puzzle door. Set the rings from outer to inner as Moth, Owl, Wolf.
30. In the trapped draugr room beyond the claw door, take and read `Spell Tome: Oakflesh`.
31. Continue through Shroud Hearth Depths. For the rotating-pillar room, set the pillars to Whale, Eagle, Snake, Whale, then continue to the word wall. Learn the Shroud Hearth Barrow word of Kyne's Peace.
32. Finish the barrow.
33. If Lakeview Manor was bought in the previous Falkreath section, route the `Fan Favorite` AE stop now. From Ivarstead, go to the camp south of town, read and take Arena Fan's Note, and take `The Crimson Dirks, v8`.
34. If you routed the camp in step 33, continue to the Ruins of Bthalft. Discover the ruins. Clear the exterior bandits, kill Urgnok, and take and preserve `Dwarven Mail`, `Dwarven Mail Boots`, `Dwarven Mail Gauntlets`, and `Dwarven Mail Helmet`. Do not activate the Aetherium pedestal or insert Aetherium Shards.
35. If Falkreath land and Lakeview Manor are still parked because Siddgeir assigned Cracked Tusk Keep, skip the `Fan Favorite` camp and Ruins of Bthalft for now. Do not pass close enough to the camp to spawn and abandon Arena Fan's corpse. The guide returns to that AE camp after the Cracked Tusk/Mehrunes closeout buys Lakeview Manor.
36. Return to a warm support hub: Ivarstead, Lakeview if available, Riverwood, Whiterun, or Goldenhills. Sell ordinary loot, store only in approved owned storage, sleep in a proper bed if a level-up is waiting, and make a rotating manual save.
### Hircine and Bloated Man's Grotto

1. Start from a warm support stop: Lakeview Manor if it is built, Falkreath, Riverwood, Whiterun, Ivarstead, or Goldenhills. Sleep in a bed if a level-up is waiting, bring food and healing, and make a rotating manual save.
2. Travel to Bloated Man's Grotto before starting `Ill Met By Moonlight`. Discover the grotto.
3. Clear the normal grotto by killing the animals and spriggans. At the Talos shrine, take Bolar's Oathblade and Bolar's Writ. Preserve Bolar's Oathblade.
4. When the normal grotto is clear, leave before starting the Hircine quest version.
5. Return to a warm support stop. Sell ordinary loot, store Bolar's Oathblade only in approved owned storage if you are not carrying it, rest if Fatigue or a pending level-up needs it, and make a rotating manual save.
6. Go to Falkreath Barracks and down to Falkreath Jail. In the barracks and jail, leave the guard papers and skill books closed for now: Guard Dossier: Aesrael, Guard's Bounty Letter Draft, Guard's Note, The Legendary Sancre Tor, and The Black Arts On Trial.
7. Approach Sinding's cell and agree to help him return the ring to Hircine. Wait in the jail room until Sinding transforms and escapes before leaving.
8. While the Cursed Ring of Hircine is equipped, travel directly for the quest objectives and avoid unnecessary outdoor waiting. If Beast Blood is active, the cursed ring can force a random werewolf transformation outdoors.
9. Make a rotating manual save before hunting the White Stag east of Falkreath. Kill the White Stag, speak to Hircine's aspect, and accept the hunt at Bloated Man's Grotto.
10. Before entering the quest version of Bloated Man's Grotto, use a warm support stop if needed. Leave followers, pets, and summons behind for this outcome save, then make `HARD SAVE: HS-DAEDRIC-HIRCINE-GROTTO` outside the grotto.
11. Branch route: enter Bloated Man's Grotto from the hard save, speak to the dying hunter if he is still alive, then find Sinding. Tell Sinding you will kill him as Hircine commands. Kill Sinding, activate his body to skin him, and receive Savior's Hide from Hircine. Do not continue from this branch save.
12. Reload `HARD SAVE: HS-DAEDRIC-HIRCINE-GROTTO`.
13. Main route: enter Bloated Man's Grotto again, speak to the dying hunter if he is still alive, then find Sinding. Tell Sinding you will spare him and help him against the hunters.
14. Kill the hunters with Sinding. Do not hit Sinding, heal Sinding, use area attacks near him, or use Beast Form against him.
15. When every hunter is dead, speak to Sinding. Loot the dead hunters now if you want their items; they disappear after the quest.
16. Exit the grotto and speak to Hircine's aspect outside. Keep the restored Ring of Hircine as the canonical reward. Record Oblivion Walker progress at 1 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
17. Do not go back inside to kill Sinding after receiving the Ring of Hircine. The main route keeps the single-artifact Ring outcome and does not use the dual-artifact reward method.
18. Return to a warm support stop. Sleep if the quest's late-night grotto state caused Fatigue, preserve the Ring of Hircine and Bolar's Oathblade, and make a rotating manual save.

## Riften, Thieves Setup, and Southeast Support

### First Riften Visit and Guild Doorway

1. Start from Ivarstead, Lakeview Manor, Falkreath, Riverwood, Whiterun, Goldenhills, or another warm support stop. Before leaving, sell ordinary duplicate loot, sleep if a level-up is waiting, carry food, healing, lockpicks, one standard Potion of Healing for Wujeeta, 100 gold for a brawl wager, and 1,000 spare gold for Galathil. Make a rotating manual save.
2. Travel to Riften Stables and discover the location.
3. Speak to Hofgrir Horse-Crusher about being the strongest brawler and wager 100 gold. Fight barehanded only: no follower, pet, summon, weapon, spell, poison, or shout. After winning, speak to Hofgrir for the payout.
4. Approach Riften's north gate and discover the city.
5. At the gate shakedown, use the Persuade option to call out the tax. If this is your first successful persuasion for `Snake Tongue`, record a Persuade success for that trophy.
6. Enter Riften, let Sapphire and Shadr finish their argument, then speak to Shadr and offer to help clear the debt.
7. Go to the Bee and Barb, speak to Sapphire, and persuade her to forgive Shadr's debt. Return to Shadr and complete `Stabilized`.
8. In the market, speak to Brand-Shei about his past and accept `Distant Memories`. Keep the search for Lymdrenn Tenvanni's Journal parked for the later northern-coast route.
9. Speak to Madesi at his stall and accept `Ringmaker`. From now on, keep two Flawless Sapphires, one Mammoth Tusk, and one Gold Ore for Madesi instead of selling them.
10. Speak to Marise Aravel and accept `Ice Cold`. Save five Ice Wraith Teeth for her.
11. Speak to Bolli in the market or at the fishery and accept his Markarth delivery. Keep the Purchase Agreement (Bolli) for Kleppr at the Silver-Blood Inn.
12. Speak to Balimund at the Scorched Hammer and accept `Stoking the Flames`. Keep saving Fire Salts, but do not hand over the first ten unless the guide tells you they are spare after Survival hot-food needs.
13. At the Bee and Barb, speak to Talen-Jei about his engagement ring and accept `Sealing the Deal`. Save three Flawless Amethysts for him. Leave Louis Letrush's Frost job for the later named-save route.
14. Go down to Elgrim's Elixirs. Speak to Hafjorg and accept `Truth Ore Consequences`; the ore sample waits for the Shor's Stone route. Speak to Ingun Black-Briar and accept `Few and Far Between`; begin saving 20 Nirnroot, 20 Nightshade, and 20 Deathbell for her.
15. Go to Riften Docks and discover the docks.
16. Enter Riften Fishery. Speak to Swims-In-Deep-Water, receive and read `List of Fair Weather Fish`, then take and read `Fishing Mastery, v1` from the back-room shelf. Speak to Viriya, receive and read `Bounty: Juvenile Mudcrab`, then agree to take over the bounty. After both introductions, leave `Catch of the Day` and `In A Pinch` active for the fishing route.
17. Find Wujeeta at the fishery, the docks, or Haelga's Bunkhouse. Give her the standard Potion of Healing and complete `Helping Hand`.
18. Ask Wujeeta where she got the skooma. Use Persuade first; if it fails, use Bribe. Record the Speech action for `Snake Tongue` only if it succeeds and fills a missing Persuade or Bribe slot. Learn that Sarthis Idren is using Riften Warehouse.
19. If From-Deepest-Fathoms is wandering the docks, accept the Lexicon from her and start `Unfathomable Depths`. Keep the Lexicon; Avanchnzel is routed in a later Rift section.
20. Go to Mistveil Keep and discover it.
21. Tell Jarl Laila Law-Giver about the skooma dealer and accept the warehouse assignment. Receive the Riften Warehouse Key.
22. Speak to Harrald in Mistveil Keep and accept `Bring It!`. Speak to Wylandriah and accept `Hunt and Gather`; her Spoon, Soul Gem, and Orichalcum Ingot wait for Ivarstead, Windhelm, and Winterhold.
23. During the day, enter Mistveil Keep Jarl's Chambers while the door is unlocked. Take the Stone of Barenziah from the end table beside the Jarl's bed and leave `Surfeit of Thieves` closed.
24. Return to Balimund, collect Harrald's repaired sword, then bring it back to Harrald. Complete `Bring It!`.
25. Go to Riften Warehouse on the docks. Enter with the Jarl's key, kill Sarthis Idren yourself, take Sarthis's Key, open the basement storage room, take and read `Shipment's Ready` from Sarthis's satchel.
26. Return to the Jarl with the evidence. Complete `The Raid`, start `Supply and Demand`. Cragslane Cavern waits for the next Rift route.
27. In the daytime market, speak to Brynjolf and agree to his scheme. When he begins the distraction, let the job fail cleanly: wait in the market until the distraction ends without stealing Madesi's Ring or planting it on Brand-Shei. Speak to Brynjolf afterward and complete `A Chance Arrangement`.
28. Enter the Ratway from the lower level of Riften. Follow the normal Ratway path toward the Ragged Flagon. In Gian the Fist's room, kill Gian and take the Gloves of the Pugilist. Preserve the gloves; do not disenchant them for Fortify Unarmed.
29. In the Ratway room with Skritch's cage, keep the Guardian Vault and any ghostly apparition parked for the later Dawnfang and Bloodthirst route. Continue to the failed pickpocket's body near the Ragged Flagon entrance, take the Skeever Cage Key and `Pickpocket's Journal`, read the journal, then return to the cage and free Skritch. Tell Skritch to wait in Riften or at an owned home before continuing.
30. In the final Ratway room, leave the `Beggar` skill-book copy closed. Lower the drawbridge shortcut if you pass the lever, then enter the Ragged Flagon.
31. Speak to Brynjolf in the Ragged Flagon and accept `Taking Care of Business`. Ask him about the three debtors so the nonlethal collection routes are clear.
32. Return to the city. At the Bee and Barb, speak to Talen-Jei about Keerava, then use his information to collect Keerava's debt without a brawl.
33. At the Pawned Prawn, collect Bersi Honey-Hand's debt by breaking the Dwemer urn across from him, then speaking to him.
34. At Haelga's Bunkhouse, leave the `Beggar` skill-book copy closed. Because Haelga is the last debtor, speak to her and collect her payment without stealing the Statue of Dibella.
35. Return to Brynjolf in the Ragged Flagon with all three payments. Complete `Taking Care of Business`, join the Thieves Guild.
36. Follow Brynjolf into the Ragged Flagon Cistern, speak to Mercer Frey, and establish the Thieves Guild headquarters.
37. Complete `Meet the Family`: speak to Vex and Delvin for introductions only; if either offers a job, back out without accepting it. Then speak to Tonilia and receive the full Thieves Guild armor set. The guide routes Delvin and Vex jobs later, after the Nightingale questline is ready to continue. From this point forward, keep Thieves Guild membership clean: do not attack or steal from guild members, and do not kill NPCs during guild jobs unless the guide explicitly says to. If `Reparations` starts or guild services shut off, reload the latest clean save instead of paying through the failure state.
38. Speak to Galathil in the Ragged Flagon, pay 1,000 gold, make any appearance adjustment you can live with, and confirm the change.
39. Use Tonilia, Vekel, city merchants, and the Bee and Barb for any selling, food, or rest you need before leaving. Do not buy Honeyside or Shadowfoot Sanctum yet; those homes need their prerequisite and gold checks in a later Riften property route. Make a rotating manual save.

### Goldenglow, Honningbrew, Solitude, and Snow Veil

1. Start in the Ragged Flagon after `Meet the Family`, with `Loud and Clear` active. Bring food, healing, lockpicks, Flames or another precise fire source, and enough carry space for several quest items. Make a rotating manual save before leaving Riften.
2. Speak to Vex about Goldenglow Estate and learn the sewer approach. Travel to Goldenglow Estate and discover it.
3. Enter through the sewer. Leave the `Guide to Better Thieving` skill-book copy closed.
4. In Aringoth's quarters, take the Queen Bee Statue and Bee in a Jar.
5. Reach Aringoth's safe by taking his key through dialogue or by picking the safe. Take and read the Goldenglow Bill of Sale from the safe.
6. Outside, burn exactly three beehives. Stop when the quest counter reaches 3 of 3; burning more than three costs the gold reward.
7. Return to Brynjolf in the Ragged Flagon. Complete `Loud and Clear`, and ask Brynjolf why Maven Black-Briar wants to meet you before ending the handoff.
8. Give the Queen Bee Statue to Delvin Mallory immediately.
9. Go to the Bee and Barb and speak to Maven. Follow her instructions to meet Mallus Maccius in the Bannered Mare, then travel to Honningbrew Meadery near Whiterun. Discover the meadery.
10. Speak to Sabjorn and accept the job to poison the meadery. If the persuade option for advance payment is available, try it once and record a `Snake Tongue` Persuade success only if it fills a missing trophy slot. Take Sabjorn's key and pest poison.
11. Clear the Honningbrew Basement and boilery path, poison the skeever nest, defeat Hamelyn, and take and read Hamelyn's Journal. Leave the `Three Thieves` skill-book copy closed.
12. Poison the Honningbrew vat, then return to the tasting room and watch the Captain's tasting scene. Speak to Mallus afterward and receive Sabjorn's dresser key.
13. Upstairs, use Sabjorn's key to take and read the Promissory Note and `Have Need of Cynric`. Take the Honningbrew Decanter. Leave the `A Game at Dinner` skill-book copy closed. Keep one bottle of Honningbrew Mead if you can spare the weight; it is useful for a later random-encounter necklace objective.
14. Report to Maven, then return to Brynjolf. Complete `Dampened Spirits`. Mallus is now the Whiterun-area fence at Honningbrew.
15. Give the Honningbrew Decanter to Delvin.
16. In the Thieves Guild headquarters, take and read `East Empire Connection` from Mercer's desk.
17. Speak to Mercer Frey and begin `Scoundrel's Folly`. Travel by carriage to Solitude. At the carriage landing, step into the farm and stable yard until Katla's Farm is discovered. Enter Solitude, let Roggvir's execution finish.
18. Go to the Winking Skeever. Speak to Gulum-Ei about Goldenglow and learn that he wants the Firebrand Wine Case. In the same inn, leave `The Restless` closed for the later Dead Man's Dread route.
19. Still in the Winking Skeever, speak to Sorex Vinius after the execution, ask who runs Solitude, and accept the Stros M'Kai Rum delivery for Falk Firebeard.
20. Speak to Octieve San about Irnskar Ironhand. Resolve Irnskar's debt with Persuade if available, otherwise use Intimidate or a clean barehanded brawl. Return to Octieve. Add Octieve's favor to the Hero of the People count.
21. Find Svari in Solitude's streets, speak to her about Roggvir, then speak to Greta. Only after Greta asks for Roggvir's Amulet of Talos, retrieve it from Roggvir's body near the chopping block or from his coffin in the Solitude Catacombs if the body has already moved. Return it to Greta, complete `Return to Grace`. Add `Return to Grace` to the Hero of the People count.
22. At Radiant Raiment, speak to Taarie and accept `Fit for a Jarl`. Put on the Radiant Raiment Fine Clothes she gives you.
23. At Evette San's stall, accept `The Spiced Wine`. At Angeline's Aromatics, accept `No News is Good News`.
24. Near the Bards College, speak to Dervenin and accept `The Mind of Madness`. Receive Pelagius' Hip Bone.
25. Go to the Blue Palace and discover it.
26. While wearing Taarie's outfit, speak to Elisif about the clothes and mention Radiant Raiment. Deliver the Stros M'Kai Rum to Falk Firebeard. Add Sorex's delivery to the Hero of the People count.
27. Ask Una or Erdi for the key to the Pelagius Wing. In Elisif's bedroom, take the Stone of Barenziah from the wardrobe beside the bed. Leave `Biography of the Wolf Queen` closed.
28. Take the Firebrand Wine Case from the lower hallway of the Blue Palace.
29. Enter the Pelagius Wing. Complete Sheogorath's three Wabbajack trials and finish `The Mind of Madness`. After you return to the wing, take the Purple Butterfly in a Jar and leave `Surfeit of Thieves` closed. Record Oblivion Walker progress at 2 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
30. In Castle Dour, step into the side room off the War Room and read and take `Guard's Dossier: Bjormund Wind-Strider`. Then speak to Captain Aldis for Angeline. Use Persuade if available, otherwise use the successful dialogue option your route state allows. Return to Angeline and complete `No News is Good News`. Add `No News is Good News` to the Hero of the People count.
31. Return to Radiant Raiment and tell Taarie what Elisif said. Complete `Fit for a Jarl`, keep the Radiant Raiment Fine Clothes. Add `Fit for a Jarl` to the Hero of the People count.
32. Go down to the Solitude docks during the day. At the East Empire Company Warehouse exterior, speak to Vittoria Vici for Evette and use Persuade to have the spices released. Discover the warehouse.
33. Return to Evette San, complete `The Spiced Wine`. Add `The Spiced Wine` to the Hero of the People count.
34. Return to Gulum-Ei in the Winking Skeever and give him the Firebrand Wine Case. Follow him to the East Empire Company Warehouse without attacking the dock wardens in front of him.
35. Inside the warehouse, take the East Empire Shipping Map from the Dockmaster's office. Leave `Heavy Armor Forging` and `The Buying Game` closed.
36. Follow Gulum-Ei into Brinewater Grotto and discover the grotto. Near the end of the grotto, take and read `Children of the Sky` and `Hanging Gardens`.
37. Before confronting Gulum-Ei, make `HARD SAVE: HS-TG-GULUM-EI-CONFESSION`.
38. Branch route: from the hard save, kill Gulum-Ei, take and read Gulum-Ei's Confession. Do not continue from this branch save.
39. Reload `HARD SAVE: HS-TG-GULUM-EI-CONFESSION`.
40. Main route: spare Gulum-Ei. Ask him about Karliah and take the Goldenglow Bill of Sale he gives you. Gulum-Ei remains alive for later fence service.
41. Return to Mercer in the Ragged Flagon. Complete `Scoundrel's Folly`.
42. Speak to Tonilia for the promised armor improvement. Choose the Thieves Guild Armor chest piece as the upgraded item so the carry-weight enchantment improves from the basic set.
43. Give the East Empire Shipping Map to Delvin.
44. Prepare for cold travel before going north. Take a carriage to Windhelm Stables, discover the stables. Do not bring a follower, pet, or summon to the Snow Veil door; Mercer must be able to reach and open the locked entrance.
45. Meet Mercer outside Snow Veil Sanctum. Discover and enter the ruin. Snow Veil's normal cleared marker is unreliable for this route, so do not wait for a cleared tag.
46. Follow Mercer through Snow Veil. Leave `Three Thieves` closed. In the room with the hanging brazier trap, take the Model Ship before the trap scatters it.
47. In the final chamber, learn the Snow Veil word of Disarm. Leave `Ice and Chitin` closed.
48. Continue the scripted Karliah scene. When you wake up with Karliah, receive Gallus's Encoded Journal, complete `Speaking With Silence`.
49. Return to the Ragged Flagon when you are able. Give the Model Ship to Delvin.
50. Before leaving the guild, start a Thieves Guild restoration note for later. Record Markarth 0/5, Solitude 0/5, Whiterun 0/5, Windhelm 0/5, total Delvin/Vex jobs 0/125, and job types still uncompleted: Numbers, Fishing, Bedlam, Burglary, Shill, Sweep, and Heist. Leave every count at zero for now.
51. Stop with `Hard Answers` active. Do not speak to Enthir, go to Calcelmo for the translation, or complete the Nightingale Blade handoff until the level-46 Thieves reward section routes it.
52. Make a rotating manual save.

### Riften Thaneship, Frost, and Rift Roads

1. Start in Riften after `Speaking With Silence`, with `Hard Answers` still untouched. Use Riften as the service base for this section. Sell ordinary loot, sleep if a level-up is waiting, carry food, lockpicks, healing, and at least one pickaxe, then make a rotating manual save.
2. If From-Deepest-Fathoms has not yet approached you at the Riften Docks and you are now level 14 or higher, visit the docks and accept the Lexicon from her. If she is still absent, skip the Avanchnzel step later in this section and return when she has appeared.
3. Go to the Temple of Mara. Speak to Dinya Balu and accept `The Book of Love`. In the Hall of the Dead below the temple, speak to Alessandra and accept `Pilgrimage`; carry Alessandra's Dagger for the later Whiterun handoff.
4. At Haelga's Bunkhouse, speak to Svana Far-Shield and accept `Caught Red Handed`.
5. Collect the three Marks of Dibella from Bolli, Indaryn, and Hofgrir Horse-Crusher. Use clean dialogue checks where they work; if a mark must be pickpocketed, make a rotating manual save first. Do not kill any of the three men.
6. Return to Haelga's Bunkhouse, confront Haelga, then speak to Svana. Complete `Caught Red Handed`.
7. Go to Black-Briar Meadery. Take and read `Attention Employees!`. Speak to Romlyn Dreth and accept his mead delivery for Wilhelm at the Vilemyr Inn.
8. Enter Riften Jail and speak to Sibbi Black-Briar about Svidi. Accept `The Lover's Requital`, learn that Svidi may be hiding under another name, and leave Sibbi's Frost business for the later Frost handoff in this section.
9. Travel to Ivarstead. At the Vilemyr Inn, give Romlyn's keg to Wilhelm, complete `Under the Table`. Romlyn is now a non-default spouse option.
10. Still at the Vilemyr Inn, speak to Lynly Star-Sung about Svidi. Use Intimidate if the Persuade check fails, and do not kill Lynly.
11. For `The Book of Love`, speak to Fastred, Boti, and Jofthor. Choose Klimmek as Fastred's partner, speak to Klimmek, then stay close enough to watch the conversation between Klimmek and Fastred.
12. At Fellstar Farm, enter only when the house is unlocked and legal to enter. Take Wylandriah's Spoon from Wylandriah's Satchel on the table.
13. Return to Riften. Tell Sibbi that Svidi went east to Morrowind, receive Sibbi's Stash Key, then loot Sibbi's chest in the Black-Briar Meadery basement. Complete `The Lover's Requital`.
14. Return to Dinya Balu in the Temple of Mara and report the Ivarstead success. Leave `The Book of Love` active at the Markarth stage.
15. From Ivarstead, clear Pinepeak Cavern. Take the bear pelts from the routed bears for Temba, and leave `Heavy Armor Forging` closed.
16. Clear Honeystrand Cave. Take the bear pelts from the routed bears for Temba, and leave `The Locked Room` closed.
17. On the road east of Nilheim, speak to Telrav and agree to escort him. Dismount before following him, and keep any follower, pet, or horse away from the bridge approach.
18. Follow Telrav to Nilheim, let him reveal the ambush, then kill Telrav and the bandits. Clear Nilheim, complete `Telrav's Request`.
19. Clear Rift Watchtower. Leave `Hallgerd's Tale` closed.
20. Clear Clearspring Tarn by killing the troll in Clearspring Cave below it.
21. At Treva's Watch, speak to Stalleo outside the fort and accept `Infiltration`.
22. Use the secret tunnel, open the main gate from inside, and clear Treva's Watch. Take and read Spell Tome: Bound Sword and Spell Tome: Courage from the fort. Leave `Twin Secrets` closed.
23. Return to Stalleo, complete `Infiltration`, take and read Spell Tome: Detect Life.
24. At Angarvunde, leave `A Dance in Fire, v7` closed at the outside camp. Enter the ruin and speak to Medresi Dran.
25. Kill the first three draugr, return to Medresi, take the key she gives you, then clear both Angarvunde side zones and pull both gate levers. Do not pickpocket Medresi's key.
26. Follow Medresi through the opened gate, let her scripted death happen, and continue to the true treasure chamber. Learn the Animal Allegiance word, clear Angarvunde, complete `Angarvunde`. Leave `Before the Ages of Man` and `Advances in Lockpicking` closed.
27. Clear Snapleg Cave. Leave `Breathing Water` closed.
28. Travel to Shor's Stone and discover it.
29. Speak to Filnjar about Redbelly Mine and accept `Mine or Yours`. Ask him for the ore sample for Hafjorg as part of `Truth Ore Consequences`.
30. Speak to Sylgja and accept her satchel delivery to Verner at Darkwater Crossing.
