31. Mine one iron ore from the veins north of Sylgja's house, then sell that ore to Grogmar gro-Burzag.
32. Clear Redbelly Mine of spiders, return to Filnjar, complete `Mine or Yours`. Filnjar is now a non-default spouse option.
33. Discover Shor's Watchtower.
34. Travel to Darkwater Crossing, discover it. Give Sylgja's Satchel to Verner Rock-Chucker and receive Verner's Satchel.
35. If you are level 18 or higher, ask the Darkwater Crossing residents about Derkeethus, then enter Darkwater Pass. If you are below level 18, leave Darkwater Pass and `Extracting an Argonian` for the later Eastmarch support route.
36. In Darkwater Pass, find Derkeethus, release him from the pit, stay close to him, and escort him out through the nearest safe exit. Leave `Catalogue of Weapon Enchantments` closed. When Derkeethus reaches safety, complete `Extracting an Argonian`.
37. Return to Shor's Stone, give Verner's Satchel to Sylgja, complete `Special Delivery: Sylgja`. Sylgja is now a non-default spouse option.
38. Enter Northwind Mine from the lower mine entrance near Shor's Stone. Traverse the mine and leave `Death Blow of Abernanit` closed.
39. Exit to Northwind Summit. Defeat the dragon, learn the Aura Whisper word.
40. Return to Riften and give Filnjar's ore sample to Hafjorg. Complete `Truth Ore Consequences`.
41. Report to Jarl Laila Law-Giver that you are ready to deal with Cragslane Cavern for `Supply and Demand`, then travel to Cragslane Cavern.
42. Clear Cragslane Cavern. Leave `The Wolf Queen, v1` closed. Return to the Jarl, complete `Supply and Demand`.
43. Discover Merryfair Farm. Speak to Dravin Llanith and accept `Bow to the Master`.
44. Return to the Ragged Flagon. From the Ragged Flagon, enter the Ratway Vaults through the door right of the bar, recover Dravin's Bow from the chest near the stairs, and return it to Dravin. Complete `Bow to the Master`.
45. Discover Lost Prospect Mine before starting Frost's quest.
46. At Heartwood Mill, discover the mill. Speak to Grosta and accept `Flight or Fight`.
47. Clear Broken Helm Hollow. Pull the hidden chain near the raised camp area, find Leifnarr's body only after `Flight or Fight` is active, and leave `Battle of Sancre Tor` closed. Return to Grosta, complete `Flight or Fight`.
48. Discover Sarethi Farm. Speak to Avrusa Sarethi and accept `Smooth Jazbay`; begin saving Jazbay Grapes for her. Collect any visible Nirnroot around the farm and lake shore for Ingun's `Few and Far Between`, but do not complete Ingun's ingredient quest until the guide names the full 20/20/20 turn-in.
49. Discover Snow-Shod Farm.
50. Clear Lost Tongue Overlook, learn the Dismay word.
51. Discover The Shadow Stone. Keep the Warrior Stone active for now.
52. Clear Autumnshade Clearing. Leave `Father Of The Niben`, `Racial Phylogeny`, and `The Gold Ribbon of Merit` closed.
53. Clear Boulderfall Cave. Leave `Herbalist's Guide to Skyrim` closed.
54. At Darklight Tower, enter through the lower entrance, speak to Illia, and accept her plan to stop Silvia. Leave `The Aetherium Wars`, `2920, Sun's Dawn, v2`, `Mystery of Talara, v3`, `Horror of Castle Xyr`, and `Proper Lock Design` closed as you climb.
55. Let Illia's plan play out, then defeat Silvia. Take the Staff of Hag's Wrath, complete `Repentance`.
56. Travel to Froki's Shack, discover it, and accept `Kyne's Sacred Trials` from Froki Whetted-Blade. Defeat the Guardian Mudcrab west of Broken Fang Cave and southeast of Gjukar's Monument, the Guardian Wolf southwest of The Guardian Stones, and the Guardian Skeever around Windward Ruins, then return to Froki. Defeat the Guardian Bear south of Knifepoint Ridge, the Guardian Mammoth west of Loreius Farm, and the Guardian Sabre Cat west of Bleakcoast Cave, then return to Froki again. Clear Graywinter Watch, leave `Vernaccus and Bourlor` closed on the barrel, take Froki's Bow, defeat the Guardian Troll Spirit, then return to Froki for Kyne's Token.
57. With a Daedra Heart and Troll Fat ready, travel to Largashbur and complete `The Cursed Tribe` while clearing Largashbur, Fallowstone Cave, and Giant's Grove. Help the stronghold survive the giant attack, follow Yamarz through Fallowstone Cave, defeat the Giant's Grove giant yourself when Yamarz tries to bargain, take Shagrol's Warhammer, return it to Largashbur, place it on the shrine, and take Volendrung. Leave `Battle of Red Mountain` in Largashbur Cellar and `Hallgerd's Tale` north-northwest of Fallowstone Cave closed for the Scholar's Insight reading window. Record Oblivion Walker progress at 3 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
58. Sweep the remaining southeast Rift and Dayspring side while Riften is still your base. Discover Rift Stormcloak Camp, Last Vigil, Gromm's Pass, and Stendarr's Beacon. Clear Stendarr's Beacon, Autumnwatch Tower, Kagrenzel, Crystaldrift Cave, Faldar's Tooth, Redwater Den, Ruins of Rkund, and Tolvald's Cave. At Autumnwatch Tower, learn the Marked for Death word and leave `Jornibret's Last Dance` closed on the top floor of the northern tower. Leave `Mystery of Talara, v 2` closed on Gadnor in Crystaldrift Cave.
59. Complete Forelhost as a full dragon-priest route from the Rift side. Speak to Captain Valmir outside Forelhost, enter the ruin, read Skorm Snow-Strider's Journal, take the Forelhost Well Key if you do not pick the well lock, read `The Exodus` in Forelhost Crypt, read `Note (Forelhost Crypt)`, `Note (Forelhost Refectory)`, `Orders`, and `Rahgot's Reply` as you pass them, take and preserve the Glass Claw, set the claw door to Fox-Owl-Snake, defeat Rahgot, preserve Rahgot and Dragon Priest Staff (Rahgot), learn Storm Call: Strun on the battlements, then kill Valmir after his betrayal and take `Valmir's Orders`.
60. If `Unfathomable Depths` is active, enter Avanchnzel with the Lexicon. If From-Deepest-Fathoms has not given you the Lexicon yet, skip Avanchnzel until she appears.
61. In Avanchnzel, follow the Lexicon route to the receptacle. Leave `Racial Phylogeny` closed. Place the Lexicon, receive Ancient Knowledge, complete `Unfathomable Depths`.
62. Return to Riften. Use city merchants, Tonilia, Vekel, Mallus, or Gulum-Ei to sell ordinary loot from this Rift pass until you have 8,000 gold set aside for Honeyside.
63. Speak to Louis Letrush in the Bee and Barb and accept `Promises to Keep`.
64. Enter Riften Jail and speak to Sibbi Black-Briar about Frost. Learn where Frost's lineage papers are kept, and ask Sibbi for his strongbox key if the dialogue is available.
65. Travel to Black-Briar Lodge, discover it. Enter the lodge, take Frost's Identity Papers from the basement, loot Sibbi's strongbox if you obtained the key. Leave `Legend of Krately House` closed.
66. In the upper bedroom, take the Stone of Barenziah.
67. Steal Frost from the lodge stable and ride him to Louis Letrush's handoff point. Before speaking to Louis, make `HARD SAVE: HS-RIFT-FROST-LETRUSH`.
68. Main route: persuade Louis to let you keep Frost. If Persuade is unavailable or fails, use the threat/intimidation outcome that leaves Frost usable as your mount. Complete `Promises to Keep`.
69. Return to Riften. Speak to Anuriel, buy Honeyside for the normal 8,000-gold price. Do not enter Honeyside before the Jarl names you Thane.
70. Immediately speak to Jarl Laila Law-Giver after buying Honeyside. Accept the Thane of the Rift title and the thane weapon reward.
71. Enter Honeyside once, confirm it is usable as a Riften sleep/storage point, then leave. Do not buy furnishings yet; the full furnishing, display, household, and steward checks happen in the later homes section.
72. Stable or park Frost somewhere safe, dismount before fights, and reload if Frost dies. End the section in Riften or Honeyside with Survival Mode on and a rotating manual save.
### Nightingale Armor Readiness Check

1. Stay in Riften or Honeyside after the Rift road section. If Survival Mode is waiting on a level-up, sleep in Honeyside or at the Bee and Barb so your displayed level is current before checking it.
2. Open the Skills menu and check your level.
3. If you are level 32 or higher, record in your route notes: `Nightingale Armor threshold reached`. This is only a readiness note; Nightingale Armor has not been acquired.
4. If you are below level 32, record in your route notes: `Nightingale Armor threshold not reached yet`, then continue to the Reach section. Do not grind here; the next route sections provide explicit quest and location work.
5. Confirm that `Hard Answers` is still active but untouched after Karliah's handoff. The Thieves Guild order from this point is `Hard Answers`, `The Pursuit`, `Trinity Restored`, `Blindsighted`, then `Darkness Returns`.
6. Keep the late Thieves chain and restoration jobs closed. Do not speak to Enthir about translating Gallus's journal, do not start Calcelmo's Museum work for `Hard Answers`, do not enter Riftweald Manor, do not go to Nightingale Hall, and do not accept Delvin or Vex jobs yet.
7. Make a rotating manual save in Riften or Honeyside before leaving for the Reach.

## Reach, Markarth, Aetherium, and Peryite

### Markarth, Nchuand-Zel, and Old Hroldan

1. Start from Riften or Honeyside. Sleep first if Survival Mode has a level-up waiting, unload Frost somewhere safe, and sell ordinary loot until you have a few hundred gold free for Markarth services, Yngvar's poem, and local purchases.
2. Take the carriage from Riften Stables to Markarth. Arrive at Markarth Stables.
3. Speak to Banning at the stables. Ask about the dogs, accept the Spiced Beef delivery for Voada, and note that Vigilance is available here as a paid non-default dog follower option. Do not buy Vigilance for the main route.
4. Walk east to Salvius Farm, discover it. Harvest a few potatoes from the farm if needed, then sell potatoes to Vigdis Salvius once for the farm-work activity.
5. Speak to Rogatus Salvius at the farm, accept the letter for his son.
6. Visit Left Hand Mine, discover it. Mine or take one iron ore and sell it to Skaggi Scar-Face for the mining-work activity.
7. Enter Markarth, discover the city.
8. In the market, the Weylin and Margret incident starts as you arrive. Stop Weylin only if you can do it cleanly without a bounty; otherwise let the guards handle him. Take Eltrys' Note when Eltrys approaches, then read it so `The Forsworn Conspiracy` points to the Shrine of Talos. Leave the Shrine of Talos for the next section.
9. Speak to Kerah at her jewelry stall and accept the ring delivery to Calcelmo.
10. Go to The Hag's Cure, speak to Bothela, and accept the Stallion's Potion delivery for the steward.
11. Find Omluag in The Warrens or near the smelter and accept his problem with Mulush gro-Shugurz. Speak to Mulush at the smelter and resolve it with Persuade or Bribe if possible; if a brawl is required, fight barehanded only. Return to Omluag, complete `A Few Words with You`.
12. Enter Understone Keep, discover it.
13. Deliver Calcelmo's Ring to Calcelmo.
14. Speak to Calcelmo about `The Book of Love`. Accept the task to get advice from Yngvar the Singer.
15. Ask Calcelmo about the excavation and accept `Nimhe, the Poisoned One`. He gives you access to Nchuand-Zel.
16. Buy the Dwarven Armored Mudcrab ownership deed from Calcelmo. Read the `Spell Tome: Teleport Pet Mudcrab`, meet the mudcrab beside Calcelmo's work area, and send it to Honeyside, Riften, or another city rather than keeping it with you for the next prison-heavy Markarth section.
17. Deliver Banning's Spiced Beef to Voada in the Understone Keep kitchen.
18. Deliver Bothela's Stallion's Potion to Raerek. Before leaving Understone Keep, speak to Jarl Igmund and ask whether he has work for you. Accept his `Kill the Forsworn Leader` assignment, record the exact target, and leave it active for the target-aware Reach redoubt pass. Starting Igmund's work now protects the later Vlindrel Hall purchase route before the Markarth prison state changes.
19. Speak to Ondolemar in Understone Keep and accept `Search and Seizure`. Go to Ogmund's house, take Ogmund's Amulet of Talos, return it to Ondolemar, complete the quest.
20. Go to the Silver-Blood Inn. Give Bolli's Purchase Agreement to Kleppr, complete Bolli's `Special Delivery`.
21. Find Yngvar the Singer in the Silver-Blood Inn during the day or the Treasury House at night. Buy the Love Poem for 200 gold.
22. Make a rotating manual save before the Calcelmo/Faleen handoffs. Deliver the Love Poem to Faleen, receive Faleen's Letter to Calcelmo.
23. Give Faleen's letter to Calcelmo, follow him until he and Faleen finish their scene, and let this `The Book of Love` stage update. The quest now points back to Dinya Balu for its final Ruki and Fenrig stage; it is not complete yet, and the Agent of Mara reward remains unclaimed.
24. Make a rotating manual save before entering Nchuand-Zel. Enter from Understone Keep, kill Nimhe, then cut through the webbing and take Alethius's Notes from Alethius. Read the notes from your inventory to start `The Lost Expedition`.
25. Continue through Nchuand-Zel in journal order. Take Stromm's Diary first, then Erj's Notes, Krag's Journal, and Staubin's Diary. Take each journal once; do not read a journal from the ground and then take a second copy.
26. In Nchuand-Zel Control, pull the defense lever. If the quest arrow points back toward the Armory afterward, ignore the arrow and exit to Calcelmo.
27. Report to Calcelmo that Nimhe is dead and report the expedition journals. Complete `Nimhe, the Poisoned One` and `The Lost Expedition`.
28. Use the Dwemer Museum access gained from Calcelmo. In the first room on the left, take the Stone of Barenziah. Take only the Stone here; leave Stendarr's Hammer, Calcelmo's Laboratory, and Calcelmo's Tower for their later routed sections.
29. Leave Markarth by the road toward Old Hroldan. Discover The Lover Stone, and keep your current standing stone active.
30. Continue to Soljund's Sinkhole, discover it.
31. Speak to Perth outside the mine and accept the draugr-clearing job. Clear Soljund's Sinkhole while leaving `Jornibret's Last Dance` closed. If `Boethiah's Proving` appears in the mine because you are level 24 or higher, leave that closed too. Return to Perth, complete `Soljund's Sinkhole`. The location does not need a cleared-map tag for this route; the quest turn-in is the completion marker. Perth is now a non-default spouse option.
32. After Perth is back at work, mine or carry one moonstone ore and sell it to him for the mining-work activity.
33. Continue to Old Hroldan Inn, discover it. Before entering the inn, harvest one sample of Juniper Berries from the bushes around Old Hroldan and keep it for Elmus at Thirsk.
34. Deliver Rogatus's Letter to Leontius Salvius, complete `Rogatus's Letter`.
35. Rent Tiber Septim's room from Eydis, sleep in that room, speak to the Ghost of Old Hroldan, then speak to Eydis. Record the exact Hjalti's Sword target that appears in your quest log. The target will be one of Broken Tower Redoubt, Bruca's Leap Redoubt, Deepwood Redoubt, Dragon Bridge Overlook, Druadach Redoubt, Hag Rock Redoubt, Red Eagle Redoubt, or Serpent's Bluff Redoubt. Do not pursue the sword until a later section names your actual target. Skuli at the inn remains a later non-default adoption option.
36. Return to Markarth. Use the Silver-Blood Inn and city merchants for rest, food, and selling, then make a rotating manual save before the next Markarth section.

### Markarth Prison, Daedric Rites, and Reach Redoubts

1. Start in Markarth with the Dwarven Armored Mudcrab sent away and no animal or pet follower active. If any Delvin or Vex special job is somehow complete but unreported, report it before entering the Shrine of Talos sequence.
2. Reduce carried junk before the prison handoff. Keep food, potions, lockpicks, and normal combat gear, but do not use any hotkeyed quest-item smuggling trick for Cidhna Mine.
3. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-MARKARTH-CIDHNA-ARREST`.
4. Go to the Shrine of Talos and speak to Eltrys. Accept the investigation into Weylin, Margret, Thonar, and Nepos.
5. Go to the Silver-Blood Inn. If Margret survived the market attack, speak to her about why she came to Markarth. In either case, enter Margret's room, take Margret's Journal from the end table, read it for the quest stage.
6. Go to The Warrens and ask Garvey for access to Weylin's room. Use a speech option, bribe, pick the lock, or take the key cleanly. In Weylin's room, take Weylin's Note from the chest, read it.
7. When Dryston confronts you outside The Warrens, keep the fight barehanded. After he yields, make a rotating manual save and pickpocket Dryston's Note from him. Reload if the pickpocket creates a bounty or hostile city state.
8. Go to the Treasury House. Make a rotating manual save, enter Thonar Silver-Blood's room, pickpocket Thonar's Journal from him, read it.
9. In the Treasury House bedroom, take the Stone of Barenziah. Leave the `Vernaccus and Bourlor` skill-book copy closed.
10. Go to Nepos's House. Make a rotating manual save, pickpocket Nepos's Journal from Nepos, read it before the confrontation. Then speak to Nepos and survive the household attack. Leave the `Mystery of Talara, Part 4` skill-book copy closed.
11. Return to the Shrine of Talos. When the guards frame you for Eltrys' murder, submit and go to Cidhna Mine.
12. In Cidhna Mine, speak to Uraccen about Madanach. Get past Borkul by a clean speech option, brawl, bribe, or the skooma-to-Grisvar shiv route. Leave the `Proper Lock Design` skill-book copy closed.
13. Speak to Madanach, hear Braig's story, return to Madanach, and accept the escape plan. Kill Grisvar when Madanach requires it, then follow Madanach through the escape route.
14. In Markarth Ruins, wait until Kaie returns your confiscated inventory and gives you the Armor, Boots, Gauntlets, and Helmet of the Old Gods. Confirm all normal gear is back.
15. Before leaving the ruins into the city, make a rotating manual save if the game allows it. Kill Madanach and the escaping Forsworn in the ruins, then loot Madanach's Note from Madanach.
16. Exit to Markarth and speak to Thonar Silver-Blood when he approaches. Receive the Silver-Blood Family Ring.
17. When the quest closes and your inventory is back, add `The Forsworn Conspiracy` and `No One Escapes Cidhna Mine` to Sideways tracking.
18. Rest, sell, and resupply in Markarth before leaving for the Reach redoubts. Keep pets dismissed until the redoubt work is done.
19. Find Vigilant Tyranus outside the Abandoned House. Agree to help him and go inside immediately. Continue through the house until Molag Bal sends you to find Logrolf.
20. Record Logrolf's actual Forsworn target. It will be Broken Tower Redoubt, Bruca's Leap Redoubt, Deepwood Redoubt, Druadach Redoubt, Hag Rock Redoubt, or Red Eagle Redoubt. Do not kill Logrolf while he is captive.
21. Speak to Lisbet at Arnleif and Sons Trading Company and accept `Lisbet's Missing Shipment`. Record her actual target: Broken Tower Redoubt, Bruca's Leap Redoubt, Deepwood Redoubt, Dragon Bridge Overlook, Druadach Redoubt, Hag Rock Redoubt, Red Eagle Redoubt, or Serpent's Bluff Redoubt.
22. Speak to Degaine in the market or at the Silver-Blood Inn and accept his Temple of Dibella statue job.
23. Go to the Temple of Dibella and make a rotating manual save. Enter the Inner Sanctum cleanly, steal the Statue of Dibella without being caught, leave the temple, return the statue to Degaine, complete his job.
24. Return to the Temple of Dibella, enter the Inner Sanctum again, and let Hamal confront you. Accept the task to find the new Sybil. Do not threaten the priestesses.
25. Go to Understone Keep and speak to Brother Verulus about the Hall of the Dead. Use Persuade or Bribe to get the key, then enter Markarth's Hall of the Dead. Leave `2920, Rain's Hand, v4` and Caravan Captain's Note closed.
26. Speak to Eola in the Hall of the Dead and choose a non-hostile answer that starts `The Taste of Death`. Return to Verulus, tell him the Hall of the Dead is safe, receive his Amulet of Arkay.
27. Travel to Karthwasten, discover it. Speak to Enmon for the Sybil route here and tell him to stay in town rather than escorting you.
28. Build this section's active target list: Broken Tower Redoubt for Fjotra, Igmund's Forsworn leader target, Logrolf's target, Lisbet's target, and the Hjalti's Sword target you recorded at Old Hroldan. If the same location appears more than once, clear it once and handle every active objective there before leaving, including killing Igmund's marked Forsworn leader if that marker points there.
29. Clear Broken Tower Redoubt. Rescue Fjotra for `The Heart of Dibella`. If Logrolf, Lisbet's statue, or Hjalti's Sword is also in Broken Tower, handle those objectives on the same pass. Leave `The Mirror` and `Liminal Bridges` closed.
30. If Logrolf's target, Lisbet's target, or Hjalti's target is Bruca's Leap Redoubt, clear Bruca's Leap Redoubt, free Logrolf or retrieve the quest item there, leave `A Tragedy in Black` closed.
31. If Logrolf's target, Lisbet's target, or Hjalti's target is Deepwood Redoubt, clear Deepwood Redoubt, free Logrolf or retrieve the quest item there, leave `Sacred Witness` and `The Legend of Red Eagle` closed.
32. If Lisbet's target or Hjalti's target is Dragon Bridge Overlook, clear Dragon Bridge Overlook, retrieve the quest item there.
33. If Logrolf's target, Lisbet's target, or Hjalti's target is Druadach Redoubt, clear Druadach Redoubt, free Logrolf or retrieve the quest item there, leave `The Rear Guard` closed.
34. If Logrolf's target, Lisbet's target, or Hjalti's target is Hag Rock Redoubt, clear Hag Rock Redoubt, free Logrolf or retrieve the quest item there, leave `2920, Last Seed, v8` closed.
35. If Logrolf's target, Lisbet's target, or Hjalti's target is Red Eagle Redoubt, read `The Legend of Red Eagle` at the boss camp, take Red Eagle's Fury from the Forsworn leader, leave `Reality & Other Falsehoods` closed, take Red Eagle's Rite at Sundered Towers, and immediately finish the Rebel's Cairn route so the sword converts into Red Eagle's Bane.
36. If Lisbet's target or Hjalti's target is Serpent's Bluff Redoubt, clear Serpent's Bluff Redoubt, retrieve the quest item there, learn `Spell Tome: Frostbite` if you have not already learned it, and leave `Twin Secrets` closed.
37. Go to Liar's Retreat while you are in the Karthwasten and Broken Tower area. Clear the cave, take The Longhammer from Rahd's body area, leave `Biography of the Wolf Queen` closed.
38. Go to Blind Cliff Cave, free Melka, follow her through Blind Cliff Bastion, and kill Petra. Take the Eye of Melka reward, leave `The Refugees`, `Mystery of Talara, Part 4`, and `2920, Rain's Hand, v4` closed. Add `The Affairs of Hagravens` to Sideways tracking.
39. Return Hjalti's Sword to the Ghost of Old Hroldan if you retrieved it in the redoubt pass, and add it to Hero of the People tracking.
40. Return Lisbet's Dibella Statue to Lisbet. Complete `Lisbet's Missing Shipment`, and add it to Hero of the People tracking.
41. If Logrolf was freed from his target, make `HARD SAVE: HS-MOLAG-LOGROLF-FREED`, then return to the Abandoned House. Follow Logrolf to the altar, complete Molag Bal's scripted finale, take the Mace of Molag Bal. Record Oblivion Walker progress at 4 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
42. Return Fjotra to the Temple of Dibella. Pray at Dibella's altar when Hamal tells you to, complete the quest, and gain Agent of Dibella. Add `The Heart of Dibella` to Sideways tracking. Anwen, Orla, and Senna are now non-default spouse options.
43. Travel to Reachcliff Cave and meet Eola outside. Clear Reachcliff Cave with her, leave `The Doors of Oblivion` closed, speak to Eola at Namira's shrine. Exit through the newly opened secret exit, discover Reachcliff Secret Entrance.
44. Return to Markarth's Hall of the Dead and convince Brother Verulus to follow you. Use Bribe if Persuade does not work.
45. Lead Verulus back through Reachcliff Secret Entrance to Namira's shrine. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DAEDRIC-NAMIRA-FEAST` before speaking to Eola at the table.
46. BRANCH ROUTE: from `HS-DAEDRIC-NAMIRA-FEAST`, kill Eola at the feast, save Verulus, fight the hostile guests, record the branch outcome, then reload the hard save.
47. MAIN ROUTE: let Eola put Verulus on the altar, kill Verulus when directed, feed from his body, receive the Ring of Namira. Record Oblivion Walker progress at 5 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
48. Leave Reachcliff Cave and return to Markarth. If Igmund's Forsworn leader target was handled in the redoubt pass, report it to Igmund in Understone Keep before sleeping or leaving the city. Use the Silver-Blood Inn and city merchants to rest and sell, then make a rotating manual save.

### Arkngthamz and the First Aetherium Shards

1. Start in Markarth with food, healing, lockpicks, a ranged attack, and enough carry space for Dwemer loot you actually want to keep. Make a rotating manual save before leaving the city.
2. Return to the Dwemer Museum under Calcelmo's access. Read the copy of `The Aetherium Wars` on the table in the southwestern corner near the Dwemer ballista display; you do not need to steal or carry the book.
3. Travel to Arkngthamz. Discover the ruin. Make a rotating manual save outside the entrance before going in.
4. Enter Arkngthamz and accept Katria's help when her ghost confronts you. This starts `Lost to the Ages`; keep it active, but do not treat the quest as complete yet.
5. Reach Katria's body, take Katria's Journal for the puzzle, and read it when the route or journal asks for the lock clues. This journal is a quest aid, not a checklist book for this route.
6. Continue through Arkngthamz with Katria until she points out her bow on the log. Take Zephyr carefully.
7. At the tonal lock, take the dead adventurer's Scrap of Paper for the in-game hint. Strike the resonators in this order: lower left, lower right, upper left, upper right, lower middle.
8. Enter the treasury, take the Arkngthamz Aetherium Shard from the podium. Record `Lost to the Ages` at 1 of 4 shards.
9. Leave Arkngthamz through the water tunnel shortcut only if the jump is clean; otherwise walk back out through the ruin. Once outside, make a rotating manual save.
10. Travel to Deep Folk Crossing in the northern Reach. Discover the landmark.
11. Cross the bridge, follow the northward path up to the stone plinth, and take the Deep Folk Crossing Aetherium Shard. Record `Lost to the Ages` at 2 of 4 shards.
12. Leave the Dwemer convector alone for the College route. Make a rotating manual save after the second shard.

### Peryite's Shrine and Bthardamz

1. Start from Deep Folk Crossing with the saved Deathbell from Falkreath and the saved Vampire Dust from Bloodlet Throne. Bring food, healing, lockpicks, a ranged option, and enough carry space for a heavy shield. Make a rotating manual save before leaving the Deep Folk Crossing area.
2. Travel to the capsized rowboat in the river north of Reachwater Rock, below the Karthspire/Sky Haven river bend. Loot the strongbox and loose gems there, take the Flawless Ruby, and keep it for Peryite. Do not enter Reachwater Rock.
3. Go to Karthwasten and the Fenn's Gulch Mine end of town. Take one loose Silver Ingot from the smelter area outside the mine. If the ingot is owned on your save, leave it and buy one Silver Ingot from Moth gro-Bagol in Markarth instead.
4. Travel to the Shrine to Peryite. Discover the shrine. Leave `The Buying Game` closed on Kesh's table.
5. Make a rotating manual save at the shrine. Speak to Kesh the Clean and ask to commune with Peryite. Wait for the four ingredient objectives to appear.
6. Give Kesh the Deathbell, Flawless Ruby, Silver Ingot, and Vampire Dust.
7. Let Kesh finish mixing the incense. Do not talk to him while he is working at the cauldron. When he tells you the incense is ready, inhale the fumes and receive Peryite's order to kill Orchendor.
8. Travel to Bthardamz. Discover the ruin. Enter through the exterior ruins and proceed into Bthardamz Upper District.
9. Work through the Upper District and Workshop. In the Workshop mage's room, take and read Afflicted's Note from the desk.
10. Continue through the Lower District, Study, and Arcanex toward Orchendor. Leave the Bthardamz skill-book copies closed, including `Biography of the Wolf Queen` in the optional Dwelling room and `Reality & Other Falsehoods` on Orchendor.
11. Kill Orchendor and loot the Key to Bthardamz Elevator from him.
12. Use the elevator key to exit Bthardamz to Skyrim. After Orchendor is dead and you have left through the elevator, continue even if the map never shows a cleared tag; this route uses Orchendor's death and the elevator exit as the completion boundary.
13. Return to the Shrine to Peryite. Inhale the fumes again, speak to Peryite, receive Spellbreaker. Record Oblivion Walker progress at 6 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
14. Preserve Spellbreaker. Return to Markarth for a warm support stop, sell ordinary loot, sleep in a bed if Fatigue or a pending level-up requires it, and make a rotating manual save. At the Silver-Blood Inn, read and take Looter's Note from the bar to start `Mightier than the Sword`. If the note is clipped into the bar, save and try different activation angles; use only a non-hostile repositioning method if needed, and reload if anyone becomes hostile or a bounty starts.
15. Before leaving Markarth for Reachwind Eyrie, make a rotating manual save. Travel to Reachwind Eyrie southeast of Markarth and northeast of Dushnikh Yal. If the quest marker or the three looters fail to appear when you approach, reload the pre-approach save and try again before entering the tower. Defeat the looters, enter the tower, and leave `Chimarvamidium` closed. At the top of the tower, loot Casival for `The Crimson Dirks, v9`, read and take Casival's Note, then take and preserve Dwarven Plate Armor and Dwarven Plate Boots from the table and floor nearby. Complete `Mightier than the Sword`.
16. Use Markarth and Old Hroldan as support for the remaining Reach roads. Discover Bard's Leap Summit, Dushnikh Yal, Runoff Caverns, and the Sundered Towers marker tied to Red Eagle's route. At Dushnikh Yal, enter Burguk's Longhouse and take the Dragonfly in a Jar from the cellar. Clear Cradle Stone Tower, Four Skull Lookout, Gloomreach, and Lost Valley Redoubt. Leave `Sithis` at Cradle Stone Tower closed for the Scholar's Insight reading window.
17. Clear Valthume as the full `Evil in Waiting` route. Speak to Valdar, collect the three Opaque Vessels, take and preserve the Iron Claw, set the claw door to Dragon - Hawk - Wolf, learn Aura Whisper from the word wall, perform the ritual, defeat Hevnoraak, and preserve Hevnoraak and Hevnoraak's Staff.
18. Clear Ragnvald north of Markarth. Take Saerek's Skull Key from Ragnvald Crypts and Torsten's Skull Key from Ragnvald Canal, place both keys in Otar's sarcophagus, defeat Otar the Mad, preserve Otar and the Staff of the Storm Wall, and learn the Kyne's Peace word from the wall.
19. Return to Markarth or another warm support hub, unload the dragon-priest masks, staves, claws, keys, books, and ordinary loot, then make a rotating manual save before the Windhelm route.

### Whiterun Mephala Interlude

1. Confirm you are level 20 or higher and that `Dragon Rising` is complete. Travel to Whiterun by carriage from the support hub, bring stealth or Pickpocket support for one key theft, and make `HARD SAVE: HS-DAEDRIC-WHISPERING-DOOR` before asking for rumors.
2. Enter The Bannered Mare and ask Hulda for rumors until she mentions strange tales about Jarl Balgruuf's children. If she gives an older rumor first, do not follow that rumor; ask again. If no rumor appears, reload the hard save, re-enter the inn, and ask again before leaving Whiterun.
3. Go to Dragonsreach and speak to Jarl Balgruuf about his children. Find Nelkir in Dragonsreach and ask through his dialogue until he points you to the Whispering Lady behind the basement door.
4. In Dragonsreach, go through the kitchen to the servant's room closet and listen at the Old Wooden Door. Follow Mephala's dialogue until the quest sends you back to Nelkir.
5. Speak to Nelkir again and learn that Balgruuf and Farengar have the key. Do not kill Farengar and do not create a Whiterun assault or bounty state for this quest.
6. Make a rotating manual save, then pickpocket the Whispering Door Key from Farengar while hidden. If the attempt is detected, reload the rotating save and improve the odds with stealth gear, invisibility, a Pickpocket potion, or a better angle before trying again.
7. Return to the Old Wooden Door, unlock it with the key, enter the sealed room, read and take `Admonition Against Ebony`, then take the Ebony Blade. Complete `The Whispering Door` and record Oblivion Walker progress at 7 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
8. Do not power the Ebony Blade with friendly-NPC kills. The quest and Oblivion Walker only need the artifact acquisition. Store the Ebony Blade in a normal owned container, not on a weapon rack, and avoid carrying it into confiscation scenes.
9. Return to the warm support route state, sell ordinary loot, rest if Fatigue or a pending level-up requires it, and make a rotating manual save before the Windhelm route.

## Windhelm, Main Quest Staging, and Eastmarch

### Windhelm Murder Investigation and White Phial

1. Start after the Whiterun Mephala interlude with cold-weather food, lockpicks, healing, and enough carry space for quest books, a necklace, and several unique curios. Travel by carriage to Windhelm Stables, which should already be discovered from the Snow Veil Sanctum trip.
2. Before entering the city, make the short farm loop east of the stables. Discover Brandy-Mug Farm, harvest wheat from the field, sell wheat to Bolfrida Brandy-Mug.
3. Continue to Hlaalu Farm, discover it, harvest wheat from the field, and keep that wheat for Belyn Hlaalu in Windhelm.
4. Continue to Hollyfrost Farm, discover it, harvest wheat from the field, sell wheat to Tulvur.
5. Enter Windhelm through the main gate, discover the city. Go directly to The White Phial in the market district before starting other Windhelm business.
6. In The White Phial, speak to Nurelion and offer to retrieve the phial. Take Nurelion's Mixture and start `The White Phial`. Behind the staircase, take Wylandriah's Soul Gem from Wylandriah's Satchel. Leave `The Black Arts On Trial` closed.
7. If the Windhelm graveyard murder scene is already active after the shop visit, pause the Forsaken Cave trip and jump to step 13 now. If the murder scene is not active, continue with `The White Phial` first and use the return to Windhelm to start the murder investigation.
8. Travel west from Windhelm along the River Yorgrim to Forsaken Cave. Discover the cave.
9. Clear Forsaken Cave and Forsaken Crypt for `The White Phial`. In the first tomb zone, leave `The Mirror` closed. In the final chamber, leave `A Game at Dinner` closed, take the Cracked White Phial, and collect the loose Fire Salts for future hot food.
10. At the word wall behind Curalmil's sarcophagus, learn Marked for Death: Aus. After Curalmil is dead and the phial chamber is looted, exit by the shortcut.
11. Return to The White Phial. Give the Cracked White Phial to Nurelion, then speak to Quintus Navale for the real payment. Complete `The White Phial`, and add `The White Phial` to Sideways tracking. `Repairing the Phial` waits until after `The Throat of the World`.
12. Return to Windhelm at night, between 7pm and 7am, and go to the graveyard. If Susanna the Wicked's murder scene is not active, use Candlehearth Hall if you need a bed, leave through the main gate, re-enter at night, and check the graveyard again. Repeat the gate loop until the scene appears; do not start the investigation by picking Hjerim's lock or stealing a Hjerim key.
13. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-WINDHELM-BLOOD-ON-THE-ICE` before speaking to the guard at the graveyard. Keep this hard save untouched until the quest is complete.
14. Speak to the guard at the crime scene. Question Helgird, Calixto Corrium, and Silda the Unseen as witnesses, report back to the guard, then go to the Palace of the Kings and speak to Jorleif for permission to investigate. Discover the Palace of the Kings.
15. While you are in the Palace, go to Wuunferth the Unliving's upstairs room. Take the Stone of Barenziah from the table in that room.
16. Go to the Hall of the Dead and speak to Helgird about Susanna's body. Follow the blood trail to Hjerim, get the key from Tova Shatter-Shield, make a rotating manual save outside Hjerim, and enter only through the quest-approved key route.
17. Search Hjerim carefully. In the chest, take one `Beware the Butcher!` pamphlet and `Butcher Journal #1`. In the hidden room behind the false wardrobe, take `Butcher Journal #2`. Take the Strange Amulet from the shelf between the wardrobes.
18. During Calixto's business hours, go to Calixto's House of Curiosities. Pay for the tour, show him the Strange Amulet, and sell it to him for 500 gold so it can later become the Necromancer Amulet.
19. Find Viola Giordano with `Beware the Butcher!` in your inventory and tell her about the investigation. Then go directly to Wuunferth in the Palace and confront him about necromancy and the amulet. Do not accuse Wuunferth through Jorleif.
20. Follow Wuunferth's instruction and patrol the Stone Quarter at night. Catch Calixto during the attack, kill him, and loot the Necromancer Amulet and Calixto's Key from his body.
21. Return to Jorleif and report that Calixto was the Butcher. Complete `Blood on the Ice`. Do not add this quest to Sideways tracking.
22. Make a rotating manual save. Use Calixto's Key to enter Calixto's House of Curiosities, go to the upstairs chest, take and read `Butcher Journal`. Take Ysgramor's Soup Spoon and The Dancer's Flute from the museum displays; if either prompt is still marked as stealing, take it only while hidden and with no witness.
23. In the market or Candlehearth Hall, find Hillevi Cruel-Sea and accept her Nightshade Extract delivery if you have not already done so. Deliver it to Wuunferth in the Palace, complete the delivery. Add Hillevi's delivery to Hero of the People tracking.
24. Find Belyn Hlaalu in Windhelm, sell him the wheat from Hlaalu Farm.
25. Go to Candlehearth Hall. Speak to Adonato Leotelli, accept Adonato's Book for Giraud Gemane. Give one gold to Angrenor Once-Honored for his beggar favor if the dialogue is available; Angrenor becomes a non-default spouse and farmhand option. Leave the loose AE journals and Crimson Dirks books in the inn closed until this guide sends you to Cronvangr Cave or Yorgrim Overlook.
26. Go to Sadri's Used Wares. Speak to Revyn Sadri, accept Viola's Gold Ring, make a rotating manual save, plant the ring in Viola Giordano's dresser, and return to Revyn. Complete `That Was Always There`, and add Revyn's favor to Hero of the People tracking. Revyn is now a non-default spouse option and remains an investment target for the later Investor route.
27. Go to the Argonian Assemblage at Windhelm Docks. Speak to Stands-In-Shallows and accept the Double-Distilled Skooma favor. Go to New Gnisis Cornerclub, steal the bottle from under the second set of stairs while hidden, return it to Stands-In-Shallows. Add Stands-In-Shallows' favor to Hero of the People tracking.
28. Still at the docks, speak to Scouts-Many-Marshes about Torbjorn Shatter-Shield. Find Torbjorn in Windhelm, use Persuade if it succeeds, otherwise use Bribe or Intimidate. If Intimidate starts a brawl, fight barehanded only, with no follower, pet, summon, weapon, poison, spell, or shout. Return to Scouts, complete `A Few Words with You`, and add Scouts' favor to Hero of the People tracking. Scouts-Many-Marshes is now a non-default spouse option.
29. Sleep at Candlehearth Hall if Fatigue or a pending level-up needs a bed. Sell ordinary loot in Windhelm only through normal barter. Make a rotating manual save before leaving the city.

### High Hrothgar, Ustengrav, Morthal, and the Embassy

1. Start from Windhelm with cold-weather food, a rested state, healing, lockpicks, and enough carry space for embassy gear handoff, quest documents, property paperwork, and one Stone of Barenziah. Take the carriage to Solitude first. Go to the Bards College, speak to Giraud Gemane, deliver Adonato's Book. Go straight to Giraud and leave; do not browse the college shelves or join the Bards College yet.
2. Take the carriage from Solitude to Riften, rest there if Fatigue is active, then follow the main road west and north to Ivarstead.
3. In Ivarstead, find Klimmek near the bridge or in town. Ask about the trip up the mountain, accept Klimmek's Supplies, and keep them in your inventory for the climb.
4. Climb the 7,000 Steps. At the top, place Klimmek's Supplies in the offering chest outside High Hrothgar, discover High Hrothgar.
5. Enter High Hrothgar and speak to Arngeir. Complete the full Greybeard training sequence: demonstrate Unrelenting Force, learn Ro, shout at all three targets, follow Borri to the courtyard, learn Whirlwind Sprint: Nah, and use Whirlwind Sprint through the gate. Do not leave High Hrothgar in the middle of this training.
6. Accept `The Horn of Jurgen Windcaller` from Arngeir. Make a rotating manual save before leaving High Hrothgar.
7. Return to Ivarstead. When the cultists confront you after the Greybeard training, defeat them, search one body for `Cultists' Orders`, read it, and keep it. The note points you toward the Windhelm docks; do not sail for Solstheim until this guide sends you there.
8. Speak to Klimmek, complete `Climb the Steps`. Add Klimmek's delivery to Hero of the People tracking.
9. Return to Riften, take the carriage to Morthal, discover Morthal. Rest at Moorside Inn, refresh food, and travel northeast to Ustengrav.
10. Discover Ustengrav. Enter for `The Horn of Jurgen Windcaller`. In Ustengrav Depths, go down to the waterfall area, learn Become Ethereal: Gron from the word wall. Leave `Mystery of Talara, v2` closed.
11. Continue through Ustengrav to the horn pedestal. Take and read Delphine's `Mysterious Note`, loot the final treasure room, and leave Ustengrav.
12. Return to Morthal. At Highmoon Hall, speak to Jarl Idgrod Ravencrone about the burned house and agree to investigate. Visit the burned house beside Highmoon Hall, speak to Helgi's ghost, and agree to find her after dark.
13. After dark, go to Helgi's grave behind the burned house. Kill Laelette, activate Helgi's spirit, then speak to Thonnir when he arrives.
14. After the quest sends you to Alva's House, enter the house, go to the basement coffin, take and read `Alva's Journal`. Return the journal to Jarl Idgrod.
15. When the Jarl sends you to Movarth's Lair, make a rotating manual save before the final approach. At the lair, speak to the gathered townspeople and send Thonnir home if the dialogue allows it. Enter Movarth's Lair, kill Movarth Piquine and the hostile vampires, leave `2920, Sun's Dawn, v2` closed, and do not attack Alva if she is present and non-hostile. Take Movarth's Boots from the bedroom area after Movarth is dead.
16. Return to Jarl Idgrod, complete `Laid to Rest`. Add `Laid to Rest` to Sideways tracking. Before leaving Morthal, enter Thonnir's House, take Michaela's Flagon from beside the fireplace behind the basket, and preserve it with the unique misc collectibles.
17. Ask Jarl Idgrod about becoming Thane of Hjaalmarch. Find Benor in or near the Morthal guardhouse, challenge him to a brawl, fight barehanded with no follower, pet, summon, weapon, poison, spell, or shout, then speak to him after winning. Benor is now a non-default follower and spouse option.
18. At the Morthal wood chopping block, chop one batch of Firewood and sell it to Jorgen.
19. Find Gorm while he is away from the Jarl, often at Moorside Inn around dinner, and accept Gorm's Letter for Captain Aldis. In Highmoon Hall, speak to Idgrod the Younger about Joric, accept Idgrod's Note, and record the active delivery target. Make Idgrod's delivery as an isolated errand: travel to the named recipient, hand over the note, add the delivery to Hero of the People tracking, then return to Morthal before continuing the Hjaalmarch route.
20. Around 1am, wait outside Falion's House and follow Falion to the summoning circle northwest of town. Speak to him at the circle, then report his nighttime activity to Jarl Idgrod. Complete `Falion's Secret`, and add Falion's objective to Hero of the People tracking.
21. Travel east from Morthal to Myrwatch. Discover Myrwatch, kill the chaurus outside, try the locked front door, then loot and read `Hans's Journal` from Hans's body. If you do not know a fire-damage spell, read the Flames spell tome from Hans now. Cast Flames or another fire-damage spell at the eye-shaped seal, follow the spectral hare to the brazier, and enter the tower.
22. Inside Myrwatch, take and read `Anora's Journal` from the main chamber table. Leave the Myrwatch skill-book copies of `Catalogue of Weapon Enchantments` and `Chimarvamidium` closed. Use Myrwatch as an owned rest and crafting stop. Do not put preserved artifacts, masks, bugs in jars, or unique weapons into Myrwatch display furniture yet.
23. Continue east to Stonehills. Discover Stonehills. In Rockwallow Mine, leave `Orsinium and the Orcs` closed on the mine scaffolding, mine Iron Ore, and sell it to Gestur Rockbreaker.
24. In Stonehills, speak to Pactur in Sorli's House about the mine and accept his message for Bryling. Return to Morthal, speak to Jarl Idgrod after Benor, Jorgen, and Gestur are helped, gain the title of Thane of Hjaalmarch. Valdimar is the later Windstad steward default, but do not assign him as steward yet.
25. Speak to Aslfur in Highmoon Hall and buy the Windstad Manor plot for 5,000 gold. If this is your first Hearthfire land purchase because Lakeview Manor was parked earlier, record Land Baron tracking at 1 of 3 Hearthfire plots purchased. If Lakeview Manor was already purchased, record Land Baron tracking at 2 of 3 Hearthfire plots purchased.
26. Travel north to Windstad Manor. Discover the property. Use the drafting table to choose `Small House Layout`; use the site chest materials and quarry stone from the manor grounds to build the foundation at the carpenter's workbench. Leave Windstad wings, furnishings, steward services, bard service, carriage driver, horse, fish hatchery, and household setup for the later Hearthfire construction route.
27. Ride or walk south from Hjaalmarch to Whiterun. Sell ordinary loot at Warmaiden's or Belethor's, rent an inn bed if a level-up is waiting, then go to Whiterun's Hall of the Dead. Give Alessandra's Dagger to Andurs and complete `Pilgrimage`.
28. Follow the road south to Riverwood. At the Sleeping Giant Inn, ask Delphine for the attic room. Follow her to the secret room, receive the Horn of Jurgen Windcaller, and continue the private conversation until `A Blade in the Dark` starts.
29. In Delphine's secret room, inspect and take the Map of Dragon Burials.
30. Before following Delphine to Kynesgrove, return to High Hrothgar with the horn. Give it to Arngeir, stand through the Greybeards' recognition ceremony, learn Unrelenting Force: Dah from Wulfgar.
31. Return down to Ivarstead, follow the road back to Riften, take the carriage to Windhelm, then follow the road south to Kynesgrove. Discover Kynesgrove.
32. Meet Delphine at the dragon burial mound outside Kynesgrove. Let Alduin finish raising Sahloknir before attacking. Kill Sahloknir, absorb the dragon soul, speak to Delphine, complete `A Blade in the Dark`.
33. Go to Braidwood Inn in Kynesgrove. Speak to Iddra about Roggi Knot-Beard, speak to Roggi, and resolve the debt by Persuade, Bribe, or a barehanded brawl. If it becomes a brawl, use no follower, pet, summon, weapon, poison, spell, or shout. Return to Iddra, complete `A Few Words with You`, and add Iddra's favor to Hero of the People tracking. The Braidwood Inn beds are now free after helping Iddra.
34. Return to Riverwood and speak to Delphine in the Sleeping Giant Inn secret room. Start `Diplomatic Immunity`.
35. Travel through Whiterun Stables and take the carriage to Solitude. Before meeting Malborn, go to Castle Dour, deliver Gorm's Letter to Captain Aldis, complete `For the Good of Morthal`. Add Gorm's delivery to Hero of the People tracking. Then find Bryling in the Blue Palace or at her house, deliver Pactur's message, complete `Payment Delivery`. Add Pactur's delivery to Hero of the People tracking.
36. Go to the Winking Skeever and speak to Malborn. Give him the gear you want inside the Thalmor Embassy: one reliable weapon setup, armor or clothing for your build, healing, lockpicks, and any compact tools you need for a hostile interior. Keep only what you are willing to surrender temporarily to Delphine.
37. Meet Delphine at Solitude Stables. Change into the party outfit, receive `Invitation to Elenwen's Reception`. Let Delphine take the rest of your equipment and board the embassy carriage.
38. At the embassy gate, present the invitation, enter the party, discover the Thalmor Embassy.
39. Speak to Malborn at the party. Use Razelan as the distraction: get Cyrodilic Brandy from Malborn, give it to Razelan, ask Razelan to make a scene, then slip through the kitchen with Malborn and retrieve your smuggled gear from the chest.
40. In Elenwen's Solar, take the Interrogation Chamber Key, `Thalmor Dossier: Delphine`, `Thalmor Dossier: Ulfric Stormcloak`, and `Dragon Investigation: Current Status` from the office chest. Read the documents.
41. In the embassy dungeon, make a rotating manual save before advancing the prisoner evidence. Free and speak to Etienne Rarnis, take and read `Thalmor Dossier: Esbern` from the dungeon chest, then kill the two Thalmor soldiers threatening Malborn before they can execute him. Keep Malborn alive for his later Windhelm objective. Take the Trap Door Key.
42. Escape through Reeking Cave. Deal with the frost troll, then take the Stone of Barenziah near the dead conjurer. Leave `Before the Ages of Man` closed.
43. Return to the Sleeping Giant Inn. Retrieve all stored gear from Delphine's chest before leaving the secret room. Report to Delphine, complete `Diplomatic Immunity`.
44. Travel through Whiterun Stables and take the carriage to Riften. Go to the Ragged Flagon through the Thieves Guild route already opened earlier. Speak to Vekel the Man about the old man in the Ratway even if your quest marker already points toward Esbern; this is the clean route for exposing Gissur before you enter the Warrens.
45. If Gissur is present in the Ragged Flagon after Vekel identifies him, kill him before he can complicate the Ratway route, loot `Gissur's Note`. If Shavari attacks during the Riften/Ratway movement, kill her, loot `Shavari's Note`.
46. Go to Esbern's door in the Ratway Warrens. Use Delphine's phrase, "Where were you on the 30th of Frostfall?", or identify yourself as Dragonborn to get him to open the door.
47. Inside Esbern's room, take and read `The Wolf Queen, v6`. Leave `Fire and Darkness` and `Lost Legends` closed.
48. Speak with Esbern until he agrees to leave. Complete `A Cornered Rat`, start `Alduin's Wall`.
49. Escort Esbern back to the Sleeping Giant Inn in Riverwood. Let Delphine and Esbern finish their reunion in the secret room, then let Esbern explain Sky Haven Temple until the active objective is to gain entrance to Sky Haven Temple.
50. In Delphine's secret room after Esbern's arrival, take and read `Annals of the Dragonguard`.
51. Stop here. Do not travel to Karthspire, enter Sky Haven Temple, use the blood seal, complete `Alduin's Wall`, take Dragonbane, take Blades armor, or begin Blades rebuilding work before the level-46 Sky Haven section. Make a rotating manual save.

### Windhelm Follow-Up and Eastmarch Roads

1. Start from Riverwood after Esbern and Delphine have finished the Sky Haven briefing. Recover, sell, and rest before the cold route. Bring hot food if you can make it, normal food, healing, lockpicks, a ranged option, and empty carry space for several unique rewards. Make a rotating manual save before traveling.
2. Take the carriage chain through Whiterun Stables to Windhelm. Arriving near Windhelm starts Malborn's danger window, so handle him before any inn books, road dungeons, ferry business, or Windhelm errands.
3. Enter New Gnisis Cornerclub in the Gray Quarter and speak to Malborn. Promise to deal with the Khajiit outside the city and start `Find the Thalmor Assassin`.
4. Leave Windhelm through the main gate, cross the bridge, and go to the Khajiit camp near Windhelm Stables. If Ri'saad is present, ask him about the strange Khajiit, then confront J'datharr. If Ri'saad's dialogue is unavailable, kill J'datharr directly; killing him still advances the quest on this route.
5. Loot and read `J'datharr's Note` from J'datharr.
6. Before reporting back to Malborn, travel east on the Morrowind road to Refugees' Rest. Discover the monument, clear the hostile wildlife around it.
7. Return to Windhelm, use Candlehearth Hall or another safe interior to pass 24 in-game hours after J'datharr's death, then go back to New Gnisis Cornerclub.
8. Tell Malborn the assassin is dead. Escort or shadow him east from Windhelm until he passes Refugees' Rest and disappears safely. Complete `Find the Thalmor Assassin`, and add Malborn's objective to Hero of the People tracking.
9. Return to Windhelm, unload ordinary loot, refresh food and healing, and check your level. If you are level 32 or higher and the courier delivers `Letter from Tyra Blood-Fire`, read and preserve the letter now. If you are still below level 32 or the courier does not reach you before the East Empire Company work, skip steps 10-11 for now, continue the route, and handle `Heart of Crimson` at the next Windhelm or Eastmarch support stop after the letter arrives; do not pick up any Blades Ebony Shield before this quest is complete.
10. At Windhelm Docks, find the old beggar near the East Empire Company office and give him one gold. Make a rotating manual save, then travel to the Shrine of Talos west-southwest of Windhelm Stables, also known as Watcher of Windhelm. Tyra Blood-Fire is hostile when you approach; defeat her without civilian splash damage, then loot her body for `The Crimson Dirks, v1`, `To (Player)`, her ruby, and the full enchanted Ebony Plate set: Ebony Plate Armor, Ebony Plate Boots, Ebony Plate Gauntlets, and Ebony Plate Helmet. Read and take the two documents, preserve the four enchanted armor pieces, and complete `Heart of Crimson`.
11. After `Heart of Crimson` completes, normal Ebony Plate Armor, Ebony Plate Boots, Ebony Plate Gauntlets, and Ebony Plate Helmet can appear as random loot and can be crafted after the route reaches the Ebony Smithing crafting window; preserve one normal copy of each when the crafting pass creates them. Do not search for the Blindfold because sources mark it unobtainable. Treat Blades Ebony Shield as an opportunistic post-quest random heavy-armor loot item only: if one appears after `Heart of Crimson`, preserve it, but do not route a prequest pickup because the source records a quest-script bug on the shield.
12. Return to Windhelm, unload ordinary loot, refresh food and healing, and dismiss any active follower or pet. Do not bring a follower, pet, or summon into the `Rise in the East` island trip. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-RISE-IN-THE-EAST` at Windhelm before starting the East Empire Company work.
13. Go to the East Empire Company office at Windhelm Docks between 4am and 8pm. Speak to Orthus Endario and accept `Rise in the East`.
14. Go next door to the Clan Shatter-Shield Office during daytime while the door is unlocked. Take and read `Suvaris Atheron's Logbook` from the northwestern alcove beside the business ledger, then leave without taking other Shatter-Shield property.
15. Return to Orthus and give him the logbook. The required `Suvaris Atheron's Logbook` pickup is complete even though the physical book leaves your inventory.
16. Travel to Dawnstar by carriage route. In Windpeak Inn, make a rotating manual save, speak to Stig Salt-Plank, pretend interest in the Blood Horkers until he names Haldyn, then challenge him to a brawl. Fight barehanded only, with no follower, pet, summon, weapon, poison, spell, or shout.
17. After Stig gives up the Japhet's Folly lead and the quest objective updates, kill Stig in Windpeak Inn, loot and read `Blood Horker Orders`, and confirm no bounty was created. If any bounty appears, reload the save from before Stig; if it repeats, return to `HS-RISE-IN-THE-EAST`.
18. Return to the Windhelm East Empire Company office. Speak to Orthus, let the Orthus/Adelaisa scene finish, then speak to Adelaisa. When she sends you to the ship, make sure you still have no active follower or pet.
19. On the ship to Japhet's Folly, speak to Adelaisa, accept the Haldyn assignment, and cross the ice to the Sea Cave. Do not swim away from the island, store items in exterior containers, or try to enter the fort by the front gate.
20. Enter through the Sea Cave and work upward through Japhet's Folly Towers. Watch for the pressure-plate spike-wall trap, clear the Blood Horkers, and keep moving toward Haldyn rather than returning to the Sea Cave entrance.
21. Kill Haldyn, loot the Japhet's Folly Key from him, and take the final chest loot. Do not reenter the Sea Cave after Haldyn dies; the bombardment changes the tower state and can trap the route.
22. Backtrack to the key-locked exterior door and exit onto Japhet's Folly. Discover the island marker if it has not already appeared. During the bombardment, clear the Blood Horkers between the tower and Adelaisa's dock position.
23. Before speaking to Adelaisa, make sure no Blood Horker is stuck near or under the bridge by the docks. If Adelaisa will not advance the quest, search the bridge, dock, and waterline for a remaining pirate and kill it from range if needed; reload `HS-RISE-IN-THE-EAST` if the island state stalls.
24. Speak to Adelaisa at the docks and choose the return dialogue. Do not stay to reenter the tower or leave items on the island.
25. Back in Windhelm, speak to Orthus Endario to complete `Rise in the East`. Add `Rise in the East` to Sideways tracking. Adelaisa Vendicci is now available as a non-default follower and steward option; do not recruit her for the route. Never fast travel or return to Japhet's Folly after completion.
26. Return to Windhelm and go to Candlehearth Hall. Keep 5,000 gold liquid for the Daedric Mail purchase; sell ordinary loot if needed, but do not sell preserved unique rewards or quest documents. Make a rotating manual save before accepting or reading the Creation quest starts.
27. Speak to the innkeeper, Elda Early-Dawn or Nils if he is covering the inn, and ask for work until you receive `Missing Merchant`. Read and take the note, then confirm `Missing Merchant` starts.
28. On the upper floor, read and take `Adonato Leotelli's Journal` to start `Caught in a Web`.
29. On the lower floor, read and take `The Crimson Dirks, v4`. Confirm `Tilted Scales` starts.
30. Leave Windhelm through the main gate and travel northeast on the Morrowind road to Traitor's Post. Discover the shack, make a rotating manual save outside, then clear the exterior and interior bandits while watching for bear traps around the building.
31. Kill Erwan inside Traitor's Post. Loot and read `Erwan's Journal` and `Letter from Edward` from her. Loot Gunther Frost-Foot's body near the ruined fireplace for the Ring of Masser and `The Crimson Dirks, v5`, then read and take the book. Take `Treasure Map VII` from the novice-locked chest inside the shack.
32. Keep the Ring of Masser. Follow the active `Missing Merchant` marker to Ma'dran's caravan, which travels between Windhelm and Solitude, and buy the Daedric Mail set for 5,000 gold instead of trading the ring. Confirm you receive Daedric Mail, Daedric Mail Boots, and Daedric Mail Gauntlets, and that `Missing Merchant` completes. Preserve the ring and the three armor pieces in owned storage; this route does not require equipping the set now.
33. Return to Windhelm or the Eastmarch road support chain, recover warmth and carry space, then travel to Yorgrim Overlook. Discover the ruin.
34. Defeat Ulfnir Bone-Skin and the skeletons. Loot the Overlook Key, loot `Poem for Zaharia` from Fathrys.
35. Use the key to claim the full Studded Dragonscale set from the chest: Studded Dragonscale Armor, Studded Dragonscale Boots, Studded Dragonscale Gauntlets, and Studded Dragonscale Helmet. Preserve the set.
36. Travel to Cronvangr Cave. Discover the cave.
37. In the first large chamber, use the button path into the vampire lair. Kill the Vampire Naturalist, loot `Vampire Naturalist's Journal` and the Spider Cage Key, free Arachnia from the cage. Send Arachnia to wait at a city or owned home after the pet dialogue; do not make the spider part of the active combat party.
38. In the vampire lair, leave `The Locked Room` skill book closed.
39. Continue through Cronvangr to the Web Mother. Kill the Web Mother. Do not give follower commands to the Grenwulf or Holrik ghosts.
40. Loot Grenwulf's corpse for Fists of Randagulf and `Grenwulf's Note`; loot Holrik's corpse for Ice Blade of the Monarch and `Holrik's Note`.
41. After the cave is finished, return to Windhelm or Kynesgrove to sell ordinary loot, eat, and rest before the next Eastmarch road leg.
42. Travel to Riverside Shack west-southwest of Kynesgrove. Discover and clear the shack, loot `Treasure Map III` from the locked chest. Leave `Rislav The Righteous` closed. The Treasure Map III chest near Solitude Lighthouse waits for the Solitude coast route.
43. Travel to Ansilvund. Discover the ruin.
44. Enter Ansilvund and work through the excavation. At the pillar puzzle, set the pillars from left to right to Eagle, Snake, Whale, Snake, then pull the lever.
45. In Ansilvund Burial Chambers, leave `2920, Sun's Dawn, v2` closed on its trapped pedestal.
46. Kill Lu'ah Al-Skaven and free Fjori and Holgeir. Take Ghostblade from the final table, take the Stone of Barenziah from the same final chamber.
47. In the exit room after Lu'ah, read and take `Imperial Condolences`. Take the bowl of Frost Salts from the shelf for Dravynea. If you have not already learned Transmute Mineral Ore from Halted Stream Camp, read the spell tome here; otherwise leave this duplicate tome.
48. Leave Ansilvund.
49. Go to Kynesgrove. In Steamscorch Mine, mine one Malachite Ore, sell it to Kjeld.
50. Find Dravynea the Stoneweaver in Kynesgrove or Braidwood Inn. Ask about her work, give her the Frost Salts from Ansilvund, complete `Dravynea's Frost Salts`. Add Dravynea's favor to Hero of the People tracking.
51. Travel to Darkwater Crossing. If Derkeethus is already safe from the earlier Darkwater leg, skip to the mining step. If he is still missing, enter Darkwater Pass, free Derkeethus, escort him out, complete `Extracting an Argonian`, and exit with Darkwater Pass finished.
52. In Goldenrock Mine, mine one Corundum Ore, sell it to Verner Rock-Chucker.
53. Ask Sondas Drenim whether he has been working in the mines long, accept `Sondas's Note`, and record the active delivery target. Complete the delivery as an isolated errand: travel to the named recipient, hand over the note, add Sondas's delivery to Hero of the People tracking, then return to the Windhelm road before continuing the Eastmarch route.
54. Travel to Mara's Eye Pond southwest of Windhelm. Discover the pond, clear the mudcrabs, enter the trapdoor on the central island, and clear Mara's Eye Den. Leave `Guide to Better Thieving` closed in the den.
55. Go to Gallows Hall on the north shore of Mara's Eye Pond. Enter the house, make a rotating manual save, and keep free looting until after `Dreams of the Dead` is complete; inside the quest, take only the named clue, puzzle, and reward items.
56. Read `Naara's Journal` beside Naara, take the three copies of `Naara's Plea`.
57. Read the Arch Necromancer's First Clue from the map table.
58. Activate the torches in this order: Morthal, Falkreath, Nightgate Inn, Fort Dunstad. When the mist clears, take the Staff of Worms.
59. Use the Staff of Worms on the skeleton mage corpse in the eastern coffin, then search the reanimated skeleton mage for the Arch Necromancer's Key.
60. Unlock the safe near the arcane enchanter. Take the Bloodworm Helm and read the Arch Necromancer's Second Clue.
61. Pray at the small Vaermina statue on the west wall, then sleep in the bedroll beside the Altar of the Revenant.
62. In the dream, collect exactly the three Dreamer's Greater Soul Gems the clue points to: one in the coffin in front of the Vaermina statue, one in the skeleton hand in the open coffin beside it, and one in the closed coffin on the lowest shelf on the other side of the main entrance.
63. When you wake, read the Arch Necromancer's Final Instructions from the altar.
64. Take the fourth Dreamer's Greater Soul Gem near Naara's body, place all four Dreamer's Greater Soul Gems in the altar holders, and activate the Altar of the Revenant. Complete `Dreams of the Dead`, unlock Gallows Hall ownership.
65. After the quest ends, collect the Helm of Oreyn Bearclaw from the middle floor table, read and take `Helm of Oreyn Bearclaw` from the same table, and read and take `Notes on the Bone Forge` near the Bone Forge.
66. Inspect the Bone Forge. Do not create a skeleton follower now; Bone Forge followers are permanent and cannot be dismissed. Leave `A Tragedy in Black` closed.
67. Use Gallows Hall as a safe Eastmarch rest and storage stop only after ownership is confirmed. Store preserved artifacts and heavy unique gear there if carry weight is tight, then make a rotating manual save.
68. Use Gallows Hall, Windhelm, and Kynesgrove as support for the rest of the Eastmarch road. Discover Frostroot Cave, Gloombound Mine, Mixwater Mill, Narzulbur, The Atronach Stone, Windhelm Military Camp, Winterhold Stormcloak Camp, and Sivdur's Respite. Clear Abandoned Prison, Bonestrewn Crest, Broken Limb Camp, Cradlecrush Rock, Cragwallow Slope, Lost Knife Hideout, and Steamcrag Camp. Across the White River from Abandoned Prison, loot Lucky Lorenz's crushed house and take `Treasure Map IX`. Leave `Song Of Hrormir` in Abandoned Prison, `Rislav The Righteous` west of Abandoned Prison, and `The Lunar Lorkhan` in Cragwallow Slope closed for the Scholar's Insight reading window.
69. Clear Hillgrund's Tomb as the `Ancestral Worship` route. Speak to Golldir outside, follow him through the barrow, use the bear-symbol chain to open the secret route, kill Vals Veran, keep Golldir alive, loot Hillgrund's Tomb Crypt Key, and speak to Golldir after leaving so he becomes available as a follower. Leave `The Black Arts On Trial` closed at the chest site south-southeast of Hillgrund's Tomb.
70. Clear Lost Knife Hideout as one dungeon through Lost Knife Cave and the hideout interior. The `Orsinium and the Orcs` and `Words and Philosophy` copies there are duplicates if you followed the Apocrypha reads earlier.
71. At Mistwatch, enter through the normal lower route, speak to Christer, proceed upward with his key, speak to Fjola, take the peaceful lie outcome, keep Fjola's Wedding Band, return to Christer, then report back to Fjola so Mistwatch completes.
72. Start and complete `A Night to Remember` before clearing Morvunskar if it is not already active. Check these inns until Sam Guevenne appears: Bannered Mare, Candlehearth Hall, Bee and Barb, Silver-Blood Inn, Winking Skeever, Moorside Inn, Windpeak Inn, Frozen Hearth, Dead Man's Drink, Vilemyr Inn, Frostfruit Inn, Four Shields Tavern, Old Hroldan Inn, and Nightgate Inn. Win the drinking contest, then wake in the Temple of Dibella.
73. In Markarth, clean up the temple or use the routed Persuade/Bribe option, then ask Senna about Rorikstead. In Rorikstead, speak to Ennis, recover Gleda the Goat from Grok's camp south of town, and return the goat to Ennis. In Whiterun, speak to Ysolda about the wedding ring, go to Witchmist Grove, kill Moira, take the Wedding Ring, return it to Ysolda, then travel to Morvunskar. Clear Morvunskar, enter the blue portal to Misty Grove, speak to Sam/Sanguine, receive Sanguine Rose, and record Oblivion Walker progress at 8 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
74. Return to Windhelm, Gallows Hall, or another support point, unload preserved Eastmarch rewards and documents, rest if a level-up is waiting, and make a rotating manual save before the Solitude route.
## Solitude, Bards, Shield Gate, and Dark Brotherhood

### Solitude Coast, Dragon Bridge, Wild Horse, and Wolfskull

1. Start from Gallows Hall, Windhelm, Whiterun, or any other warm support stop with easy carriage access. Travel to Solitude, arrive at Katla's Farm and Solitude Stables, refresh food and cold-road supplies, sell ordinary duplicate loot if needed, and make a rotating manual save.
2. At Katla's Farm, harvest a few leeks, potatoes, or wheat from the farm plots and sell one accepted crop turn-in to Katla. This may make the stable horses look free to ride, but keep using only owned route mounts.
3. At Solitude Stables, speak to Geimund, or Horm if Geimund is unavailable, and buy the wild horse map package for 250 gold. Read `Wild Horse Notes` and inspect the Map of Wild Horses.
4. In Solitude, speak to Ahtar and accept his Captain Hargar bandit-leader job. Speak to Noster Eagle-Eye and accept his helmet retrieval; complete the assigned retrieval as a direct out-and-back and return the helmet to Noster before continuing Solitude work unless the marker is Broken Oar Grotto, which this section routes below.
5. Speak to Jaree-Ra near the Solitude docks and start `Lights Out!`.
6. Travel southwest to Solitude Sawmill. Discover the sawmill.
7. At the sawmill, chop one batch of Firewood at the wood chopping block and sell it to Hjorunn.
8. Make a rotating manual save, cross the river into the forest south of Solitude Sawmill, and find the Dapple Brown Wild Horse before the dragon mound. Mount it and stay on until it is tamed. If the horse throws you into lethal terrain or a dragon fight takes over the area, reload the save and approach again after the area is calm.
9. Ride or walk to the Solitude Lighthouse exterior. Discover the lighthouse. Loot the Treasure Map III chest northeast of the lighthouse under the cliff edge, then extinguish the lighthouse beacon for Jaree-Ra. Treasure Maps tracking is now 4 of 13 resolved map-and-cache entries.
10. Return to Jaree-Ra at the Solitude docks, then travel to the Wreck of the Icerunner. Follow the ship route, take `Note From Jaree-Ra` from Deeja, then continue to Broken Oar Grotto.
11. In Broken Oar Grotto, kill Captain Hargar for Ahtar before finishing the cave. Read `Hargar's Journal`, take Hargar's Chest Key, collect enough loose bear pelts from the crates, rowboat, and cart to bring Temba's pelt total to exactly ten, clear the grotto, and kill Jaree-Ra to complete `Lights Out!`. Keep those ten pelts together in inventory or owned storage until the next Ivarstead contract. If Noster's Helmet is assigned to Broken Oar Grotto, retrieve it before leaving.
12. Return to Solitude. Report Captain Hargar's death to Ahtar, and return Noster's Helmet if this section recovered it.
13. Travel west-northwest to The Steed Stone. Discover the standing stone. Keep your current standing-stone power active.
14. Continue to Dragon Bridge. Discover the town.
15. At the Lylvieve crops, harvest cabbage, gourds, potatoes, or wheat and sell one accepted crop turn-in to Azzada Lylvieve.
16. At Dragon Bridge lumber camp, chop one batch of Firewood and sell it to Horgeir.
17. Speak to Olda at Horgeir's House and accept her request for Dragon's Breath Mead. Follow the road downhill, cross the river, take the rough trail west to the overhanging rock, take Dragon's Breath Mead from the crate, and return it to Olda. Add Olda's favor to Hero of the People tracking.
18. Go to Four Shields Tavern. Read and take `Suicide at Dragon Bridge`. Search downstream northeast of the bridge on the south bank for the dead guard, loot and read `Guard's Note on Aldepius`.
19. Return to Four Shields Tavern, read and take `Aldepius' Confession`. Go to Dragon Bridge, defeat Peladius, loot the enchanted Steel Soldier armor pieces from his body. Preserve the armor set; the crafting recipe is now unlocked for later equipment reconciliation.
20. Continue to Wolfskull Cave before speaking to Falk Firebeard about it. Enter the cave in its bandit state, kill the bandit boss in the ice-cave section that gives Wolfskull its cleared state, then exit.
21. Return to Solitude and make a rotating manual save outside the Blue Palace. Enter during court hours, let Varnius Junius's audience with Elisif play out, then speak to Falk Firebeard and accept `The Man Who Cried Wolf`.
22. Return to Wolfskull Cave. Follow the quest route through the changed cave and into Wolfskull Ruins, reach the ritual tower, and learn `Necromancer's Ritual` from the spell tome pedestal.
23. Leave `The Refugees` closed on the ritual altar. Kill the Ritual Master, stop Potema's binding ritual, use the exit shortcut, and return to Falk Firebeard.
24. Report to Falk, complete `The Man Who Cried Wolf`. Add it to Sideways tracking. When a courier later delivers `Letter from Falk Firebeard`, keep the letter for the level-40 Shield of Solitude section; do not take Falk's final Wolf Queen reward before the guide tells you the shield gate is open.
25. Speak to Elisif in the Blue Palace and accept `Elisif's Tribute`. Take Torygg's War Horn.
26. Travel to the Shrine of Talos north of The Ritual Stone, near the river path by Shimmermist Cave. Make a rotating manual save before the final approach to the shrine.
27. Place Torygg's War Horn at the shrine. Defeat Agent Lorcalin and the Thalmor soldier when they appear, loot `Lorcalin's Orders` from Agent Lorcalin, read it.
28. Return to Elisif, complete `Elisif's Tribute`. Add Elisif's tribute to Hero of the People tracking. Proudspire Manor purchase permission is now unlocked, but do not buy Proudspire until the later property-economy section funds the full Solitude home route.
29. Return to a warm support stop, store preserved uniques only in approved owned storage, sleep in a bed if a level-up is waiting, and make a rotating manual save.
### Bards College, Lost Library, and Instrument Roads

1. Start in Solitude with the city calm, no Dark Brotherhood Solitude wedding state active, no Civil War treaty state active, warm travel supplies refreshed, and a rotating manual save made near the Winking Skeever or Bards College.
2. In the Winking Skeever, speak to Lisette and ask about the Bards College. Go to the Bards College, speak to Viarmo, finish the introductory `Investigate the Bards College` objective, and accept `Tending the Flames`.
3. Make a rotating manual save after `Tending the Flames` is active. Travel to Dead Men's Respite with food, hot soup or Fire Salts if available, and enough carry space for a claw, verse, and dungeon loot.
4. Enter Dead Men's Respite, follow Svaknir's ghost, and take the Ruby Dragon Claw from the first chamber.
5. Continue through the dungeon to Svaknir's body and take `King Olaf's Verse`.
6. At the claw door, use the Ruby Dragon Claw combination Wolf - Hawk - Wolf. In the final chamber, defeat King Olaf One-Eye, take King Olaf's Treasury Key, learn the `Whirlwind Sprint` word `Wuld` from the word wall.
7. Loot the treasury, use the shortcut exit, and leave Dead Men's Respite cleared.
8. Return to Viarmo at the Bards College and make a rotating manual save before handing over the verse. Give him `King Olaf's Verse`, then fill the two missing lines with the strongest available answers: use the Persuade line about Olaf being Numinex if it is available, then use the strongest available verse line for the sacking of Winterhold.
9. Follow Viarmo to the Blue Palace during court hours. Let him perform for Elisif, speak to him afterward, and return to the Bards College.
10. Speak to Jorn and tell him to prepare the festival. After dusk, make a rotating manual save in Solitude, attend the Burning of King Olaf, speak to Viarmo after the effigy burns, complete `Tending the Flames`. Add `Tending the Flames` to Sideways tracking.
11. With Bards College membership complete, collect these three routed books inside the college now: read and take `Invocation of Azura`, `N'Gasta! Kvata! Kvakis!`, and `The Wild Elves`. Leave skill books and quest-starting books in the college closed unless this guide names them.
12. Speak to Inge Six Fingers and accept `Finn's Lute`; speak to Pantea Ateia and accept `Pantea's Flute`; speak to Giraud Gemane and accept `Rjorn's Drum`. Confirm all three instrument objectives are active, then make a rotating manual save before leaving Solitude.
13. Take a carriage or ride to Windhelm, use Candlehearth Hall or Gallows Hall as the warm support stop, then travel through the Kynesgrove road network to Stony Creek Cave. Make a rotating manual save outside the cave, then enter and clear it.
14. In the boss chamber, kill the bandit leader and loot Finn's Lute, Treasure Map X, and Ruin's Edge. Take the Stone of Barenziah from the table near the alchemy lab. Leave `Mystery of Talara, Part 4` closed.
15. Continue through the rest of Stony Creek Cave, take the Bliss Bug in a Jar, exit after the cave is cleared.
16. West of Stony Creek Cave, find the White Spotted Wild Horse. Make a rotating manual save, mount it, and stay on until it is tamed.
17. Return to the Bards College, give Finn's Lute to Inge Six Fingers, verify the lute leaves your inventory, complete `Finn's Lute`. The reward raises Sneak, Lockpicking, Pickpocket, Light Armor, Speech, and Alchemy by one point each.
18. Travel to Dawnstar before the Winterhold road. Discover Dawnstar. At Iron-Breaker Mine, mine one iron ore with your carried pickaxe and sell it to Beitild before any Brotherhood contract involving her exists. Use Windpeak Inn if you need warmth, food, or a bed, then travel to Winterhold before Hob's Fall Cave. Discover Winterhold.
19. In The Frozen Hearth, speak to Haran about Ranmir, then speak to Ranmir and resolve his debt with Persuade, Bribe, or Intimidate. Return to Haran, complete her favor, add it to Hero of the People tracking.
20. Give Ranmir one accepted alcoholic drink before you start his Isabelle follow-up.
21. Speak to Dagur about work around the inn. Ask Ranmir and Haran about Isabelle until the lead points toward Hob's Fall Cave. This objective may not show in the journal, so treat Isabelle's Letter as the thing you must recover and keep safe.
22. Leave Korir's Helm of Winterhold request unstarted for now. The guide will handle it as an isolated random assignment instead of building this Winterhold road around one possible cave.
23. Prepare for the cold road and enter Hob's Fall Cave. Near the entrance, search Isabelle Rolaine's body and take `Isabelle's Letter`. Keep the letter in your inventory until it is handed to Ranmir.
24. Make a rotating manual save inside the first safe pocket, then clear through the cave.
25. In the lower cave, take the Stone of Barenziah near the alchemy lab under the bridge. Leave `The Exodus` closed.
26. In the library area, open the Ancient Tome Chest, complete `The Lost Library`, and read the Arcane Accessories spell tomes from the chest. Leave `Enchanter's Primer` and `Boethiah's Proving` closed.
27. Defeat the final enemies, loot Pantea's Flute from the boss chest, and exit with Hob's Fall Cave cleared.
28. Return to Winterhold. Give `Isabelle's Letter` to Ranmir, complete `Drowned Sorrows`, add it to Hero of the People tracking.
29. Return to Winterhold.
