30. Return to the Bards College, give Pantea's Flute to Pantea Ateia, verify the flute leaves your inventory, complete `Pantea's Flute`. The reward raises Alteration, Conjuration, Destruction, Enchanting, Illusion, and Restoration by one point each.
31. Travel to Falkreath before Halldir's Cairn. Leave Runil's missing-journal request unstarted for now; the guide will handle it later as an isolated random assignment.
32. Travel to Halldir's Cairn and make a rotating manual save outside before entering. Enter the cairn, follow the route through the ghosts and key doors, and learn `Raise Zombie` from the spell tome in the cairn.
33. Before the final chamber, make another rotating manual save and do not overwrite it until the drum is accepted by Giraud. Defeat Halldir.
34. Loot Halldir's Staff and Rjorn's Drum. Exit with Halldir's Cairn cleared.
35. Travel to The Lady Stone area and loot the Treasure Map X cache. Treasure Maps tracking is now 5 of 13 resolved map-and-cache entries.
36. Return to the Bards College, give Rjorn's Drum to Giraud Gemane, verify the drum leaves your inventory, complete `Rjorn's Drum`. The reward raises Archery, Block, Heavy Armor, One-handed, Smithing, and Two-handed by one point each.
38. Use Solitude services to sell ordinary loot, refresh cold-road food, sleep in an inn bed if a level-up is waiting, store preserved artifacts only in approved owned storage, and make a rotating manual save.

### Potema, Shield of Solitude, and Bone Wolf

1. Start in Solitude at level 40 or higher, with `Letter from Falk Firebeard` available from the courier follow-up to `The Man Who Cried Wolf`. If a level-up is waiting, sleep in an inn bed and take the level before speaking to Falk. Do not begin Falk's Wolf Queen conversation below level 40.
2. Read `Letter from Falk Firebeard`, make `HARD SAVE: HS-SOLITUDE-SHIELD-LEVEL40`, and keep that save until the Shield of Solitude reward and Bone Wolf courier follow-up are both stable.
3. Speak to Falk Firebeard in the Blue Palace about Potema and start `The Wolf Queen Awakened`. Go to the Hall of the Dead and speak to Styrr. Let his dialogue finish fully, receive the key for Potema's Catacombs, receive `Spell Tome: Turn Undead`, read the tome immediately.
4. Enter the Temple of the Divines route into Potema's Catacombs and make a rotating manual save inside. Work through the catacombs and refuge. In the early room with the purple liquid and table, leave `Surfeit of Thieves` closed.
5. Continue to Potema's Sanctum. Take Potema's Sanctum Key when the route presents it, then make a rotating manual save before the final chamber and keep it until Potema's remains are safely turned in.
6. Defeat Potema's summoned waves and her final spirit.
7. Take Potema's Skull. Loot the boss chest and take `Legend of Krately House` as the selected copy for that skill-book title, but keep it unopened for the Scholar's Insight reading window. Do not count the skill-book read yet.
8. Return to Styrr, give him Potema's Skull, let the quest update to the Falk report step, and add Styrr's help to Haafingar favor tracking.
9. Return to Falk Firebeard. Confirm level 40 or higher one last time, then report Potema's defeat and receive the Shield of Solitude. Verify the max-tier shield before moving on: armor rating 32, Magic Resistance 30%, and Blocking 35%. Add `The Wolf Queen Awakened` to Sideways tracking.
10. Before leaving the Blue Palace, go to the corner table outside Sybille Stentor's quarters and read `Lost Legends`. This starts `Forbidden Legend` after the level-36 Gauldur weapon gate is safe.
11. Preserve the Shield of Solitude. Do not disenchant it; the route learns Resist Magic from a disposable source later.
12. Leave the Blue Palace and exit Solitude through the main gate. When the courier delivers `Letter from Bolgeir Bearclaw`, read it and start `Let Sleeping Wolves Lie`.
13. Travel from Solitude toward Folgunthur, across the bay from the Solitude docks. At Daynas Valen's camp outside the ruin, read `Daynas Valen's Journal` now that `Lost Legends` has already started the quest. Discover Folgunthur.
14. Enter Folgunthur and make a rotating manual save inside. Work through the first rooms and leave `Fire and Darkness` closed. On Daynas Valen's body, take the Ivory Dragon Claw and `Daynas Valen's Notes`; read the notes.
15. Use the Ivory Dragon Claw to lower the bridge and continue. For the four-gate lever puzzle, pull the first left-hand lever and the second right-hand lever. For the pillar room, match the symbols Snake - Whale - Eagle, then use the revealed chain. At the claw door, use Hawk - Hawk - Dragon.
16. In Folgunthur Crypt, defeat Mikrul Gauldurson. Loot the Gauldur Blackblade, the Gauldur Amulet Fragment, and `Writ of Sealing (Mikrul)` from him. Preserve the Gauldur Blackblade and do not disenchant it; Absorb Health is learned later from a disposable weapon.
17. Use the Ivory Dragon Claw to open the path to the word wall, learn `Frost Breath: Krah`, loot the boss chest, and exit with Folgunthur cleared. `Forbidden Legend` remains active for the Saarthal, Geirmund's Hall, and Reachwater Rock stages later.
18. Travel to the necromancer camp northwest of Folgunthur and make a rotating manual save before approaching the camp.
19. At the camp, defeat the necromancer and the two wolf thralls. Loot `Necromancer's Journal`, Key to Wolf Cage, and Totem Bone from the necromancer, read the journal.
20. Unlock the cage, give the Totem Bone to the Bone Wolf, complete `Let Sleeping Wolves Lie`.
21. Keep Bone Wolf following until the next owned home or city stop, or send it to an owned home now if that dialogue option is available. It does not replace the current horse or humanoid follower. Return to Solitude or another warm support stop, sell ordinary loot, store the Shield of Solitude, Gauldur Blackblade, Ivory Dragon Claw, and other preserved items in approved owned storage if needed, sleep if a level-up is waiting, and make a rotating manual save.

### Haafingar Caves, Volskygge, and Night Hunter

1. Start in Solitude after the Bone Wolf route. Sell ordinary loot, sleep in an inn bed if a level-up is waiting, stock hot food for the cold western loop, and make a rotating manual save.
2. Go to the Blue Palace and speak to Sybille Stentor. Ask what problem she needs handled and accept `Kill the Vampire`.
3. Travel through Dragon Bridge to Pinemoon Cave, using Four Shields Tavern if you need warmth or rest on the way. Enter Pinemoon, kill the Master Vampire in the altar chamber, clear the remaining vampires and wolves, and leave `Incident at Necrom` closed in the sleeping quarters. Exit with Pinemoon Cave cleared.
4. Stop at Clearpine Pond. Clear the spriggans on the island, leave `De Rerum Dirennis` on the dead alchemist.
5. Clear Shadowgreen Cavern, Lost Echo Cave, Chillwind Depths, Harmugstahl, Dragontooth Crater, Orphan's Tear, Pinefrost Tower, Steepfall Burrow, and Lower Steepfall Burrow while this Haafingar road is open. Discover Haafingar Stormcloak Camp, Reach Stormcloak Camp, Cliffside Retreat, Castle Volkihar Balcony, Blackbone Isle, and the Statue to Meridia. Leave `The Marksmanship Lesson` at Cliffside Retreat closed for the Scholar's Insight reading window.
6. Go to Rimerock Burrow for Clavicus Vile. Kill Sebastian Lort, take the Rueful Axe, then return to Haemar's Shame and make `HARD SAVE: HS-DAEDRIC-CLAVICUS` before the final choice. Play the Rueful Axe outcome from the save for branch experience, reload, spare Barbas, return the axe, and take the Masque of Clavicus Vile as the main-route artifact. Record Oblivion Walker progress at 9 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
7. Finish Meridia while you are at her statue. Make a rotating manual save before committing to the Beacon path. If Meridia's Beacon is already in inventory, place it at the statue. If it is not, activate the statue, follow the assigned Beacon marker as an isolated out-and-back, return to the statue, then place the Beacon. If the marker points into a route-locked space, leave `The Break of Dawn` active, continue the route, and return from the first later support stop after that space is unlocked; do not force a random reroll or enter a locked quest space early.
8. Before Meridia lifts you, reduce carry weight below the limit and dismiss or park followers where you can recover them cleanly. Enter Kilkreath through the unlocked shrine door, guide Meridia's light through Kilkreath Temple, the balcony, Kilkreath Ruins, and Kilkreath Catacombs by activating each light pedestal, and clear the corrupted shades.
9. In the final Catacombs pedestal room, make a rotating manual save before opening the Malkoran chamber. Loot anything you want from this side of the final doors now; after you enter Malkoran's chamber, the doors close and are not part of the normal return route.
10. Enter the final chamber, defeat Malkoran and then Malkoran's Shade. Do not use paralysis, freeze-locking, or force effects for the killing blow if they cause the shade to fail or stop fighting; reload the pre-chamber save if the shade cannot be killed.
11. Loot the final chamber before taking Dawnbreaker because the pickup transports you back to Meridia. Do not use area-effect spells to knock Dawnbreaker from its pedestal for a duplicate. Take Dawnbreaker, complete `The Break of Dawn`, preserve Dawnbreaker, and record Oblivion Walker progress at 10 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts. If Meridia's sky sequence hangs, drops you to your death, or is interrupted by a dragon, reload the pre-Dawnbreaker save, reduce carry weight further, and clear any nearby dragon before trying the pickup again.
12. Learn the Elemental Fury word outside Kilkreath if it has not already been learned.
13. Continue west to Volskygge and enter through the main ruin door. Clear the exterior bandits, then work through Volskygge from the front entrance rather than trying to shortcut from the peak.
14. At the four-animal puzzle, activate the handles in this order: Snake, Bear, Fox, Wolf. Continue into Volskygge Passages. Leave `Surfeit of Thieves` closed.
15. At the summit, learn `Whirlwind Sprint: Kest`, defeat Volsung, loot and preserve the Volsung mask, and check the final treasure area for `Kahvozein's Fang`. If `Kahvozein's Fang` is present, take it, preserve it for `Alteration Ritual Spell`. Exit with Volskygge cleared.
16. Travel back toward the north coast and visit Widow's Watch Ruins. Defeat the hag, climb the ruined tower, leave `2920, Rain's Hand, v4` closed near the upper chest.
17. Travel to Ironback Hideout from the road near the Thalmor Embassy approach. Clear the bandits and bandit chief at the ruined house, read `Kragrash's Letter` on the mantel to start `Night Hunter`, and leave `Beggar` closed on the table.
18. Follow Kragrash's trail to the burnt corpse on the cliff south of the Thalmor Embassy entrance. Loot Ironback Hideout Cellar Key from the corpse, then return to Ironback Hideout.
19. Enter Ironback Hideout Cellar through the trapdoor under the tent. Complete `Night Hunter`, take the Ebony Crossbow, Elven Crossbow, and useful bolts from the vault. Leave the enhanced crossbow variants for the later smithing/crossbow crafting pass.
20. Travel to Ravenscar Hollow. Clear the hagravens, loot the boss chest, leave `The Art of War Magic` closed on the nearby table.
21. Return to Solitude. Report to Sybille Stentor, complete `Kill the Vampire`. Add Sybille's favor to Haafingar help tracking and Hero of the People tracking. Use Solitude services, store preserved gear only in approved owned storage if needed, and make a rotating manual save.
### Cicero, Aventus, and the Sanctuary Door

1. Start from a warm support stop after the Haafingar cave loop. Travel to Whiterun Stables, then take the road north to Loreius Farm and discover the farm.
2. Speak to Cicero by the broken wagon on the road near Loreius Farm. Go to Vantus Loreius at the farm, persuade him to help repair Cicero's wheel, then return to Cicero and complete `Delayed Burial`. Add `Delayed Burial` to Sideways tracking.
3. Travel to Windhelm, make a rotating manual save before entering Aretino Residence, then enter the house and speak to Aventus Aretino to start `Innocence Lost`. In the ritual room, leave `A Hypothetical Treachery` closed behind the shelf.
4. Travel to Riften and enter Honorhall Orphanage. Kill Grelod the Kind, then leave the orphanage after Constance reacts.
5. Return to Aventus Aretino in Windhelm, make a rotating manual save before speaking to him, then report Grelod's death. Complete `Innocence Lost`, take the Aretino Family Heirloom reward.
6. Leave Windhelm and wait for the courier to deliver `Mysterious Note`. Read the note and preserve it. Do not sleep for the kidnapping scene until this note has been delivered.
7. Sleep in an inn or owned bed for any duration. When you wake in the Abandoned Shack, do not speak to Astrid and do not attack anyone yet. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DB-ABANDONED-SHACK`.
8. On the branch save, kill Astrid, loot the Abandoned Shack key, leave the shack, and speak to any city guard about what happened. Travel to the Penitus Oculatus Outpost in Dragon Bridge, speak to Commander Maro, then go to the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary and use the passphrase he gives you. Kill the Dark Brotherhood members inside, return to Commander Maro, complete `Destroy the Dark Brotherhood!`. Reload `HS-DB-ABANDONED-SHACK`; all branch loot and branch-only kills are discarded.
9. On the main save, speak to Astrid, kill Vasha, loot one Execution Hood from the captive, then speak to Astrid again and receive the shack key.
10. Leave the Abandoned Shack and travel to Meeko's Shack on the Hjaalmarch road between Morthal and Solitude. Discover the shack. If Meeko is outside or on the nearby road, speak to him once and record him as available; leave him at the shack unless you deliberately want him following. Inside the shack, leave `A Dance in Fire, v6` closed on the barrel.
11. Travel to the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary west of Falkreath, using Dead Man's Drink or Lakeview Manor as support if needed. Discover the Sanctuary.
12. At the Black Door, answer `Silence, my brother`, enter the Sanctuary, and speak to Astrid. Complete `With Friends Like These...`, receive the Shrouded Armor reward set.
13. Before going deeper, search the shelves near the map table for the shrouded clothing set. Take Shrouded Robes, Shrouded Hood, Shrouded Hand Wraps, Shrouded Shoes, and the maskless Shrouded Cowl. Leave `Sithis` and `Lost Legends` closed.
14. Go into Astrid and Arnbjorn's bedroom and take the Stone of Barenziah from the dresser.
15. Go to the Sanctuary word wall and learn `Marked for Death: Lun`.
16. Let the Sanctuary introduction play out, then speak to Nazir. Accept the first three contracts: `Kill Narfi`, `Kill Beitild`, and `Kill Ennodius Papius`. Complete `Sanctuary`.
17. Do not use the Sanctuary as long-term artifact storage. Carry preserved gear to the next owned-home storage stop, or store only expendable supplies here. Make a rotating manual save before leaving the Sanctuary.

### First Brotherhood Contracts and Muiri's Revenge

1. Start from the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary with `Kill Narfi`, `Kill Beitild`, and `Kill Ennodius Papius` active from Nazir. Travel to Ivarstead. Before killing Narfi, bring exactly ten bear pelts, cave bear pelts, or snow bear pelts to Temba Wide-Arm and complete `Grin and Bear It`; leave any extra pelts in storage before the turn-in. Then make a rotating manual save near Narfi's ruined house and kill Narfi without drawing a bounty. You already completed `The Straw that Broke`; after this contract, Narfi is no longer a possible Goldenhills farmhand.
2. Travel to Anga's Mill. Discover the mill.
3. Find Aeri at the mill. Chop one batch of Firewood at one of the mill chopping blocks and sell it to her. Ask her about the Jarl's order and accept Aeri's Note for Skald. Aeri is now available as a possible spouse; keep Ysolda as the guide default unless you intentionally choose otherwise.
4. Go west from Anga's Mill to Ennodius Papius's camp. Make a rotating manual save before the kill. Kill Ennodius while he is awake or standing outside the tent.
5. Travel to Dawnstar and go to The White Hall. Give Aeri's Note to Skald, complete the delivery. Add Aeri's delivery to Hero of the People tracking.
6. Find Beitild in Dawnstar after the earlier Iron-Breaker Mine ore sale has already been handled. Make a rotating manual save, kill her quietly, and leave Dawnstar.
7. Return to the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary. Let the Cicero arrival scene finish, then speak to Astrid when she approaches and accept the Markarth contract. After Astrid gives you `Mourning Never Comes`, speak to Nazir and collect the rewards for Narfi, Ennodius Papius, and Beitild.
8. Travel to Markarth and find Muiri at The Hag's Cure during the day or the Silver-Blood Inn in the evening. Speak to her about Alain Dufont, accept the optional Nilsine Shatter-Shield objective, and ask for the full story so she gives you the two Lotus Extract poisons.
9. Travel to Windhelm. Blood on the Ice is already complete on this route; if it is not complete on your save, stop and return to the Windhelm murder investigation section before killing Nilsine.
10. Make a rotating manual save in Windhelm. Find Nilsine Shatter-Shield when she is alone, kill her quietly, and take the Clan Shatter-Shield House Key from her body.
11. Enter the House of Clan Shatter-Shield with the key. Go upstairs to Nilsine's bedroom, take the Stone of Barenziah from the shelf.
12. Travel to Nightgate Inn or another warm support stop, then go to Raldbthar. Discover Raldbthar, make a rotating manual save outside, clear the exterior bandits, and enter the ruin.
13. Work through the first zone to Alain Dufont's camp chamber. Kill Alain and the bandits with him, loot Aegisbane from Alain, and preserve it as a unique weapon. Leave `2920, Last Seed, v8` closed near Alain's chamber.
14. Continue deeper into Raldbthar and take the lift down to Raldbthar Deep Market. In the large gear chamber, remove the four obstructions from the jammed gears, press the bridge button, defeat the centurion, and continue into the final room.
15. Take the Raldbthar Aetherium Shard from the small side room. Do not activate the Blackreach mechanism unless the guide has already given you Septimus's Attunement Sphere.
16. Exit through the lift back to the surface. Discover the Great Lift at Raldbthar.
17. Return to Markarth and report both deaths to Muiri. Complete `Mourning Never Comes`, receive Muiri's Ring, preserve it.
18. Return to the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary and report to Astrid. Let `Whispers in the Dark` start, then make a rotating manual save. Stop here; the next instructions will handle `The Dainty Sload` for Erikur before any wedding-assassination step in `Bound Until Death`.

### Whispers, Solitude Jobs, Nazir Contracts, and the Wedding Assassination

1. Start in the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary with `Whispers in the Dark` active. Speak to Astrid, go to the Night Mother's chamber, pick the coffin lock, and enter the coffin. Listen through the full scene, then step out, speak to Cicero, and speak to Astrid.
2. Speak to Nazir and accept both new contracts: `Kill Hern` and `Kill Lurbuk`. This completes `Whispers in the Dark`.
3. Travel to Riften and enter the Ragged Flagon through the Thieves Guild route. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-TG-SOLITUDE-JOBS`.
4. Complete exactly five Solitude Thieves Guild jobs before continuing the wedding contract. For each job below, make a rotating manual save before asking for the job, ask the listed giver for the listed job type, and accept the job only if the assigned city is Solitude. If the assigned city is not Solitude, reload the pre-request save and ask again. Finish each accepted job using its quest marker, return to the giver, and turn it in before requesting the next one.

| Order | Giver | Job Type | Solitude Task |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Delvin | Numbers Job | Change the marked ledger in the Solitude business. |
| 2 | Vex | Burglary Job | Steal the marked item from the Solitude house. |
| 3 | Vex | Sweep Job | Steal the three marked items from the Solitude house. |
| 4 | Vex | Shill Job | Plant the marked item in the Solitude house. |
| 5 | Vex | Heist Job | Steal the marked item from the Solitude shop strongbox. |

5. After the fifth turn-in, record Solitude Thieves Guild jobs at 5 of 5 and total Delvin/Vex jobs at 5 of 125.
6. Speak to Delvin and accept `The Dainty Sload`. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-THIEVES-DAINTY-SLOAD` before leaving the Flagon.
7. Travel to Solitude and speak to Erikur in the Blue Palace or Erikur's House for the job details.
8. Go to the Red Wave at the Solitude docks. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DAINTY-BALMORA-BLUE`. Speak to Sabine Nytte and buy the key to her underwater footlocker. Then go below deck, unlock the Red Wave Safe, read and take `Balmora Blue Note`.
9. Swim under the dock beside the Red Wave, unlock Sabine's Footlocker, and take the Balmora Blue. Confirm the Balmora Blue is in your inventory before leaving the dock.
10. Travel to the Dainty Sload near Solitude Lighthouse. Discover and board the ship.
11. Fight or sneak through the ship to the captain's room. Plant the Balmora Blue in the Captain's Chest. In the same final room, take the Stone of Barenziah from the table. Leave `A Dance in Fire, v6` closed on that table.
12. Clear the remaining corsairs and the first mate if they are still alive.
13. Return to Erikur for his spell-tome reward. If the tome teaches a spell you have not learned and it is not a skill book, read it now; otherwise keep or sell the duplicate later.
14. Return to Delvin in the Ragged Flagon, complete `The Dainty Sload`. Solitude's Thieves Guild influence is now restored; keep the other three city influence blocks and the 125-job counter for the later Thieves Guild route.
15. Travel to Morthal and go to Moorside Inn. Make a rotating manual save, wait until Lurbuk is alone or in his room, kill him without a bounty, and leave the inn.
16. Travel to Half-Moon Mill. Make a rotating manual save near the mill, speak to Hern, choose a dialogue line that makes him attack, and kill him. Leave Hert alive unless she becomes an unavoidable active attacker.
17. Return to the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary. Let Astrid approach and start `The Silence Has Been Broken`, then speak to Nazir and collect the rewards for Lurbuk and Hern.
18. Speak to Astrid for the Volunruud order, then travel to Volunruud. Discover the ruin.
19. Enter Volunruud. At Heddic's body near the entrance, read and take `Heddic's Volunruud Notes`, start `Silenced Tongues`.
20. From the entry hub, go southwest to Amaund Motierre's chamber. Speak to Amaund, receive the Jeweled Amulet and `Amaund Motierre's Sealed Letter`.
21. Return to the Volunruud hub. Take the northwest branch for the Ceremonial Axe; leave `Mace Etiquette` closed on the throne arm. Return to the hub, take the northeast branch for the Ceremonial Sword, then use both ceremonial weapons on the Elder's Cairn door.
22. Make a rotating manual save before entering Kvenel's chamber. Let Kvenel stand up or move off his throne before killing him. Search his remains twice if needed, take both Eduj and Okin, preserve them.
23. Open the boss chest and take the `Night Falls on Sentinel` copy without reading it. Keep it for a later routed use. Continue to the word wall, learn Aura Whisper: Nir.
24. Continue to the final loot area, take the Dragon Priest Dagger. Leave `A Minor Maze` alone. Exit Volunruud.
25. Return to the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary and give Astrid the sealed letter and Jeweled Amulet. Travel to Riften, speak to Delvin in the Ragged Flagon about the amulet, and receive the Letter of Credit.
26. Return to Astrid and give her the Letter of Credit. Complete `The Silence Has Been Broken`, start `Bound Until Death`.
27. Before leaving for the wedding, speak to Nazir again and accept the next three contracts: `Kill Deekus`, `Kill Ma'randru-jo`, and `Kill Anoriath`.
28. Travel to Whiterun. At night, enter The Drunken Huntsman, make a rotating manual save, and kill Anoriath cleanly while he is away from the market crowd. Leave his brother and the rest of the inn alone.
29. Find Ri'saad's caravan on the Whiterun-Markarth route or camped outside either city. Speak to Ma'randru-jo, choose the hostile insult so he attacks first, then kill only Ma'randru-jo and leave Ri'saad's caravan unharmed.
30. Travel to Hela's Folly northeast of Dawnstar. Discover the wreck, kill Deekus, and leave the site after searching his immediate camp and wreck if you want the small locked chests.
31. Return to Nazir in the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary. Turn in Anoriath, Ma'randru-jo, and Deekus, then accept `Kill Agnis`.
32. Travel to Fort Greymoor. Enter the keep, make a rotating manual save inside, kill Agnis cleanly, and leave without fighting neutral soldiers.
33. Return to Nazir. Turn in Agnis, then accept `Kill Maluril` and `Kill Helvard`.
34. Travel to Falkreath. Lakeview Manor has already been secured on this route, so kill Helvard cleanly in or around the Jarl's Longhouse and do not fight Siddgeir's household.
35. Travel to Riften before going to Mzinchaleft. Speak to Mjoll the Lioness, ask about her missing sword, and accept `Grimsever's Return`.
36. Travel to Mzinchaleft southwest of Dawnstar. Discover the ruin and enter. In the first zone, kill Maluril in his room, read `Maluril's Journal`, and leave `A Dance in Fire, v7` closed. Continue through Mzinchaleft, read `Mzinchaleft Work Order`, take Grimsever from the Gatehouse, and clear the ruin.
37. Return Grimsever to Mjoll in Riften so she becomes available as a follower. Then return to Nazir, turn in Maluril and Helvard, and accept `Kill Safia`.
38. Travel to the Solitude docks and board the Red Wave. Speak to Safia, use any dialogue option that makes her attack, kill only Safia, and avoid combat with the crew.
39. Return to Nazir and collect the reward for Safia. The finite Nazir contract chain is now exhausted.
40. Travel to Solitude and go to the Temple of the Divines courtyard. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DB-WEDDING` before entering the reception.
41. Reach the small parapet opposite Vittoria Vici's balcony by using the door northwest of the Temple entrance. Take Firiniel's End, the arrows, and the Elixir of True Shot from Gabriella's setup. Preserve Firiniel's End.
42. Wait until Vittoria Vici steps onto the balcony and begins addressing the guests. Use Firiniel's End to kill her during the speech.
43. During the escape chaos, loot Vittoria's body for Vittoria's Wedding Band, Wedding Dress, Wedding Sandals, and Wedding Wreath. Do not attack Asgeir for his wedding band; the guide will take it from him during a controlled pickpocket pass.
44. Escape Solitude and return to the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary. Speak to Astrid, complete `Bound Until Death`, receive Summon Spectral Assassin.
45. Make a rotating manual save before speaking to Gabriella. The next section starts with `Breaching Security`.

### Security, Shadowmere, and the Brotherhood Endgame

1. Speak to Gabriella in the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary and start `Breaching Security`. Take the `Incriminating Letter` from her.
2. Travel to Dragon Bridge and enter the Penitus Oculatus Outpost when you can do so quietly. Take and read `Gaius Maro's Schedule`.
3. Use the schedule to intercept Gaius Maro in Whiterun on Turdas. The clean default is Dragonsreach or the Bannered Mare. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DB-GAIUS-CITY` before engaging him.
4. Speak to Gaius, choose the hostile line that makes him attack first, then let the city guards or court join the fight. Kill Gaius after he is openly hostile, loot `Letter from Father`, plant the `Incriminating Letter` on his body. If you receive a bounty, reload `HS-DB-GAIUS-CITY`.
5. Return to Gabriella. Complete `Breaching Security`, receive Olava's Token. Keep the token until Olava's reading; after the reading, continue whether or not the token remains in your inventory.
6. Go to Whiterun and speak to Olava the Feeble behind Breezehome. Show her Olava's Token and accept the reading that points to Deepwood Redoubt and Hag's End.
7. Travel to Deepwood Redoubt. If the Reach target branch already cleared Deepwood Redoubt in your save, pass through the cleared ruin to Deepwood Vale. Otherwise, discover and clear Deepwood Redoubt now. Leave the `Sacred Witness` and `The Legend of Red Eagle` copies closed.
8. In Deepwood Vale, fight through the Forsworn camp and take Predator's Grace from the top-of-waterfall chest area before entering Hag's End.
9. Enter Hag's End and make `HARD SAVE: HS-DB-ASSASSIN-OLD` before the throne room. Leave `Mystery of Talara, Part 4` closed.
10. In the throne room, use the handle behind the throne to open the hidden chamber. Loot the Assassin of Old for Ancient Shrouded Armor, Ancient Shrouded Boots, Ancient Shrouded Cowl, and Ancient Shrouded Gloves.
11. Continue through Hag's End to the word wall. Learn Slow Time: Tiid.
12. Finish the final Hag's End balcony fight, take Bloodthorn from the altar, and leave once Hag's End is clear.
13. Return to the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary and speak to Astrid to start `The Cure for Madness`. Search Cicero's room, read `Cicero's Journal - Final Volume` for the pass phrase, then return to Astrid.
14. Outside the Sanctuary, make `HARD SAVE: HS-DB-SHADOWMERE`. Watch Shadowmere rise from the pool, mount him once.
15. Travel to Dawnstar Sanctuary. Discover the map marker, read Cicero's journal pass phrase at the Black Door, enter, and make a rotating manual save inside.
16. Work through Dawnstar Sanctuary. Leave the loose skill books and quest books closed. If Summon Spectral Assassin is available, summon him once before reaching Cicero so his Cicero line is heard during the route.
17. At Cicero, make `HARD SAVE: HS-DB-CICERO`. Main route: spare Cicero by leaving him alive and walking out of the Sanctuary. Do not kill him for his clothes; the guide preserves him as a follower and handles his clothing set later.
18. Return to Astrid and complete `The Cure for Madness`.
19. Speak to Festus Krex and start `Recipe for Disaster`. Receive the autographed `Uncommon Taste - Signed`.
20. Travel to Markarth and go to the Understone Keep kitchen. Question Anton Virane until he reveals Balagog gro-Nolob as the Gourmet, then kill Anton without witnesses before leaving Markarth.
21. Travel to Nightgate Inn. Discover the inn.
22. While you are inside Nightgate Inn, read `Nightgate Inn Patron's Note`. Go behind the inn, search the barrel named by the note, read `Undercover Guard's Journal`.
23. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DB-GOURMET-BODY`. Find Balagog gro-Nolob either outside by the pond in the afternoon or in the Nightgate Inn Cellar at night. Kill him, take `Gourmet's Writ of Passage`, and hide his body in the pond or behind the cellar haystack/barrel before leaving.
24. From Nightgate, follow the `Brothers in Irons` lead while it is active. Travel prepared for cold to Clam Digger's Camp near Icewater Jetty, west of Northwatch Keep. Defeat the Thalmor at the camp, search Pale-Eyes, take the full enchanted Iron Plate armor set and `Pale-Eyes' Journal`.
25. Until you present the writ in Solitude, keep watch for Balbus as a random road encounter. If he appears naturally, tell him you are the Gourmet, accept Balbus's Fork and the ingredients. Do not roam solely to force the encounter; the guide will revisit Balbus if he does not appear during this writ window.
26. Return to Festus in the Dark Brotherhood Sanctuary. Complete `Recipe for Disaster`, receive Nightweaver's Band for hiding Balagog's body.
27. Speak to Astrid and start `To Kill an Empire`. Take Jarrin Root from her, but preserve it for the later alchemy-effect route. Do not eat it, cook with it, drop it, or store it in a respawning container.
28. Travel to Castle Dour in Solitude. Show `Gourmet's Writ of Passage` to Commander Maro, put on the chef's hat when Gianna requires it, and cook the Potage le Magnifique. When Gianna asks for a final secret ingredient, finish the potage without adding Jarrin Root.
29. Follow Gianna to the dining room. Leave `Catalogue of Weapon Enchantments` closed on the table before the dining room; the guide returns to this copy after Scholar's Insight. Kill the decoy Emperor manually after the meal is served, then escape across the bridge after Commander Maro's ambush. Do not loot the decoy Emperor's body.
30. Return to the Falkreath Sanctuary by way of Falkreath rather than fast-traveling directly to the Sanctuary. Enter the Sanctuary to complete `To Kill an Empire`.
31. Fight through the burning Sanctuary during `Death Incarnate`. Reach Nazir, save him from Arcturus, then make a rotating manual save before entering the Night Mother's coffin.
32. Enter the coffin. After Nazir and Babette dig you out, speak to the Night Mother, find Astrid, take the Blade of Woe beside her, and kill Astrid.
33. Return to the Night Mother, complete `Death Incarnate`, start `Hail Sithis!`.
34. Speak to Nazir, then travel to Whiterun and meet Amaund Motierre in the Bannered Mare. He sends you to the real Emperor aboard the Katariah near Solitude.
35. Travel to the Solitude docks. Commander Maro is outside the East Empire Company Warehouse. Approach him, let him attack first, and kill him without a bounty before boarding the Katariah.
36. Swim to the Katariah's anchor chain and enter the ship. Fight or sneak through the lower ship, then reach the deck. Discover the ship.
37. On the deck, go to the bowsprit and take Windshear. Leave the `King` skill book closed on the high deck near the throne.
38. Enter the Emperor's Quarters. Hear Titus Mede II out, then kill him. Take the Emperor's key, Emperor's Robes, Gilded Wristguards, `Brothers of Darkness`, and `Glories and Laments`. Leave the duplicate `Lost Legends` copy closed; the guide already started `Forbidden Legend` from the Blue Palace copy before Folgunthur.
39. Finish clearing the Katariah's remaining hostile crew and agents, then leave the ship.
40. Return to Amaund Motierre at the Bannered Mare and receive the Volunruud dead-drop instruction. After the dialogue, make a rotating manual save and kill Amaund privately in his room to fulfill the Emperor's final request.
41. Go to Volunruud, return to Amaund's chamber, and collect the 20,000 gold payment from the urn. Then return to Nazir at Dawnstar Sanctuary. Complete `Hail Sithis!`.
42. Because Cicero was spared, confirm he has returned to the Dawnstar Sanctuary after `Hail Sithis!`.
43. Speak to Nazir and start `Where You Hang Your Enemy's Head...`. Travel to the Ragged Flagon and buy every Dawnstar Sanctuary upgrade from Delvin Mallory: New Banners, Poisoner's Nook, Torture Chamber, Secret Entrance, and Master Bedroom. The full restoration costs 19,000 gold.
44. After all five upgrades are bought, ask Delvin about the Sanctuary again until the quest completes.
45. Return to Dawnstar Sanctuary. In the restored master bedroom, take Tumblerbane Gloves. Confirm both Dark Brotherhood Initiates are now available as followers.
46. In the torture chamber, speak repeatedly to all four torture victims until every hidden-treasure marker has been revealed. Then collect all four caches: the hollowed-out tree stump in the marsh south of Solitude and northeast of the Apprentice Stone, the hollowed-out rock north of Windhelm near the northeast wall, the hollowed-out rock due west of Pelagia Farm near Whiterun, and the hollowed-out tree stump northwest of Riften just beyond Merryfair Farm. After all four caches are looted, return to the Dawnstar Sanctuary.
47. Approach the Night Mother for one representative `The Dark Brotherhood Forever` contract. Record the actual client and target the game assigns.

| Possible Client | Location |
| --- | --- |
| Blasphemous Priest | Temple of Kynareth, Whiterun |
| Corrupt Agent | Penitus Oculatus Outpost, Dragon Bridge |
| Desperate Gambler | Windhelm Barracks |
| Dishonored Skald | Bards College, Solitude |
| Griefstricken Chef | Windpeak Inn, Dawnstar |
| Indolent Farmer | Vilemyr Inn, Ivarstead |
| Nervous Patron | Candlehearth Hall, Windhelm |
| Scheming Servant | Understone Keep, Markarth |
| Wary Outlaw | Bee and Barb, Riften |
| Unemployed Laborer | Frostfruit Inn, Rorikstead |

| Possible Target | Location |
| --- | --- |
| Visiting Noble | Dragonsreach, Whiterun |
| Big Laborer | Katla's Farm |
| Itinerant Lumberjack | Morthal logging camp |
| Reckless Mage | The Frozen Hearth, Winterhold |
| Seasoned Hunter | Outside Falkreath |
| Poor Fishwife | Riften Docks |
| Grim Shieldmaiden | Markarth streets |
| Traveling Dignitary | Solitude |
| Coldhearted Gravedigger | Windhelm Hall of the Dead |
| Beautiful Barbarian | Ivarstead |

48. Go to the assigned client, accept the payment, then kill the assigned target once. After the target is dead, record the representative contract completion and return to normal route travel.

## Pale, Winterhold, College, and Level-36 Locks

### Dawnstar, Vaermina, and Cold-Weather Setup

1. Start from the restored Dawnstar Sanctuary after the Brotherhood endgame. Use the Sanctuary cooking pot to make at least one hot food item before the northern route opens. The easiest recipes are `Hot Cabbage Soup` with Fire Salts and cabbage, `Hot Potato Soup` with Fire Salts and potato, `Hot Cabbage Potato Soup` with Fire Salts, cabbage, leek, and potato, and `Hot Vegetable Soup` with Fire Salts, cabbage, leek, tomato, and potato. Use one saved Fire Salts now, but keep Balimund's ten-Fire-Salts turn-in incomplete until the guide explicitly sends you back to him.
2. Keep at least one hot food item in your inventory for the next exposed cold leg. If you can make extras, carry them; if Fire Salts are tight, carry normal cooked food as backup and save the rest of your Fire Salts.
3. Travel into Dawnstar during the day. At Windpeak Inn, rent a room from Thoring or Karita if Fatigue is active or a level-up is waiting, then sleep in the rented bed. Buy cooked food if your carried food is low.
4. Outside Windpeak Inn, use the woodcutter's axe and the wood chopping block by the inn to get three firewood if you are not already carrying it. If you do not have leather, take the loose deer hide from the Dawnstar Sanctuary sleeping quarters and tan it at the blacksmith setup outside Rustleif's House. Use the forge with three firewood and one leather to craft Camping Supplies.
5. Carry one Camping Supplies as emergency support only. Do not place it unless the guide tells you to set camp or cold, fatigue, or distance makes an emergency camp safer than pushing onward.
6. Go to Rustleif's House or the forge and speak to Rustleif. Ask about his forge work, then give him the unread `Night Falls on Sentinel` copy taken from Volunruud. Complete his `Rare Gifts` favor, and add Rustleif's favor to Hero of the People tracking. Do not count the skill-book title as read; the guide will route a separate `Night Falls on Sentinel` reading later.
7. Leave Captain Wayfinder's Fine-Cut Void Salts request unstarted for now.
8. Leave Frida's Ring of Pure Mixtures request unstarted for now.
9. Go to Silus Vesuius's house. If `Museum Pamphlet` is in your inventory, read it now. Speak to Silus outside or inside the museum, let the museum-opening scene proceed, accept the Razor-piece job, and take `The Keepers of the Razor` from him. `Pieces of the Past` is now active; the three Razor pieces remain with their later routed locations.
10. Return to Windpeak Inn and speak to Erandur about Dawnstar's nightmares. Accept `Waking Nightmare`, follow him to Nightcaller Temple, discover the temple.
11. Make a rotating manual save outside Nightcaller Temple. Enter with Erandur and stay with him through the entry hall and first barrier sequence. Followers and pets wait outside this quest space; do not try to force them in.
12. In the library, after Erandur asks for the book, take and read `The Dreamstride` from the upper level lectern, then return to Erandur.
13. Follow Erandur to the laboratory, take `Vaermina's Torpor`, and leave `Mannimarco, King of Worms` closed. Before drinking the Torpor, backtrack out to the temple exterior if the route is open, make a rotating manual save outside, then return to Erandur. Drink the Torpor only when you are ready to continue without saving during the Dreamstride.
14. In the Dreamstride, release the Miasma, wake back in the temple, remove the soul gem from the barrier, and follow Erandur toward the inner sanctum. After the Dreamstride is over, exit to the temple exterior once more if the route is open and make `HARD SAVE: HS-DAEDRIC-VAERMINA-SKULL`.
15. Re-enter, continue with Erandur, and in the dormitory take one set of Vaermina Robes.
16. Defeat Veren Duleri and Thorek. When Vaermina tells you to kill Erandur, run the branch first: let Erandur finish destroying the Skull, speak to him when he offers to travel with you. Do not continue from this branch save.
17. Reload `HS-DAEDRIC-VAERMINA-SKULL`. Return to the Skull room, defeat Veren and Thorek again, kill Erandur when Vaermina commands it, and take the Skull of Corruption before it is destroyed. Complete `Waking Nightmare`. Record Oblivion Walker progress at 11 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
18. Complete `Pieces of the Past` now that Silus has opened the Razor hunt. Go to Morthal and get Jorgen's Razor hilt from his house chest through the active quest path. Clear Cracked Tusk Keep in Falkreath Hold, take Ghunzul's Razor piece, and leave `Light Armor Forging` closed near the forge. Clear Dead Crone Rock in the Reach, learn the Dismay word from the wall, take the Stone of Barenziah from the altar opposite the wall, leave `A Game at Dinner` closed, and take Drascua's Razor piece.
19. Return to Silus Vesuius in Dawnstar with all three Razor pieces, then travel with him to the Shrine of Mehrunes Dagon. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DAEDRIC-MEHRUNES-SHRINE`. Play the spare-Silus outcome from the save for branch experience, then reload and choose the main-route artifact outcome: kill Silus, reforge Mehrunes' Razor, enter the shrine, defeat the Dremora, and clear the shrine interior. Record Oblivion Walker progress at 12 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
20. If Siddgeir's bandit-leader job was parked at Cracked Tusk Keep, use the Cracked Tusk clear from step 18 as the marked-leader kill. Travel to Falkreath, report the kill to Siddgeir, and record one additional Hero of the People finite miscellaneous objective. Speak to Siddgeir again, accept the Falkreath thaneship objective, immediately report the three completed Falkreath help actions from the earlier Thadgeir, Mathies, and Hert loop, then accept the Thane of Falkreath title, the Blade of Falkreath, and Rayya's housecarl assignment.
21. If step 20 applied, speak to Nenya and buy the Lakeview Manor plot for 5,000 gold. Ride to Lakeview Manor, discover the property, choose `Small House Layout` at the drafting table, quarry ten stone, use the starting materials and log pile at the site, and build the foundation at the carpenter's workbench. Behind Lakeview, down the hill near the dead wood cutter, pick up The Woodsman's Friend and preserve it. Leave Rayya's steward assignment and remaining Lakeview construction or services for the homes section.
22. If `Fan Favorite` was skipped in the Southern Warm Corridor because Lakeview Manor was parked, route it now before returning north. Travel to Ivarstead, go to Arena Fan's camp south of town, read and take Arena Fan's Note, and take `The Crimson Dirks, v8`. Continue to the Ruins of Bthalft, discover the ruins, clear the exterior bandits, kill Urgnok, and take and preserve `Dwarven Mail`, `Dwarven Mail Boots`, `Dwarven Mail Gauntlets`, and `Dwarven Mail Helmet`. Do not activate the Aetherium pedestal or insert Aetherium Shards.
23. Return to Dawnstar and speak to Skald in The White Hall. Ask for work and accept the giant bounty if he offers it. Record the exact giant camp target and leave the bounty active until the guide routes that camp. This giant job is required before the Pale land permission can be completed.
24. Use Windpeak Inn, The Mortar and Pestle, Rustleif's forge, and the Dawnstar Sanctuary to sell ordinary loot, sleep if a level-up is waiting, refresh food and hot food, and repair cold-travel supplies. Leave in daylight with Shadowmere available, food, at least one hot food item, Camping Supplies, healing, cure disease support, a torch, and enough empty carry space for a College/Winterhold entry loop.
### Winterhold, College Entry, and Saarthal

1. Start from Dawnstar or Winterhold with cold-weather supplies refreshed, Shadowmere available, `Forbidden Legend` already active from Folgunthur, and a rotating manual save made before you approach the College bridge.
2. At Birna's Oddments during business hours, ask Birna about her bad business deal and buy the Coral Dragon Claw for 50 gold. Keep it with preserved quest and collectible items; Yngol Barrow waits until its own route.
3. Approach the College bridge and speak to Faralda. Use the standard spell test, not persuasion or main-quest shout bypasses. If she asks for a spell you do not know, buy the 30-gold tome she offers, read it, and cast that test spell at the College seal or on Faralda if she asks for `Healing Hands`. Record the exact test spell and tome your save used if Faralda had to teach you one: Firebolt, Conjure Flame Atronach, Fear, Healing Hands, or Magelight.
4. When Faralda admits you, cross the bridge, discover the College of Winterhold. If `Visit the College of Winterhold` was active in your journal.
5. Speak to Mirabelle Ervine, receive the College clothing, and take the tour. Your assigned Hall of Attainment bed is valid for resting; the wardrobes, end tables, and drawer in that room are safe College storage, but food barrels are not.
6. Go to the Hall of the Elements and attend Tolfdir's lesson. When he asks for a ward, learn `Lesser Ward` from him if you do not already know it, cast it for the demonstration, complete `First Lessons`. `Under Saarthal` starts automatically.
7. Before leaving the College, ask Drevis Neloren about College business and accept `Out of Balance`. Use the Mystic Tuning Gloves on the focal points in the main courtyard, Hall of Countenance, and Hall of Attainment, then return the gloves to Drevis.
8. Use Tolfdir's walk to Saarthal for the Winterhold town loop. In the Jarl's Longhouse, speak to Malur Seloth and accept the request for the Staff of Arcane Authority.
9. Go to The Frozen Hearth. In Nelacar's room, take the Staff of Arcane Authority from the shop counter while hidden, then return it to Malur.
10. Travel to Whistling Mine, discover it, mine one iron ore with your carried pickaxe, and sell the ore to Thorgar. Leave `Heavy Armor Forging` closed.
11. Travel to the Shrine of Azura, discover it, speak to Aranea Ienith, and accept Azura's Star lead.
12. Return to The Frozen Hearth, speak to Nelacar about Azura's Star until the quest points to Ilinalta's Deep, and leave `The Black Star` active for the Black Star and Ilinalta route after the College support stop.
13. Leave Korir's Helm of Winterhold request and Winterhold thaneship for the later isolated random-assignment route.
14. Travel to Saarthal and meet Tolfdir at the excavation. Discover Saarthal, speak to Tolfdir when he is ready, and enter with him.
15. Follow Tolfdir into the excavation. Speak to Arniel Gane so the artifact markers appear, then collect all three Enchanted Rings before taking the amulet. Keep the rings instead of returning them to Arniel.
16. Take the Saarthal Amulet, equip it, talk to Tolfdir before breaking the wall, then cast a targeted spell or shout at the resonating wall. Keep and preserve the amulet.
17. After Nerien's warning, talk to Tolfdir, continue through the opened passage, and solve the first pillar room as follows: north wall west to east, Eagle - Snake - Whale; south wall west to east, Whale - Eagle - Eagle.
18. In the second pillar room, rotate the pillars in this order: southeast pillar to Whale, northeast pillar to Snake, southwest pillar to Eagle, northwest pillar to Whale. Pull the lever only after all four are set.
19. Make a rotating manual save before the Jyrik Gauldurson fight. Wait for Tolfdir to break Jyrik's link to the orb before spending resources on the boss. Do not use a flame atronach or heavy fire effects near the central altar until Staff of Jyrik Gauldurson is safely in your inventory.
20. Loot Jyrik for the Gauldur Amulet Fragment and `Writ of Sealing (Jyrik)`, take Staff of Jyrik Gauldurson from the altar. If Tolfdir picked up the staff during the fight, buy it back from him before leaving Saarthal.
21. Use the exit passage behind the orb, learn the `Ice Form` word `Nus` from the word wall, loot the boss chest, and leave Saarthal cleared.
22. Return to the College and speak to Savos Aren. Receive Staff of Magelight, complete `Under Saarthal`, start `Hitting the Books`. Go into the newly unlocked Arch-Mage's Quarters, take the Stone of Barenziah.
23. In the Arcanaeum, speak to Urag gro-Shub about the Saarthal artifact so `Hitting the Books` points to Fellglow Keep. `Hitting the Books` stays active; Fellglow itself waits for the College continuation route.
24. On or near Urag's desk, read `Spell Tome: Telekinesis Arrows` and `Telekinesis Arrows Research Notes`. Read only those two Arcane Archer items from the desk now; leave `Soran's Journal` and `Eranya's Research Notes` closed for their later routed quests.
25. Find Brelyna Maryon in the College. Make a rotating manual save, stand in clear view, and let her cast the first test spell on you. Wait until the first effect wears off, return to Brelyna, let her cast the second spell, complete `Brelyna's Practice`.
26. Speak to J'zargo and accept `J'zargo's Experiment`. Take his ten flame cloak scrolls and enter The Midden without a follower, pet, summon, or temporary ally.
27. In The Midden, use J'zargo's scrolls on exactly three undead, one controlled fight at a time. Stop using the scrolls as soon as the counter reaches 3 of 3, even if you have scrolls left.
28. Continue to The Midden Dark and find the strange gauntlet room. Take Investigator's Key and read the Midden Incident Report so the ring trail is clear.
29. Return to the Arcanaeum, make a rotating manual save, and use Investigator's Key on the locked chest while hidden. Take Pithi's Ring, Treoy's Ring, Balwen's Ring, and Katarina's Ring.
30. Return to the gauntlet and place the rings as follows: Katarina's Ring on the index finger, Treoy's Ring on the middle finger, Balwen's Ring on the ring finger, and Pithi's Ring on the little finger.
31. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-COLLEGE-VELEHK-SAIN`. Release Velehk Sain, let his full dialogue finish, receive Velehk Sain's Treasure Map, complete `Forgotten Names`.
32. Return to J'zargo, report the scroll results, complete `J'zargo's Experiment`.
33. Travel to the Talos statue west-northwest of Winterhold and north of Saarthal. Approach until Velehk's hidden cache appears, loot the treasure. Treasure Maps tracking is now 6 of 13 resolved map-and-cache entries.
34. Use Winterhold and the College as support for the northern coast. Discover Giant's Tooth, Pilgrim's Trench, Septimus Signus's Outpost, The Serpent Stone, and The Tower Stone. Clear Bleakcoast Cave, Journeyman's Nook, Sightless Pit, Skytemple Ruins, Wreck of The Pride of Tel Vos, Wreck of the Winter War, and Stillborn Cave. At Journeyman's Nook, take `Treasure Map II`; southeast of Bleakcoast Cave, loot the Dead Hunter for `Treasure Map VIII`. Traitor's Post and `Treasure Map VII` were already handled during `Missing Merchant`. Leave `Battle of Sancre Tor` at Bleakcoast Cave and `The Doors of Oblivion` on the skeleton southwest of Sightless Pit closed for the Scholar's Insight reading window.
35. Clear Yngol Barrow with the Coral Dragon Claw bought from Birna. Set the claw door to Snake - Wolf - Moth, defeat Yngol's Shade, and take the Helm of Yngol.
36. Return to Winterhold or the College, warm up, sell ordinary loot, store the Helm of Yngol and preserved documents only in approved storage, and make a rotating manual save before returning to Dawnstar.
### The Black Star and Ilinalta's Deep

1. Start with `The Black Star` active after the Shrine of Azura and Nelacar leads. Use Winterhold or College support to sleep, sell, store, and restock before the long return to Lake Ilinalta. Bring cold-weather supplies for the return, healing, magic resistance, lockpicks, and enough empty carry space for Dremora loot and the quest reward. Make a rotating manual save outside Ilinalta's Deep.
2. Enter Ilinalta's Deep. If an earlier Falkreath pass only handled the accessible first zone, continue from the opened route. If the Deluge path is still blocked by the quest boulder while `The Black Star` points here, exit and re-enter before continuing. If the exterior trapdoor is missing, leave the cell, return from a nearby support marker, and recheck the trapdoor.
3. Clear Ilinalta's Deep and Ilinalta's Deluge. Leave the local skill-book copies `Breathing Water`, `A Tragedy in Black`, and `The Doors of Oblivion` closed because their selected reading-window copies are routed later. Leave `Spell Tome: Soul Split` for the selected Phinis vendor source unless the books/spells table is later changed.
4. In the final Deluge room, defeat the boss necromancer, take the Broken Azura's Star, read and take `Malyn Varen's Grimoire`, loot the chest, and exit by the ladder. Record Ilinalta's Deep clear.
5. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DAEDRIC-BLACK-STAR` before choosing who repairs the star.
6. Branch route first: return to Aranea at the Shrine of Azura, give her the Broken Star, enter the Star through Azura, fight through the Dremora, loot desired Dremora hearts and equipment before killing Malyn, kill Malyn, receive Azura's Star, and confirm Aranea's follower outcome. Do not continue from this branch.
7. Reload `HS-DAEDRIC-BLACK-STAR`. Main route: return to Nelacar in The Frozen Hearth, give him the Broken Star, enter the Star through Nelacar, fight through the Dremora, loot desired Dremora hearts and equipment before killing Malyn, kill Malyn, return to The Frozen Hearth, and receive The Black Star. Complete `The Black Star`, preserve the artifact in owned storage when you are not using it, and record Oblivion Walker progress at 13 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
8. If two necromancers attack later because Malyn is dead, kill them and read/take `A Scrawled Note` from the carrier. Do not wait-loop for that encounter; continue the route until a later random-encounter policy resolves it.
9. Return to Winterhold, the College, or Dawnstar support, sell ordinary loot, store the artifacts and grimoire only in approved storage, rest if a level-up is waiting, and make a rotating manual save before the Dawnstar route.
### Dawnstar, Pale Blade, and Heljarchen

1. Return to Dawnstar after the Black Star Ilinalta branch. Use Windpeak Inn, The White Hall, the Dawnstar Sanctuary, and the town merchants to sleep, sell ordinary loot, refresh food and hot food, and make a rotating manual save before leaving town again. Confirm you are level 27 or higher before this section reaches Frostmere Crypt.
2. Find Kharjo with Ahkari's caravan at Dawnstar or Riften and accept `Amulet of the Moon`. Complete the assigned retrieval as a direct out-and-back and return the amulet to Kharjo before continuing the Pale route unless the marker is inside a dungeon this section routes below.
3. Go to Quicksilver Mine during the day. Mine at least one quicksilver ore, leave `Cherim's Heart` closed, sell the ore to Leigelf, and add Leigelf's favor to Hero of the People tracking.
4. Buy or keep one cheap alcoholic drink, find Karl in Dawnstar or Iron-Breaker Mine, give him the drink. Add Karl's favor to Hero of the People tracking.
5. Ride south to the destroyed Hall of the Vigilant. Discover and enter the ruined hall. Read `Letter to Keeper Carcette` on or under the ruined table to start `Unholy Vigil`. Read only that letter here; leave `The Aetherium Wars` closed.
6. Return to Windpeak Inn in Dawnstar and read the `Mysterious Note` for `Unholy Vigil`.
7. Ride to Windward Ruins north of Dawnstar. Discover and clear the ruins, loot Azarain's body for `Azarain's Journal` and the full Vigil Veteran armor set, then read the journal.
8. Continue north to Agrane Peryval's Saturalia camp. Speak to Agrane to complete `Saturalia Preparation`. Buy `Saturalia Boots`, `Saturalia Clothes`, `Saturalia Gloves`, `Saturalia Hat`, and the `Reindeer Bill of Sale`. Keep Shadowmere as the route's active mount for now unless the guide later tells you to switch.
9. From the Dawnstar coast, travel to Yngvild with hot food available. Discover Yngvild.
10. Clear Yngvild in one pass. Take `Arondil's Journal, Part 1`, `Arondil's Journal, Part 2`, and `Arondil's Journal, Part 3` as you move through the cave. In the throne room area, defeat Arondil, take `Arondil's Journal, Part 4`, and take the Stone of Barenziah from the bedroom behind the throne. `Toying With The Dead` is now ready to turn in to Vekel when the guide returns to the Ragged Flagon.
11. In Yngvild's treasure room, take and read `Spell Tome: Conjure Haunting Spirit`. Take the Masked Necromancer Hood and Runic Necromancer Robes from the Yngvild Throne Room. Leave `Reality & Other Falsehoods` closed.
12. Leave Yngvild. Captain Wayfinder's `Salty Sea-Dogs` remains unstarted for its later isolated assignment.
13. At night, return to Quicksilver Mine. Make a rotating manual save, then wait inside until Harald leaves the ordinary mining area for the lower mine path. Kill Harald while hidden before he opens the secret wall; if you gain a bounty or a civilian witness sees the kill, reload and repeat it cleanly. Loot and read `Miner's Note` from Harald before the cult is dead.
14. Use `Miner's Note` to find the button near the dead-end shaft and open the secret Molag Bal cavern in Quicksilver Mine. In the cavern, take `Notes on Dissection` from the bone-and-tools table and `Galur Rithari's Papers` from the table south of the altar. Loot the Vigil Enforcer set from the sacrificed Vigilant corpse and the Vigil Silver Hand set from the northeastern table.
15. Kill Fenrik and the remaining cultists. Loot Fenrik for `Fenrik's Journal` and the full Vigil Corrupted armor set. Complete `Unholy Vigil`.
16. Refresh in Dawnstar, then ride west to High Gate Ruins. Discover High Gate Ruins, enter, speak to Anska, and accept `A Scroll For Anska`. Keep Anska alive; if she dies before the quest is complete, reload the save from before High Gate.
17. Follow Anska through High Gate. Leave `A Hypothetical Treachery` closed. In the lever room, pull the levers in this order: Eagle, Whale, Fox, Snake.
18. In Vokun's throne room, defeat Vokun, take Vokun and the Staff of Fireballs, learn the `Storm Call` word `Bah` from the word wall, and take the Sealed Scroll from the stone table in front of the wall.
19. Return the Sealed Scroll to Anska, receive and read `Spell Tome: Conjure Flaming Familiar`, complete `A Scroll For Anska`. Add `A Scroll For Anska` to Sideways tracking only if the Sideways trophy is still open.
20. Return to Dawnstar or another warm support point, then prepare for Frostmere Crypt. Before entering Frostmere, confirm both conditions: level 27 or higher, and a rotating manual save made.
21. Travel toward Frostmere from the west road so Eisa Blackthorn's outside scene triggers correctly. On the approach, discover The Lord Stone, and keep your current standing-stone blessing active.
22. At Frostmere Crypt, let Eisa's bandit scene play, speak to her if she survives, and discover the location.
23. Enter Frostmere Crypt. Take `Kyr's Bounty` from the first bandit-table area. In the shelf passage, leave `The Red Kitchen Reader` closed.
24. Continue through Frostmere. Take `Kyr's Log` from Kyr's room, then take `Ra'jirr's Note` and `Eisa's Journal` from the next bedroom table.
25. In Frostmere Depths, let Ra'jirr and the Pale Lady sequence resolve, then fight and kill the Pale Lady. Take and preserve The Pale Blade from Ra'jirr or the ground; do not place it on the pedestal as the final route outcome. Complete `The Pale Lady`, and add `The Pale Lady` to Sideways tracking only if the Sideways trophy is still open.
26. Learn the `Ice Form` word `Iiz` from the Frostmere word wall, loot the boss chest, and leave Frostmere cleared.
27. Refresh supplies again, then use Skald's giant-bounty marker to identify which Pale giant camp is the active bounty target. Clear all four Pale giant camps in this loop; at the camp with Skald's marker, make sure the marked giant is dead before leaving.
28. Clear Blizzard Rest.
29. Clear Red Road Pass.
30. Clear Stonehill Bluff. West of Stonehill Bluff and south-southeast of the Shrine of Mehrunes Dagon, loot the female Bosmer corpse on the rocky outcrop for `Treasure Map VI`.
31. With Treasure Map VI in your inventory, go to Korvanjund but stay outside the ruin. Discover Korvanjund, climb onto the barrow entryway, and loot the Treasure Map VI chest under the tree. Treasure Maps tracking is now 7 of 13 resolved map-and-cache entries.
32. Clear Tumble Arch Pass.
33. Sweep the Hjaalmarch marsh and cold passes from Dawnstar, Morthal, or Heljarchen support. Discover Hjaalmarch Imperial Camp, Hjaalmarch Stormcloak Camp, Kjenstag Ruins, The Apprentice Stone, Shrine of Mehrunes Dagon, North Cold Rock Pass, and South Cold Rock Pass. Clear Brood Cavern, Cold Rock Pass, Eldersblood Peak, Fort Snowhawk, Rannveig's Fast, and Skyborn Altar. In Rannveig's Fast, learn the `Kyne's Peace` word `Drem`, fall through Sild's trap route, and take the Stone of Barenziah beside the alchemy lab and chest. Leave `A Dance in Fire, v2` in Fort Snowhawk closed for the Scholar's Insight reading window.
34. Sweep the Dawnstar and Pale coast. Clear Fort Fellhammer, Frostflow Lighthouse, and Wreck Of The Brinehammer. At Frostflow Lighthouse, take the Torchbug in a Jar, read and take Habd's Journal, Ramati's Journal, Mani's Letter, Sudi's Journal, the Bloodstained Note, and the Scrawled Page, retrieve Habd's remains from the Chaurus Reaper, and place the remains in the lighthouse brazier for Sailor's Repose.
35. Sweep the Nightgate and Pale pass. Discover Bloodchill Cavern, Pale Stormcloak Camp, Bronze Water Cave, and Wayward Pass. Clear Bronze Water Cave, Darkshade, Duskglow Crevice, Ironbind Barrow, Mount Anthor, Shearpoint, Shrouded Grove, Silverdrift Lair, Snowpoint Beacon, Valtheim Towers, and Weynon Stones. At Wayward Pass, take the Ancient Traveler's Skull from the Arkay altar and preserve it. In Duskglow Crevice, take the Moth in a Jar. At Valtheim Towers, loot the Treasure Map II chest. Treasure Maps tracking is now 8 of 13 resolved map-and-cache entries. At Shearpoint, kill the dragon and Krosis, preserve Krosis, and learn all three Throw Voice words from the wall.
36. Return to Skald in The White Hall and collect the giant bounty. Complete `Kill the Giant`, and add Skald's bounty to Hero of the People tracking.
37. Speak to Skald again about helping the people of the Pale. Because the route has already completed at least three Pale help objectives, accept Thane of the Pale, receive the Blade of the Pale, and unlock Gregor. Gregor is also a non-default marriage option; leave him unrecruited and unassigned for now.
38. Ask Skald or Bulfrek about buying a house in the hold and purchase the Heljarchen Hall land plot for 5,000 gold.
39. Ride to Heljarchen Hall. Discover the property. Use the drafting table to choose `Small House Layout`, quarry stone on site if the chest materials are short, and use the carpenter's workbench to build the foundation. This should be the third Hearthfire land plot in the route.
40. Stop Heljarchen construction after the foundation. The full Heljarchen build, Library/Trophy Room/Enchanter's Tower wings, Gregor steward assignment, bard, carriage driver, stable horse, livestock, and grain mill are routed later with the full Hearthfire construction pass.
41. If level 30 has not been reached yet, continue the College route and return to this step at the first support stop after reaching level 30. Do not enter Knifepoint Ridge before this route block.
42. From a warm support stop, travel to Whiterun and enter Dragonsreach Dungeon. On the table near the jail, read and take `Death of a Crimson Dirk` to start `Beyond the Grave`. On the hallway dining table left of the first cell door, read and take `The Crimson Dirks, v3`.
43. Travel to Falkreath Graveyard. Find Edward's dug-up grave and the dead Khajiit nearby, then read and take `Khajiit's Note`. Confirm the `Beyond the Grave` marker now points to Knifepoint Ridge, but do not go there until Boethiah has sent you there too.
44. Hire Stenvar in Candlehearth Hall as the route-default sacrifice follower, strip him of any borrowed inventory, then travel to the Sacellum of Boethiah. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DAEDRIC-BOETHIAH` before ordering him to use the Pillar of Sacrifice.
45. Complete the sacrifice and defeat the cultists. When Boethiah sends you to Knifepoint Ridge, refresh supplies, make a rotating manual save outside the camp, and enter the exterior camp for both active quests.
46. Clear the Knifepoint exterior camp. Kill the Daedric Plate bounty hunter, loot and preserve the Daedric Plate Armor and Daedric Plate Helmet, then read and take `Bounty Hunter's Note`. `Beyond the Grave` is complete when the armor is retrieved.
47. Enter Knifepoint Mine through the now-open Boethiah path. Kill Boethiah's Champion and the mine bandits, avoid the cramped gap under the wooden ramp unless you are ready to reload if stuck, loot the Ebony Mail, equip it from inventory when the quest objective requires it, then preserve it afterward. Record Oblivion Walker progress at 14 of 15 qualifying Daedric artifacts.
48. If Siddgeir's bandit-leader job ever assigned Knifepoint Ridge, do not try to solve it here; that assignment should have been reloaded at `HS-FALKREATH-LAND-JOB` as a bug-conflict result.

### Fellglow Keep and Good Intentions

1. Prepare at the College, Whiterun, or another warm support point before the Fellglow trip. Bring food, healing, lockpicks, a ranged option, and enough empty carry space for books, a Stone of Barenziah, and College rewards.
2. Travel to Fellglow Keep. Discover Fellglow Keep, then enter through Fellglow Keep Dungeons. This pass enters the keep for `Hitting the Books`; the keep will not receive its clear tag yet.
3. Work through the dungeon until you reach Orthorn's cage. Pull the lever to free Orthorn. When he offers to come with you, send him away rather than using him as a bargaining piece.
4. Continue through Fellglow Keep Dungeons and into Fellglow Keep. In the room with the Conjuration skill book, leave `The Doors of Oblivion` closed.
5. In the west room with the alchemy lab, arcane enchanter, workbench, and anvil, take the Stone of Barenziah from the counter.
6. Continue upstairs to the circular library room. Leave `A Hypothetical Treachery` closed. Take and read `Spell Tome: Sparks` if you do not already know the spell. If you already know Sparks, take the tome as a tracked tome copy without reading a duplicate.
7. Enter the Fellglow Ritual Chamber and speak to the Caller. Fight and kill her rather than trading Orthorn away or using the persuade outcome, then loot the Fellglow Ritual Chamber Key from her.
8. Take the three quest books from the Ritual Chamber: `Fragment: On Artaeum`, `The Last King of the Ayleids`, and `Night of Tears`. Make sure the Ayleid book has the exact title `The Last King of the Ayleids`.
9. Leave Fellglow through the Ritual Chamber exit path to the exterior. Do not backtrack through the keep after the Caller fight.
10. Return to the College and speak to Urag gro-Shub in the Arcanaeum. Turn in the three stolen books, complete `Hitting the Books`.
11. Urag gives six magic skill books as the reward: `Daughter of the Niben`, `2920, Hearth Fire, v9`, `Response to Bero's Speech`, `Catalogue of Weapon Enchantments`, `The Black Arts On Trial`, and `Racial Phylogeny`. Keep all six unread in safe storage or in your inventory; do not count any of them as read yet.
12. Speak to Arniel Gane at the College and accept the first part of `Arniel's Endeavor`. Record the objective to bring him ten Dwarven Cogs. Do not make a special Dwemer trip for the cogs here.
13. Make a rotating manual save, then go to the Hall of the Elements and speak to Tolfdir about `Night of Tears` to begin `Good Intentions`.
14. Listen to Tolfdir until Ancano interrupts. Follow Ancano to the Arch-Mage's Quarters only after he tells you to come with him, then speak to Quaranir.
15. After Quaranir leaves, ask Tolfdir about the Augur of Dunlain before going into the Midden.
16. Enter the Midden, continue into the Midden Dark, speak to the Augur of Dunlain, and return to Savos Aren.
17. Report the Augur's warning to Savos. Receive and preserve Mage's Circlet, complete `Good Intentions`. `Revealing the Unseen` should start after this handoff.
18. Before leaving the College, speak to Tolfdir about his missing alembic. Search the Hall of Countenance for Tolfdir's Alembic in these possible spots: the top-floor northern room on a barrel beside the central dining bench, under the left display case in the top-floor enchanting room, or on a crate in the bottom-floor eastern room with the large barrel. Return it to Tolfdir, complete `Tolfdir the Absent-Minded`. After it is complete, do not ask him about the missing alembic again.
19. Make a rotating manual save at the College before asking Mirabelle about the Staff of Magnus.

### Mzulft and the Winterhold Crisis

1. Start at the College or Winterhold after `Good Intentions`. Dismiss followers and pets for this College crisis pass. Bring hot food, healing, lockpicks, a ranged option, and enough empty carry space for ten heavy Dwemer Cogs.
2. Speak to Mirabelle Ervine and ask about the Staff of Magnus. Take the Mzulft lead for `Revealing the Unseen`.
3. Travel to Mzulft, southeast of Windhelm. Discover Mzulft.
4. Before entering the main ruin, enter the Dwarven Storeroom south of the entrance. Pick through to the shard room, take the Aetherium Shard. Record Aetherium Shards at 4 of 4. If Katria points you toward the Forge, leave `Lost to the Ages` active for its later named-save forge route.
5. In the Dwarven Storeroom, collect the four `Dwemer Cog` items if you have room. They count toward Arniel's request; `Dwarven Gear` and `Dwemer Gyro` do not.
6. Enter Mzulft. Let Gavros Plinius finish, loot Mzulft Key and `Research Log`.
7. Work through Mzulft and Mzulft Boilery toward the Aedrome. Collect exactly ten total `Dwemer Cog` items between the Dwarven Storeroom and the main ruin. If you open the master-locked Boilery side room, leave `The Lunar Lorkhan` closed.
8. In Mzulft Aedrome, defeat the Falmer group and take the Focusing Crystal from the Falmer carrying it.
9. Take Mzulft Observatory Key from the east-wing chest after the automaton room. Use it to reach Paratus Decimius and give him the crystal.
10. At the Oculory controls, take `Spell Tome: Flames` and `Spell Tome: Frostbite`. Flames is already in your spell list from character creation. If Frostbite is not already known, read the tome now. If Frostbite was learned earlier, take the tome without reading a duplicate.
11. Make a rotating manual save before placing the Focusing Crystal. After placing it, finish the Oculory puzzle before leaving the Aedrome or quitting. Use Frostbite on the crystal until the beams line up with the three blue bands; use Flames only if you need to move the beams back. Then press the left button once, the middle button six times, and the right button five times.
12. Speak to Paratus until the quest points back to the College and Labyrinthian is identified. Take the shortcut exit and let Nerien's warning play if it triggers.
13. After the full Mzulft route is complete and you have left the ruin, return to Winterhold and the College.
14. Return to the College, but do not enter the Hall of the Elements yet. Find Arniel Gane and give him ten Dwemer Cogs to finish the first part of `Arniel's Endeavor`. Record Arniel part one complete, but do not treat the full quest as complete. End the conversation after the cog reward; his next project is routed later.
15. Make a rotating manual save outside the Hall of the Elements. Enter, report to Savos, help break the barrier if prompted, and let the Ancano/Eye scene play through. When Mirabelle wakes you, confirm `Revealing the Unseen` completes and `Containment` begins.
16. Follow `Containment` immediately. Go to the courtyard, speak to Tolfdir by Savos Aren's body, then go down to Winterhold with Faralda and Arniel.
17. Leave your mount and pets out of the town fight, then kill all ten magic anomalies attacking Winterhold.
18. Return to Mirabelle at the College. Tell her Labyrinthian is the Staff's location, receive Savos Aren's Amulet and the Torc of Labyrinthian, complete `Containment`. Preserve Savos Aren's Amulet. Keep the Torc of Labyrinthian ready for the next route section.
19. `The Staff of Magnus` is now active. Make a rotating manual save and leave Labyrinthian for the next section.

### Labyrinthian and the Eye of Magnus

1. Start with `The Staff of Magnus` active, Savos Aren's Amulet preserved, and the Torc of Labyrinthian in your inventory. Dismiss followers and pets for this section. Bring hot food, healing, lockpicks, a ranged option, and enough empty carry space for several unique items and spell tomes.
2. Travel to Labyrinthian, southeast of Morthal. Discover Labyrinthian and enter the ruined circular building in the exterior complex. Take the Wooden Mask from the skeleton beside the note. Preserve the mask for the later dragon-priest-mask shrine route; do not equip it in the sanctuary yet.
3. Enter Lost Valkygg through the proper northeast entrance: from the circular building, go up the two stairways east of the building, turn north, and go down to the entrance door. Do not use the nearby upper exit door as your entrance.
4. Clear Lost Valkygg from the proper entrance to the final deathlord room. In the southern side cell, leave `Aevar Stone-Singer` closed; that skill-book title waits for the Scholar's Insight reading window. Defeat the final deathlord, loot the boss chest, and leave through the exit.
5. Return to the Labyrinthian exterior and enter Shalidor's Maze without a follower. Use the projected staves or your own spells on the four sigils: Magelight for Alteration, Firebolt or another targeted Destruction spell for Destruction, Fear or another targeted Illusion spell for Illusion, and Repulsion or another targeted Restoration effect for Restoration.
6. In Shalidor's Maze, learn the `Dismay` word `Maar` from the word wall. In the shutter section after the Illusion sigil, take and read `Spell Tome: Heal Other`. Leave the `Liminal Bridges` skill book beside the skeleton closed for the later Scholar's Insight reading window.
7. Complete the maze trial. When the dremora trial begins, damage the dremora enough to return to the entrance rather than trying to kill him in one opening blow. Kill him after the maze returns you to the entrance, then take the Diadem of the Savant from his corpse.
8. Make a rotating manual save before entering the main Labyrinthian ruin. Go to the Ceremonial Door, use the Torc of Labyrinthian, and enter for `The Staff of Magnus`.
9. Work through the first Labyrinthian zone, defeat the skeletal dragon group, and continue into Labyrinthian Chasm.
10. In Labyrinthian Chasm, use Flames on the frost-sealed door. Behind the lightning-rune door, enter the small enchanter room and take and read `Spell Tome: Equilibrium`.
11. Continue through the chasm and into Labyrinthian Thoroughfare. At the fire-sealed door, use Frostbite or another frost effect to open the way.
12. In Labyrinthian Tribune, take and read `Spell Tome: Steadfast Ward` from the podium near the beginning of the zone.
13. At the adept-locked side gate before the throne room, open the gate and take the Ancient Helmet of the Unburned from the small catacomb.
14. Defeat the throne-room draugr and spectral warhound, then learn the `Slow Time` word `Ul` from the word wall.
15. Continue to Morokei's chamber. Kill the two enthralled wizards maintaining Morokei's shield, then defeat Morokei. Loot both the Staff of Magnus and Morokei from his remains, and preserve both.
16. Leave through the shortcut route. Defeat Estormo when he blocks the exit, then leave Labyrinthian.
17. Return to Winterhold and approach the College on foot from town. Clear the magic anomalies at the gate with Tolfdir, Faralda, and Arniel, then speak to Tolfdir. Complete `The Staff of Magnus` and begin `The Eye of Magnus`.
18. Make a rotating manual save at the College barrier. Equip the Staff of Magnus, use it on the barrier until the path opens, and follow Tolfdir toward the Hall of the Elements.
19. At the Hall of the Elements, let Tolfdir enter and begin the Ancano scene. Stay near the entry until Ancano's dialogue and paralysis effect properly start, then use the Staff of Magnus on the Eye until Ancano becomes vulnerable. Defeat Ancano; if he becomes invulnerable again, repeat the staff-on-Eye step.
20. Speak to Tolfdir after Ancano falls, then wait through Quaranir and the Psijic Order scene. When Tolfdir speaks to you again, complete `The Eye of Magnus`, receive the key to the Arch-Mage's Quarters, receive the Archmage's Robes, and become Arch-Mage.
21. Go to the Arch-Mage's Quarters after the quest is complete. Take the Arch-Mage's Boots from the quarters and preserve the boots with the Archmage's Robes.
22. Make a rotating manual save. College services are restored; leave Arniel's later stages, Onmund's request, College repeatables, master rituals, and spell-vendor buying for the controlled College side-content section.

### Gauldur Legend Finale

1. Start after `The Eye of Magnus` with College services restored. Retrieve the Ivory Dragon Claw from storage if you put it away after Folgunthur. Carry the Ivory Dragon Claw, food, healing, lockpicks, a ranged option, and enough empty carry space for the Gauldur Blackbow, Emerald Dragon Claw, and final amulet. Keep followers, pets, summons, and temporary allies dismissed until the Reachwater finale is complete.
2. Travel to Ivarstead, then to the island ruin east of town. Discover Geirmund's Hall, make a rotating manual save, and enter.
3. Drop through the central sinkhole into the underground pool, leave the water through the western door, and continue to the flooded pillar chamber.
4. Set the flooded-chamber pillars to Eagle - Whale - Snake - Whale, then pull the lever and continue deeper.
5. At Lord Geirmund's tomb, read and take `Geirmund's Epitaph`, then take Lord Geirmund's Key from the skeleton's hand. Use the key on the door behind the tomb.
6. Continue through the enchanter room and upper passage to the raised bridge. Ignore the obvious spear-trap lever facing the bridge; use the hidden lever behind you to lower the bridge.
7. Cross the bridge route and continue to Sigdis Gauldurson's flooded chamber. Approach the coffin, let Sigdis fully emerge, then defeat the real Sigdis. The real Sigdis is the one without the blue illusion glow and with downward-curving helmet horns.
8. Loot Sigdis for the Gauldur Blackbow, `Writ of Sealing (Sigdis)`, and the final Gauldur Amulet Fragment. Preserve the Gauldur Blackbow as unique leveled gear.
9. Enter the treasure room behind Sigdis's coffin, loot the boss chest, and leave `Words and Philosophy` closed. That skill-book title waits for the later Scholar's Insight reading window.
