15. Speak to Dexion at Fort Dawnguard. `Seeking Disclosure` completes when you bring him the Elder Scrolls, and `Scroll Scouting` is satisfied because the Dragon scroll is already in your inventory. Accept Dexion's instruction for `Unseen Visions`.
16. Travel to Ancestor Glade northeast of Falkreath and discover the location. During this visit, harvest one Yellow Mountain Flower and one Ancestor Moth Wing for the final alchemy-effects pass.
17. In Ancestor Glade, take the Draw Knife from the shrine, use it on a Canticle Tree to obtain Canticle Bark, attract seven Ancestor Moth swarms, stand in the light beam, and read Elder Scroll (Blood). Defeat the attackers after the vision. `Unseen Visions` completes and `Touching the Sky` begins.
18. Travel to Darkfall Cave, south-southwest of Mor Khazgur, and discover the cave. Follow the cave route to the dead Breton camp, pull the chain behind the lean-to to open the concealed passage, and continue to Knight-Paladin Gelebor.
19. Speak to Gelebor, accept the task to kill Vyrthur, and receive the Initiate's Ewer. Enter the Wayshrine of Illumination portal into Darkfall Passage.
20. Route through Darkfall Passage. Take at least one Chaurus Hunter Antennae from a Chaurus Hunter, harvest one Gleamblossom, harvest one Poison Bloom, and keep at least twenty-one ordinary Elven Arrows by the end of the Forgotten Vale route so Gelebor and Serana can each make their special arrows later. Fill the ewer at the Wayshrine of Illumination and enter the Forgotten Vale.
21. Discover Forgotten Vale. Follow the climb out of the entry cave to Prelate Athring and fill the ewer at the Wayshrine of Sight.
22. Continue west through the narrow pass toward the river valley. Take `Unknown Book, Vol. I` from beside the skeleton and chest in the valley, then speak to Prelate Celegriath and fill the ewer at the Wayshrine of Learning.
23. From the Learning area, go northwest to the river and paragon platform area. Kill the frost giant on the east shore near the waterfall and take the Amethyst Paragon. Cross toward the platform area, kill the second nearby frost giant, and take the Sapphire Paragon.
24. Follow the path toward the frozen lake. Speak to Prelate Nirilor and fill the ewer at the Wayshrine of Resolution. On the frozen lake south of the wayshrine, defeat Naaslaarum and Voslaarum, then learn the Forgotten Vale word of `Drain Vitality` from the word wall.
25. Continue through the Falmer canyon path beyond the Wayshrine of Resolution. Before Glacial Crevice, enter the fenced frost giant area and take the Emerald Paragon from the giant. In the mammoth-skull Falmer tent before the Glacial Crevice entrance, take `Unknown Book, Vol. III`.
26. Route through Glacial Crevice and emerge into the southwest Forgotten Vale. Immediately take the left-hand mountain path, defeat the frost giant beyond the Falmer outpost, and take the Diamond Paragon. Continue through the Falmer settlements; in the large ornate tent before the tunnel to the Wayshrine of Radiance, take `Unknown Book, Vol. IV`.
27. Speak to Prelate Edhelbor and fill the ewer at the Wayshrine of Radiance. Cross to the Inner Sanctum, pour the full ewer into the basin, and enter.
28. Inside the Inner Sanctum, use the ewer doors and left-side passage before the final chapel to reach the frost giant holding the Ruby Paragon. Take the Ruby Paragon, then continue to Auriel's Chapel.
29. Defeat Vyrthur and loot his body for Ancient Falmer Boots, Ancient Falmer Cuirass, and Ancient Falmer Gauntlets. Speak to Gelebor when the wayshrine appears. Ask him to bless one batch of ordinary Elven Arrows into Sunhallowed Elven Arrows, leaving at least one ordinary Elven Arrow unconverted. Take Auriel's Bow from the wayshrine.
30. Speak to Serana and ask her to treat ordinary Elven Arrows with her blood. Keep the Bloodcursed Elven Arrows. Do not ask Serana about curing her vampirism; her vampire state is still needed for the next transformation section.
31. During daylight in an isolated outdoor area, equip Auriel's Bow with a Sunhallowed Elven Arrow, aim at the sun, and fire once to use the bow's special power. Keep Auriel's Bow and the remaining special arrows.
32. Before leaving the Vale, return by wayshrine portal to the Wayshrine of Learning and use the paragon platform. Insert each paragon, enter its portal, take the named objective or chest loot, then return to the platform for the next paragon.

| Paragon | Portal destination | Route instruction |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Amethyst Paragon | Darkfall Grotto ledge | Enter the portal and loot the otherwise inaccessible chest. |
| Sapphire Paragon | Inner Sanctum room | Enter the portal and loot the hidden Inner Sanctum room. |
| Emerald Paragon | Forgotten Vale Overlook | Enter the portal, take `Unknown Book, Vol. II` near the waterfall chest, and leave by the portal. |
| Diamond Paragon | Glacial Crevice ledge | Enter the portal and loot the otherwise inaccessible chest. |
| Ruby Paragon | Forgotten Vale Forest | Enter the portal, defeat the Falmer Warmonger, and take Auriel's Shield. |

33. Leave the Forgotten Vale by using a wayshrine portal to Darkfall Cave and exiting back to Skyrim.
34. Before returning to Isran, travel to the College of Winterhold and go to the Arcanaeum. Give Urag gro-Shub all four Unknown Books. Receive the translated copies `The Betrayed`, `Journal of Mirtil Angoth`, `Diary of Faire Agarwen`, and `Touching the Sky`, and complete the Forgotten Vale Books Quest.
35. Return to Fort Dawnguard and speak to Isran to begin the Dawnguard assault for `Kindred Judgment`.
36. Travel with the Dawnguard assault to Castle Volkihar. Fight through the exterior defenders and the keep, raise the portcullis with the chain, and enter Volkihar Cathedral.
37. Confront Harkon with Auriel's Bow. Refuse to give him the bow, defeat him, and use Auriel's Bow to break his altar shield whenever he retreats to it. After Harkon dies, loot Harkon's Sword and Vampire Royal Armor from his remains. `Kindred Judgment` completes the canonical Dawnguard main questline.
38. Leave Serana alive, uncured, and available. Her Vampire Lord service is routed next, then the route will return to the final mortal-state plan.

### Volkihar Finale Branch Continuation

1. On the canonical Dawnguard save after `Kindred Judgment`, make `HARD SAVE: HS-DG-MAIN-RETURN`. This is the return save for the branch continuation.
2. Load `HARD SAVE: HS-DG-BLOODLINE`. BRANCH ROUTE: accept Harkon's gift, complete the Vampire Lord tutorial enough to regain control, and enter the Volkihar questline.
3. Complete `The Bloodstone Chalice`: speak to Garan Marethi, take the chalice to Redwater Den, fill it at the Bloodspring, kill Stalf and Salonia when they attack, add vampire blood to the chalice, and return to Garan.
4. Complete vampire-side `Prophet`: speak to Harkon, follow the Dragon Bridge carriage lead, take and read `Vampire's Note`, enter Forebears' Holdout, take the Weystone Focus from Malkus, lower Dexion's barrier, defeat Dexion, use Vampire's Seduction and feed on him, command him to Castle Volkihar, and have him read Elder Scroll (Sun).
5. Continue the required vampire-side main quest spine through `Chasing Echoes`, `Beyond Death`, `Seeking Disclosure`, `Unseen Visions`, and `Touching the Sky`. Use the same Castle Volkihar, Soul Cairn, Ancestor Glade, Darkfall, and Forgotten Vale route already established on canonical continuity, but take only what the quests require. The long Soul Cairn side sweep, Forgotten Vale translated-book handoff, paragon-platform side work, alchemy ingredient staging, and Auriel's Bow sun-shot action were already completed on the canonical save.
6. After receiving Auriel's Bow on this branch, return directly to Castle Volkihar. Go to Volkihar Cathedral with Serana, confront Harkon, refuse to hand over Auriel's Bow, defeat him, and use Auriel's Bow to break his altar shield whenever he retreats to it. Speak to Garan when he enters after the fight; this completes the Volkihar-side `Kindred Judgment` outcome.
7. Speak to Garan Marethi in Volkihar Keep after the finale and accept the free courtyard repair if he offers it. This branch now records Castle Volkihar as the vampire-side home and faction outcome.
8. Ask Garan Marethi or Fura Bloodmouth for post-finale work until you receive `Destroying the Dawnguard`. If one of them gives a different Volkihar assignment first, do that assignment directly and return to the castle without adding extra side trips:

| Assignment | Direct branch handling |
| --- | --- |
| `Culling the Beast` | Kill the boss vampire at the marked vampire lair and return. |
| `The Hunt` | Kill the marked traveling bard, merchant, or pilgrim spy and return. |
| `Protecting the Bloodline` | Kill the feral-blooded vampire at the marked bandit camp or warlock lair and return. |
| `Deceiving the Herd` | Wear Dawnguard armor, kill an outdoor city target, plant the Incriminating Letter, and return. |

9. When `Destroying the Dawnguard` is active, travel to Dayspring Canyon and enter Fort Dawnguard. Kill the eight required Dawnguard members inside the fort: Agmaer, Beleval, Celann, Durak, Florentius Baenius, Gunmar, Isran, and Sorine Jurard. Bran, Sceolang, and exterior patrols are not part of the required kill list; fight them only if they block the assignment.
10. Return to the quest giver at Castle Volkihar and complete `Destroying the Dawnguard`.
11. Reload `HARD SAVE: HS-DG-MAIN-RETURN`. MAIN ROUTE: canonical Dawnguard-side `Kindred Judgment` is complete, Serana is alive and uncured, Fort Dawnguard remains intact, and the Volkihar finale plus Dawnguard-destruction state exists only in the branch record.

### Companions Finale, Totems, and Beast-Blood Preservation

1. Continue from the canonical Dawnguard save after the Volkihar branch reload. Leave Serana alive, uncured, and available, but do not ask her to make you a Vampire Lord yet. Keep Beast Blood active; the Solstheim route still needs werewolf access at Frostmoon Crag.
2. Store extra loot, sleep if a level-up is waiting, carry hot food, healing, lockpicks, cold gear, and the five Glenmoril Witch Heads, dismiss Serana or any other follower, and make a rotating manual save.
3. Go to Jorrvaskr. With `Purity of Revenge` active, speak to Vilkas and let him join you. Travel to Driftshade Refuge, discover it, and enter with Vilkas. Do not use Beast Form while Vilkas is with you.
4. Clear Driftshade Refuge and Driftshade Cellar. In Driftshade Cellar, leave `The Rear Guard` closed. In the final room, take the Fragments of Wuuthrad, loot the boss chest, leave `2920, Morning Star, v1` and `Warrior` closed, and use the shortcut out.
5. Return to Jorrvaskr. When Vilkas sends you to the Skyforge, `Purity of Revenge` completes and `Glory of the Dead` begins.
6. Attend Kodlak's funeral at the Skyforge. Give the fragments to Eorlund Gray-Mane, go to Kodlak's room in Jorrvaskr Living Quarters, take the final Fragment of Wuuthrad from the end table, then return to Eorlund.
7. Meet the Circle in the Underforge. Receive Wuuthrad from Eorlund, then travel to Ysgramor's Tomb with the Circle and discover the tomb.
8. Inside Ysgramor's Tomb, place Wuuthrad on Ysgramor's statue to open the way, then activate the statue again and take Wuuthrad back. Preserve Wuuthrad.
9. Clear Ysgramor's Tomb with Aela and Farkas as quest followers. In the room before the final chamber, leave `The Legendary Sancre Tor` closed.
10. At the Flame of the Harbinger, speak to Kodlak, place exactly one Glenmoril Witch Head in the flame, defeat Kodlak's Wolf Spirit, and speak to Kodlak again. `Glory of the Dead` completes and you become Harbinger.
11. Do not purify yourself. Keep Beast Blood active and keep the remaining Glenmoril Witch Heads.
12. After Kodlak's spirit is released, climb the ramp in the final chamber and loot the boss chest for the Shield of Ysgramor. Use the exit chain and western passage to leave, then learn Animal Allegiance: Tah from the outside word wall.
13. Return to Jorrvaskr. Go to Kodlak's room, which is now the Harbinger's room, and take the Stone of Barenziah from the bookshelf.
14. Aela, Farkas, Vilkas, Athis, Njada Stonearm, Ria, and Torvar are now follower options. Keep your current follower plan unless you deliberately choose one; Aela must remain available for the totem work.
15. Make a rotating manual save in Whiterun.
16. Complete all three `Totems of Hircine` assignments with Aela while you are still a werewolf. For each assignment, ask Aela if there is any work to be done, record the assigned dungeon, travel directly there with Aela, retrieve the marked Totem of Hircine from the target site, clear that assigned site if it has a normal boss or clear objective, return to the Underforge, place the totem, pray at it once, then leave and re-enter the Underforge before taking the next totem assignment.
17. After the third totem is placed, pray once at each available totem in the Underforge: Totem of Brotherhood, Totem of the Hunt, and Totem of Fear. Finish with Totem of the Hunt active as the route default unless you prefer a different active howl for combat.
18. Continue building the werewolf perk tree whenever the route sends you through fresh hostile content with eligible corpses and no follower or quest-state conflict. Finish the fight in normal form if that is safer, transform with the Ring of Hircine before leaving, feed on every eligible corpse, open the werewolf perk tree while transformed, and buy every available perk. The full tree requires 165 total feedings; do not cure or replace Beast Blood until all 11 werewolf perks are purchased.
19. Ask Farkas for his personal cure work first. Travel with him to Ysgramor's Tomb, activate the Flame of the Harbinger with one Glenmoril Witch Head, defeat Farkas's Wolf Spirit, speak to Farkas, and complete his `Purity`.
20. Return to Jorrvaskr, ask Vilkas for his personal cure work, travel to Ysgramor's Tomb, activate the flame with one Glenmoril Witch Head, defeat Vilkas's Wolf Spirit, speak to Vilkas, and complete his `Purity`.
21. Keep your own Beast Blood after the Companion cures. Complete the representative `Dragon Seekers` assignment before leaving the Companions behind. Ask Farkas or Vilkas for work. If he gives a different normal Companions job first, complete that assignment as a direct out-and-back under the random-job rules from the earlier Companions section, turn it in, and ask again. When `Dragon Seekers` is offered, travel directly with Farkas or Vilkas to the assigned dragon lair, kill the dragon, absorb the soul if it is not stolen, speak to the quest giver to complete the radiant, and record Dragon Hunter progress if a soul was absorbed. This completes the Companions finite post-quest radiant set: all three `Totems of Hircine` assignments, Farkas's `Purity`, Vilkas's `Purity`, and one representative `Dragon Seekers` assignment.
22. Serana's Vampire Lord conversion, Vampire Mastered, Falion's cure, and the final mortal-state setup wait until after the Solstheim Frostmoon Crag ring purchase.

## Solstheim and Dragonborn Spine

### Solstheim Entry, Raven Rock Core, and Frostmoon Rings

1. Start from Whiterun or the nearest carriage hub with `Cultists' Orders` read, Beast Blood still active, the Ring of Hircine available, Serana alive and uncured, hot food, lockpicks, carry space, and enough gold for ferry passage, Raven Rock services, and four expensive werewolf rings. Make a rotating manual save before leaving for Windhelm.
2. Travel to Windhelm Docks. Speak to Gjalund Salt-Sage on the `Northern Maiden`; persuade, intimidate, or pay 500 gold to make him sail to Solstheim.
3. On arrival, discover Raven Rock and accept Adril Arano's greeting. Ask him about Miraak and the Earth Stone lead, but keep the first Solstheim pass centered on Raven Rock support and the Frostmoon work that still requires Beast Blood.
4. Before leaving the dock area, go below the Raven Rock dock where the `Northern Maiden` is moored and take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox beside the pearl oyster.
5. Treat Raven Rock as the active Solstheim hub. Use The Retching Netch, Fethis Alor, Glover Mallory, Milore Ienth, the forge, and the Bulwark as services. Reserve actual sleep on Solstheim for Fatigue or pending level-ups until the All-Maker Stones are cleansed.
6. Enter Alor House during normal hours. Speak to Fethis Alor and agree to bring him East Empire Company Pendants. While hidden downstairs, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox in the southeast room.
7. Speak to Glover Mallory at his smithy. Accept `An Axe to Find`. Ask him about the shadowmark and accept `Paid in Full`; leave the Castle Karstaag formula job active for the later north-island route.
8. Speak to Milore Ienth and accept `Fetch the Netch`. The first two netch jelly samples come from Raven Rock Mine later in this section.
9. Enter The Retching Netch. Rent a room or buy food if Survival penalties need it. Ask Geldis Sadri about Sadri's Sujamma and accept `It's All In The Taste`. Ask him about rumors of hunters at Frostmoon Crag. Leave the green-glowing note and Fork of Horripilation on the keg; the Staff of Sheogorath trial is routed later.
10. While hidden in The Retching Netch, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the bottom shelf of Geldis's bar. Teldryn Sero is available here as a non-default follower option; keep the current follower plan unless you deliberately choose him.
11. Distribute Sadri's Sujamma to nine Raven Rock recipients before the Severin conspiracy is resolved: Fethis Alor, Dreyla Alor, Glover Mallory, Milore Ienth, Garyn Ienth, Cindiri Arano, Lleril Morvayn, Mirri Severin, and Tilisu Severin. Keep the tenth sample for Aphia Velothi or Crescius Caerellius when the route enters Raven Rock Mine.
12. Go to Raven Rock Temple. Speak to Elder Othreloth and accept `Clean Sweep`. Leave the Heretic Dossier in the Temple closed; the Ghosts of the Tribunal branch is routed later.
13. Enter Temple Ancestral Tomb, clear the ash spawn, and take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox in the northernmost alcove, sitting in the ash near the bones. Return to Elder Othreloth and complete `Clean Sweep`.
14. Travel south from Raven Rock to Old Attius Farm. Help Captain Veleth kill the ash spawn, search the ash pile for `Declaration of War`, read it, and show it to Veleth.
15. Go east to Fort Frostmoth. In the small storage room to the left as you enter the courtyard from the main entrance, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the bottom shelf with the Imperial equipment.
16. Clear Fort Frostmoth, kill General Falx Carius, take and preserve Champion's Cudgel, then return to Captain Veleth in Raven Rock and complete `March of the Dead`.
17. After `March of the Dead`, go to the Bulwark. While hidden in the back right room, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox in the northwest corner of the table. Speak to Captain Veleth, accept `Recipe for Distraction`, find the hidden Emberbrand Wine barrel between the ruined-house foundations southwest of the abandoned building, then return to Veleth and complete the favor.
18. Enter Raven Rock Mine from Raven Rock. Give the saved Sadri's Sujamma sample to Aphia Velothi or Crescius Caerellius. Speak to Crescius, start `The Final Descent`, receive `Gratian's Letter` and the Raven Rock Mine Key, then ask him for Glover Mallory's ancient Nordic pickaxe and choose the dialogue that makes him hand it over.
19. In the mine's first room, go up to the loft and take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox under the table in the northwest corner.
20. Break through the wooden slats deeper in the mine. In the room just past the boards, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the crate to the right of the locked gate.
21. Continue through Raven Rock Mine. Take all four Potions of Waterwalking as you pass them: the basin between the draugr thrones, the shelf beside the alchemy lab, the floor beside the expert-locked enchanter chest, and the ledge behind the three pots at the top of the wooden spiral ramp. At the alchemy lab, take the two bowls of Netch Jelly for `Fetch the Netch`.
22. Learn Ice Spike from the spell tome on the candlelit plinth, then continue to Gratian Caerellius's remains. Take and read `Gratian's Journal`, take the Bloodskal Blade, and use Bloodskal Blade power attacks to open the red-line door.
23. Defeat Zahkriisos, take and preserve the Zahkriisos mask, and learn Dragon Aspect: Qah from the word wall.
24. Read `Black Book: The Winds of Change` and enter Apocrypha. Work through the scryes and defeat the lurker. Leave `Words and Philosophy` closed when you first see it on the table near the fourth scrye.
25. At the end of the Black Book realm, choose Scholar's Insight. Before leaving Apocrypha, return to the table near the fourth scrye and read `Words and Philosophy`, then exit through the Black Book.
26. Continue out through Bloodskal Barrow. Clear the reavers, read a copy of `Deathbrand` near the beds in the northwest room, and leave the Deathbrand treasure circuit for the later north-island route. Exit to Solstheim.
27. Return to Raven Rock. Speak to Crescius Caerellius and complete `The Final Descent`. Return the ancient Nordic pickaxe to Glover Mallory; he lets you keep it as the quest reward, so preserve it for Solstheim mining and stalhrim work.
28. Enter Caerellius House and, while hidden if necessary, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the northwest wall ledge in the first room.
29. Return to Geldis Sadri and complete `It's All In The Taste`. Sell the East Empire Company Pendants collected so far to Fethis Alor. Keep bringing him pendants whenever later Solstheim route sections name another strongbox.
30. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-SERVED-COLD` before accepting Raven Rock's conspiracy work. Speak to Captain Veleth, then Adril Arano, and start `Served Cold`.
31. Follow Adril's instructions: speak to Geldis Sadri, stake out Ulen Ancestral Tomb, identify Tilisu Severin, report to Adril, and receive the Severin Manor Key.
32. Enter Severin Manor only for the quest evidence. Recover and read `The Ulen Matter` from the safe, then report back to Adril. Do not use Severin Manor storage or displays before Councilor Morvayn awards the house.
33. Travel to Ashfallow Citadel. Clear the Morag Tong outside and inside. Just inside the citadel, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the shelf directly west of the entrance, half buried in ash. In the interior, take and read `Ahzidal's Descent` from the cupboard shelf near the barrels.
34. Kill Vendil Severin and end the threat to Councilor Morvayn. Before returning to Raven Rock, collect the two nearby Ashfallow pendant strongboxes: one at the unmarked reaver camp across the water west of the citadel, next to the sheltered bedroll west of the campfire; the other by following the stream west of the citadel north to the barred gate at Usha's End, where the strongbox sits on the right side of the wooden structure between a low table and a chest.
35. Return to Adril Arano and follow him to Councilor Morvayn. Complete `Served Cold`, receive Severin Manor, and become Raven Rock's house owner.
36. While inside Morvayn Manor Chambers, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the ledge to the left of the door straight ahead in the first room.
37. Enter Severin Manor as owner. Use it as the Solstheim base for sleeping, crafting, sorted storage, and safe carry recovery. Store preserved unique items and finite collectibles only in normal containers, not in display furniture.
38. Sell the new East Empire Company Pendants to Fethis Alor, resupply hot food, and make a rotating manual save in Raven Rock.
39. Travel northwest to the Shrine of Zenithar near Frostmoon Crag. Take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the ground beside the pickaxe at the shrine, then continue to Frostmoon Crag.
40. Approach Frostmoon Crag while Beast Blood is still active. Let Rakel welcome you, speak to Majni, and buy Ring of Bloodlust, Ring of Instinct, Ring of the Hunt, and Ring of the Moon. Preserve all four rings.
41. After the four rings are purchased, clear Frostmoon Crag. Continue using the Ring of Hircine feeding pattern until all 11 werewolf perks are purchased; do not replace Beast Blood with Vampire Lord until Werewolf Mastered is complete.
42. Return to Raven Rock and make a rotating manual save at Severin Manor or The Retching Netch. Serana's Vampire Lord service is now unblocked by Frostmoon access; the next section handles the actual transformation and starts Vampire Lord mastery tracking.

### Post-Frostmoon Vampire Lord Window

1. At Severin Manor, store the four Frostmoon werewolf rings with your preserved unique gear. Keep the Ring of Hircine on you until Beast Blood is replaced.
2. Before speaking to Serana, transform with the Ring of Hircine somewhere private and open the werewolf perk tree. The werewolf tree is complete only when these 11 perks are purchased: Bestial Strength 1, Bestial Strength 2, Bestial Strength 3, Bestial Strength 4, Totem of Ice Brothers, Totem of the Moon, Totem of the Predator, Totem of Terror, Animal Vigor, Gorging, and Savage Feeding.
3. If any werewolf perk is still missing, keep Beast Blood and continue the Ring of Hircine feeding pattern in fresh hostile content until the tree is complete. Return to this section only after all 11 werewolf perks are purchased. Once the tree is complete, stop using Beast Form and make a rotating manual save.
4. Travel from Raven Rock to Windhelm by the Northern Maiden, then reach Fort Dawnguard by carriage and road. Bring hot food, healing, cold gear, and one filled Black Soul Gem if you already have one stored. Do not use The Black Star for Falion's later ritual.
5. Speak to Serana at Fort Dawnguard and ask her to turn you into a vampire. Confirm the choice. This replaces Beast Blood, gives you Vampire Lord on the canonical Dawnguard save, and starts the final vampire-state window.
6. After Serana turns you, dismiss her back to Fort Dawnguard before the perk work. Her vampire service is preserved, but her follower behavior interferes with the main Vampire Lord kill method.
7. Track Vampire Lord mastery separately from normal skill perks. Start at 0 of 11 Vampire Lord perks purchased on main continuity. Progress comes from kills made with Vampire Lord Vampiric Drain or power-bite attacks; use hostile living enemies, draugr, and vampires. Do not count small animals, constructs, summoned creatures, or most other undead.
8. Buy Vampire Lord perks as soon as points are available. The full tree is Power of the Grave, Detect All Creatures, Mist Form, Supernatural Reflexes, Blood Healing, Unearthly Will, Poison Talons, Night Cloak, Vampiric Grip, Summon Gargoyle, and Corpse Curse.
9. Use Vampire Lord only in hostile areas and out of sight of towns, guards, and friendly hubs. Revert before looting, reading, talking, using city services, or traveling through settlements.
10. Do not start `Rising at Dawn` yet, and do not ask Serana about curing herself yet. The guide will route Falion's cure and the final Serana cure-state choice after Vampire Mastered is complete.
11. Return to Raven Rock with Vampire Lord active as the current transformation state. Use Severin Manor for storage, sleeping, crafting, and cold recovery, then continue the Solstheim route.

### Temple of Miraak, Skaal, and Stalhrim

1. Start from Severin Manor or The Retching Netch with hot food, cold gear, lockpicks, the Ancient Nordic Pickaxe, the saved Potions of Waterwalking from Raven Rock Mine, carry space for stalhrim and armor, and enough stored dragon souls to unlock Bend Will. Continue Vampire Lord mastery tracking from the current count, but use Vampire Lord only in hostile areas and revert before settlements, dialogue, books, looting, and All-Maker Stone fights involving freed NPCs.
2. Travel to the Temple of Miraak. Approach the Tree Stone, then step away from the work site and speak to Frea. `Dragonborn` completes and `The Temple of Miraak` begins.
3. Defeat the cultists with Frea and enter the Temple of Miraak. Clear the temple with her: pass the swinging-blade room, work through the draugr and cultist chambers, and learn Dragon Aspect: Diiv from the word wall.
4. Kill the Gatekeeper, take the Temple of Miraak Key, unlock the sarcophagus door behind his resting place, and continue through the dining room and kitchen. Pull the hidden kitchen handle to open the dining-room passage, use Frea's button/handle prompts to open the deeper route, and continue through the sanctum to the Black Book pedestal.
5. Read `Black Book: Waking Dreams`. Let the Miraak scene play out. When you return to the sanctum, speak to Frea. `The Temple of Miraak` completes, `The Fate of the Skaal` begins, and `Black Book: Waking Dreams` is now part of your Black Book collection for the later Miraak finale and perk-reset system.
6. Follow Frea to Skaal Village. Speak to Storn Crag-Strider, accept the Saering's Watch objective, and prepare for a cold northern dragon-lair trip.
7. Travel to Saering's Watch. Clear the dragon lair, learn Bend Will: Gol from the word wall, and unlock it with a dragon soul before leaving.
8. Return to the Wind Stone west of Skaal Village. Revert to normal form before approaching the enthralled villagers. Use Bend Will on the Wind Stone, let the Lurker fully emerge, kill it with controlled attacks away from the Skaal villagers, then activate the cleansed Wind Stone to gain North Wind.
9. Before crossing back into Skaal Village, make `HARD SAVE: HS-SKAAL-STALHRIM-SCENE`. Return to Storn and complete `The Fate of the Skaal`; `Cleansing the Stones` and `The Path of Knowledge` begin. Frea is now available as a non-default follower option.
10. When Deor Woodcutter and Fanari Strong-Voice begin discussing Baldor Iron-Shaper, stand back until their conversation finishes. Speak to Deor afterward and start `A New Source of Stalhrim`.
11. While Skaal Village is active and safe, speak to Edla, then Nikulas, and convince Nikulas to stay in the village. Return to Edla to complete the favor.
12. Speak to Wulf Wild-Blood and start `Filial Bonds`. From now until the Dragonborn finale, if Torkild appears during normal Solstheim travel, speak to him, kill the werebear, take `Torkild's Letter to Wulf`, and return it to Wulf at the next Skaal Village stop. If he does not appear naturally, the guide will run the search before Storn.
13. Travel southwest toward the Beast Stone. Use Bend Will on the stone, kill the Lurker, and activate the cleansed Beast Stone to gain Conjure Werebear.
14. Continue to the camp south-southwest of Skaal Village, near the lake by Thirsk Mead Hall. Read `Skjol's Journal` at the dead Skaal villager's camp to start `Ancient Ice`.
15. Travel to the Abandoned Lodge and make `HARD SAVE: HS-SKAAL-ABANDONED-LODGE` before entering the combat area. Kill the Thalmor outside, take the Key to Abandoned Lodge and `Hand-written Note` from the Thalmor soldier who carries them, enter the lodge, and free Baldor Iron-Shaper from the basement.
16. Follow Baldor's lead to Northshore Landing. At the landing, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the expert-locked strongbox on the south-wall bookcase inside the ruined shack. Mine the ebony ore vein just south of the abandoned shack and keep the ore for Halbarn.
17. Speak to the Thalmor guard and ask to discuss business with Ancarion. Equip the Amulet of Articulation before speaking to Ancarion, tell him you are there about the Stalhrim map, choose the Stalhrim-weapons deal, and persuade him to hand over the Stalhrim Source Map. This starts Ancarion's stalhrim sale objective; the actual sale waits until the later crafting pass creates a spare stalhrim item.
18. Read the Stalhrim Source Map, then go to the Horker Iceberg northeast of Northshore Landing. Use one saved Potion of Waterwalking for the safer cold-water approach. Kill Skjol the Tongueless, take the full Stalhrim Fur set from the sarcophagus, and mine the two stalhrim deposits with the Ancient Nordic Pickaxe.
19. Travel south to the Stalhrim Source. Discover the landmark and mine all ten stalhrim deposits with the Ancient Nordic Pickaxe.
20. Swing southwest to the Water Stone. Use Bend Will on the stone, kill the Lurker, and activate the cleansed Water Stone to gain Waters of Life.
21. Return to Skaal Village and give the Stalhrim Source Map to Baldor. `A New Source of Stalhrim` completes, stalhrim crafting knowledge is unlocked, and the Stalhrim Source marker is secured. Preserve the Stalhrim Fur set and the mined stalhrim; the Stalhrim Crafter trophy item and Ancarion sale wait for the later Smithing/crafting block.
22. When Tharstan approaches after Baldor is rescued, accept `Lost Legacy`. The next central-island route takes Tharstan to Vahlok's Tomb and handles the Amethyst Claw halves, Battle Fury words, and Vahlok fight.
23. Return to Raven Rock by way of the Earth Stone. Use Bend Will on the stone, kill the Lurker, and activate the cleansed Earth Stone to gain Bones of the Earth. Leave `Cleansing the Stones` active for the Sun Stone in the Tel Mithryn route; the Tree Stone remains tied to Miraak's temple and the Dragonborn finale.
24. In Raven Rock, speak to Bralsa Drel and offer to talk to Geldis Sadri. Go into The Retching Netch, convince Geldis to let Bralsa return, then report back to Bralsa and complete `Feeding the Addiction`.
25. Back in Raven Rock, sell the Northshore East Empire Company Pendant to Fethis Alor, store the Stalhrim Fur set, stalhrim, Northshore ebony ore, and Bralsa's reward at Severin Manor, buy food, clear active Fatigue with a Severin Manor bed, update the Vampire Lord perk count if this loop earned any perks, and make a rotating manual save.

### Vahlok's Tomb, Riekling Roads, and Thirsk

1. Start from Severin Manor with the Ancient Nordic Pickaxe, hot food, cold gear, lockpicks, one meat item for Bilgemuck such as venison, leg of goat, rabbit, salmon, clam meat, dog meat, or pheasant breast, the saved Juniper Berries from Old Hroldan, the stored Northshore ebony ore, 10 stalhrim, and enough carry space for a long Riekling Spear count. Harvest or withdraw 10 Scathecraw around Raven Rock before leaving town.
2. Enter the reopened Raven Rock Mine. Mine all nine ebony ore veins, then use the Raven Rock smelter with the Northshore ore to make 15 Ebony Ingots. Keep the 15 Ebony Ingots and 10 stalhrim together for Halbarn.
3. Travel west-northwest to Ramshackle Trading Post. Discover the shack and take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the crate outside the west wall.
4. Continue through the central island toward the Altar of Thrond. Clear the hagraven cave, use its fire or braziers for warmth before leaving, and return to the road toward Vahlok's Tomb.
5. Meet Tharstan at Vahlok's Tomb. Before entering, go to the Dunmeri camp just east of the tomb, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the tent strongbox, and collect the 18 loose Riekling Spears stuck in the tree and corpses. Keep a running count of Riekling Spears from here until Hilund's favor is complete.
6. Enter Vahlok's Tomb with Tharstan. In the first chamber, let Tharstan translate the inscription, drag one dead draugr onto the grate over the firepit, and use the handle to open the north and south routes.
7. Take the southern route first. In the impact-stone puzzle room, strike the three pillars by their matching methods: melee for the first pillar, archery for the second, and magic for the third. In the final room, loot the boss draugr for the Amethyst Claw, Left Half, learn the first Battle Fury word, and return to the main chamber through the opened sarcophagus passage.
8. Take the northern route next. Cross the nine square stones without stepping on the same stone twice. In the final room, loot the boss draugr for the Amethyst Claw, Right Half, learn the second Battle Fury word, and use the shortcut back to the main chamber.
9. Insert both Amethyst Claw halves into the cage locks and use the cage handle. Cross the timed magical platforms, then stay with Tharstan through the flooded bridge room and the Hall of Stories so he translates each inscription before you use the next handle or puzzle door.
10. At the puzzle door, set the rings to Eagle, Wolf, Dragon from top to bottom. Defeat Vahlok the Jailor in the final chamber, learn the third Battle Fury word, loot the boss chest and Vahlok's remains, then speak to Tharstan to complete `Lost Legacy`. Keep both Amethyst Claw halves with preserved collectibles.
11. Travel northwest to Snowclad Ruins. Clear the outdoor werebear ruin, then continue west toward Moesring Pass.
12. Clear Moesring Pass. Take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the end table north of the Strange Vessel, and collect every Riekling Spear dropped or thrown during the fight. Climb to the nearby Mount Moesring marker from the pass side, trigger the map marker, then return to the main path without lingering on the unstable summit.
13. Travel north toward Hrothmund's Barrow, then continue northwest to Bristleback Cave before resolving Thirsk. Clear the exterior camp and the cave. Collect all 14 guaranteed loose Riekling Spears, every dropped or thrown spear from the Rieklings, and the East Empire Company Pendant from the expert-locked strongbox on the barrels in the lower east tunnel.
14. Follow the shore southwest of Northshore Landing to the Riekling-painted chest between Northshore Landing and Bristleback Cave. Take the seven Riekling Spears sticking out of the chest, then continue southeast to Broken Tusk Mine.
15. Clear Broken Tusk Mine. Take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the crate beneath the upper exterior lookout platform, mine the two Heart Stone deposits inside, and collect every Riekling Spear dropped or thrown during the fight.
16. Travel to Bujold's Retreat and discover the camp. Speak to Elmus before the Thirsk side choice, accept his Ashfire Mead favor, then go to Thirsk Mead Hall. Discover Thirsk Mead Hall, enter the hall under Riekling control, take one Ashfire Mead from the floor along the west side of the firepit, and return it to Elmus.
17. At Bujold's Retreat, make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-THIRSK-CHOICE`.
18. Branch route from `HS-DRAGONBORN-THIRSK-CHOICE`: go to Thirsk Mead Hall and speak to the Riekling Chief. Feed Bilgemuck the meat item and bring him back to the pen, give the Chief 10 Scathecraw, then agree to attack the Nords. Follow the Rieklings to Bujold's Retreat, kill the Nords, defeat the Chief when he turns on you, and speak to a Riekling afterward so the Riekling follower outcome is experienced on the branch. Reload `HS-DRAGONBORN-THIRSK-CHOICE`.
19. Main route after reload: speak to Bujold at Bujold's Retreat and agree to retake Thirsk for the Nords. Follow the Nords to the hall, let them arrive and the assault objective settle, then clear the exterior and interior Rieklings. Collect all Riekling Spears from the hall fight before speaking to Bujold afterward.
20. Meet Bujold at Hrothmund's Barrow. Discover the barrow, enter with her, and listen to Hrothmund's judgment. Tell Bujold you will keep her secret so she returns to Thirsk and Kuvar remains friendly.
21. Before returning to Thirsk, go southwest of Hrothmund's Barrow to Reaver's End. Kill the two Rieklings, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the expert-locked strongbox in front of the crate, and collect the three loose Riekling Spears from the corpse.
22. Return to Thirsk Mead Hall. When Kuvar asks about the barrow, tell him it was an honor to stand with Bujold. `Retaking Thirsk` completes with Kuvar's Heavy Armor training service preserved.
23. Speak to Elmus at Thirsk Mead Hall and give him the saved Juniper Berries. Speak to Halbarn and give him the 10 stalhrim and 15 Ebony Ingots. Halbarn is now available as a blacksmith merchant and non-default spouse option.
24. Count your Riekling Spears before speaking to Hilund. The route has supplied the 18 spears at the Dunmeri camp, 14 in Bristleback Cave, seven at the Riekling-painted chest, three at Reaver's End, plus normal drops from Bristleback Cave, Broken Tusk Mine, Moesring Pass, and the Thirsk assault. Give Hilund 50 Riekling Spears. Hilund is now a non-default spouse option.
25. Sell the Ramshackle, Vahlok, Bristleback, Broken Tusk, Moesring, and Hrothmund East Empire Company Pendants to Fethis Alor at Raven Rock. Store the Amethyst Claw halves, spare Heart Stones, and any preserved Solstheim gear at Severin Manor. Update the Vampire Lord perk count if this section earned any perks, then make a rotating manual save.

### Tel Mithryn, Nchardak, and Kagrumez

1. Start from Severin Manor with the Ancient Nordic Pickaxe, cold gear, hot food, lockpicks, at least one stored Heart Stone, one filled or empty soul gem, enough gold for Tel Mithryn spell tomes, 400 gold for the Nix-Hound, and one each of these stored ingredients if your alchemy cache has them: Briar Heart, Daedra Heart, Giant's Toe, Hagraven Claw, Vampire Dust, and Void Salts. Take the two taproots from Severin Manor's alchemy area.
2. Travel east to Tel Mithryn. Discover the settlement, enter Neloth's tower, rise to the main floor, and return outside. Kill Talvas Fathryon's loose Ash Guardian, then report back to Talvas inside the tower to complete `From the Ashes`. If Talvas offers `Spell Tome: Conjure Ash Guardian` as the reward, choose it; otherwise buy it from him after the quest. Buy and read `Spell Tome: Ash Shell`, `Spell Tome: Ash Rune`, `Spell Tome: Bound Dagger`, `Spell Tome: Frenzy Rune`, and `Spell Tome: Poison Rune` from Talvas before leaving Tel Mithryn.
3. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-TEL-MITHRYN-NCHARDAK`. Speak to Neloth about the Black Book that can help against Miraak, then follow him directly to Nchardak.
4. At Nchardak, clear the reavers outside with Neloth, let him open the Reading Room, and follow him down into the Great Chamber. After he demonstrates the pump pedestal, take his control cube from that pedestal and open the south gate to Nchardak Workshop.
5. In Nchardak Workshop, take the control cube from the first pedestal at the start of the east path, continue down past the disabled flamethrowers, and enter the flooded workshop hall. Place a cube in either east pedestal to drain the room, defeat the awakened Dwemer constructs, and use the lower-level switch to raise the stone stairs. Follow the raised path to the small room with the next cube, take it, defeat the spiders, then return to the east pedestal and remove the cube you placed there so the water rises above its original level. Swim through the south submerged passage, clear the ballista room, take the final workshop cube from the north alcove, and use the nearby switch to lower the bridge back to the entrance. Return to the Great Chamber with four cubes.
6. In the Great Chamber, place two cubes in the upper pedestals to drain the water and follow Neloth down the ramps. At the south alcove, place another cube in the pedestal to drain the chamber fully, then retrieve that cube before opening the north gate to Nchardak Aqueduct. In the Aqueduct, use the left bridge switch and then the right bridge switch above the entrance to lower the bridges, cross to the east pedestal, and place a cube there to drain the room. On the floor level, open the west bronze doorway with a cube, pass the trap corridor, use the blade-control switch on the west platform to open the gate, and take the final cube from the pedestal beyond it.
7. Return to Neloth in the Aqueduct and let him take back the pump cube. Before leaving the Aqueduct, swim and climb to the upper northeast chamber north of Neloth's waiting spot, kill the ballista, and take the Kagrumez Resonance Gem from the shelf or floor near the chest. Return to the Great Chamber, take one cube back from the upper pump pedestals, activate all four boiler pedestals on the central platform, defeat the centurion, and return to the Reading Room. Press the pedestal button to raise the Black Book, then read `Black Book: Epistolary Acumen` to complete `The Path of Knowledge` and enter Apocrypha.
8. In Epistolary Acumen, work through Chapters I and II. In Chapter III, go to the far platform and read `Death Blow of Abernanit`, `Fire and Darkness`, and `Orsinium and the Orcs`; take and read `Spell Tome: Conjure Seeker` from the same platform.
9. Continue through Chapter IV, read the opened Black Book, speak to Hermaeus Mora, learn Bend Will: Hah, choose Dragonborn Force, and return to Nchardak. Speak to Neloth after you return, then leave Nchardak and kill Krosulhah outside. Keep `The Gardener of Men` parked at the Skaal objective until Wulf's brother Torkild has been resolved.
10. Return to Tel Mithryn and make `HARD SAVE: HS-TEL-MITHRYN-VARONA`. Enter the tower and speak to Neloth about Varona to start `Reluctant Steward`.
11. Before leaving the tower grounds, take Neloth's local research work that can be completed at Tel Mithryn: sell him one staged Heart Stone for `Heart Stones`; accept the ash-spawn sample job for `Telvanni Research`; and agree to the first experimental health spell when he offers `Experimental Subject (A)`. Stop using Neloth's open-ended research dialogue after those local jobs.
12. Speak to Elynea Mothren in the apothecary, ask about Varona, and start `Healing a House`. If she sells taproot and you still have fewer than three, buy enough to reach three; otherwise the Headwaters route supplies the last taproot.
13. Travel west toward the Sun Stone marker to find Varona. Kill the three ash spawn around her body, use Neloth's Ash Extractor on one of their corpses, and examine Varona.
14. Use Bend Will on the Sun Stone, kill the Lurker, and activate the cleansed Sun Stone to gain Sun Flare and complete `Cleansing the Stones`.
15. Return to Neloth. Report Varona's death, turn in the ash sample for `Telvanni Research`, and step into the deep water near Tel Mithryn long enough to expose the side effect of Neloth's health experiment. Return to Neloth and report the side effect to complete `Experimental Subject (A)`.
16. Go to Raven Rock and speak to Drovas Relvi in The Retching Netch. Send him to Tel Mithryn as Neloth's steward, then return to Neloth and complete `Reluctant Steward`. Receive the Staff Enchanter key and access to the locked southern chamber.
17. In the Staff Enchanter room, read `Black Book: The Hidden Twilight`. Work through the book's chapters. In Chapter V, activate the scrye that opens the northwest gate and read `A Hypothetical Treachery`, `Racial Phylogeny`, `Breathing Water`, `Before the Ages of Man`, and `2920, Hearth Fire, v9` from the altar. Finish the book, choose Mora's Boon, and return to Tel Mithryn.
18. Use Neloth's Staff Enchanter before leaving the room. Buy an unenchanted Destruction staff from Neloth if you do not already have one, then craft a Staff of Flames with the unenchanted staff, the Flames spell, and one Heart Stone.
19. Let Talvas's research stock settle while you handle Elynea's route and the north-coast errands. Buy and read `Spell Tome: Conjure Ash Spawn` from Talvas on the next Tel Mithryn return in this section.
20. Take `Healing a House` north to the Headwaters of Harstrad. Collect the missing taproot from the spriggans if you still need one, enter the cave behind the waterfall, soak three taproots in the headwaters, loot the boss chest, and return to Elynea.
21. Give Elynea the soaked taproots, plant the remaining taproot in the withered part of Tel Mithryn tower, and return to Elynea to complete `Healing a House`.
22. Ask Elynea for her rare-ingredient request. She may ask for Briar Heart, Daedra Heart, Giant's Toe, Hagraven Claw, Vampire Dust, or Void Salts. Give her the requested ingredient from the prepared cache and complete `Hunting and Gathering`.
23. Speak to Neloth about Briarheart research. If you already brought a Briar Heart harvested from a Forsworn Briarheart, give it to him and complete `Briarheart Necropsy`; otherwise record the active request and use the next routed Forsworn Briarheart corpse to supply it.
24. After `Reluctant Steward` and `Briarheart Necropsy` are complete, accept `Old Friends` from Neloth. Use the Ring of Tracking around Tel Mithryn graveyard, find Ildari's sarcophagus, take the heart stone, and return to Neloth. Travel to Highpoint Tower, clear the tower, read `Ildari's Journal, vol. I`, `Ildari's Journal, vol. II`, and `Ildari's Journal, vol. III`, defeat Ildari, remove the heart stone when she kneels, then report back to Neloth.
25. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-TEL-MITHRYN-RESEARCH-RADIANTS` before asking Neloth for more research work. Accept `Azra's Staffs`; do not reload for a preferred dungeon. If the marker sends you to Solstheim, travel to that marker. If it sends you to Skyrim, ferry from Raven Rock to Windhelm and use the safest already-open mainland path to the active marker. Enter only the assigned dungeon, go straight to the boss-container or quest-marker objective, take Azra's staff, leave, and return to Neloth for his staff reward. If the assigned dungeon is unreachable because an earlier clear left a required section inaccessible, reload the hard save and record the failed target as bug recovery, not normal target selection.
26. After returning Azra's staff, ask Neloth for more research and complete `Experimental Subject (B)`. Let him cast the silence experiment, wait for the blindness and stamina-drain effect to wear off, then demand compensation and take the 250 gold. If the screen stays black after the conversation, open and close any Black Book from inventory before continuing.
27. If Destruction is at least 40, ask Neloth for more research again and accept `Wind and Sand`. Do not reload for a preferred dungeon. Use the same isolated-radiant rule: follow the active marker directly, enter only the assigned dungeon, recover and read or take `Wind and Sand`, return to Neloth, and collect the gold reward. If Destruction is below 40, write `Wind and Sand - return to Neloth at Destruction 40` in route notes; before the Books, Spells, and Documents vendor-buying pass, return to this exact step, complete the active-marker retrieval, then continue with Talvas.
28. After `Wind and Sand` is complete, check Talvas after his next merchant restock. Buy and read `Spell Tome: Whirlwind Cloak` if he stocks it now; otherwise buy and read it from Talvas during the Books, Spells, and Documents vendor pass.
29. Travel to Revus Sarvani's silt strider camp northwest of Tel Mithryn. Buy the Kagrumez Resonance Gem from Revus. Buy the tame Nix-Hound for 400 gold, read `Spell Tome: Teleport Pet Nix-Hound`, introduce yourself to the Nix-Hound, then send it to Severin Manor.
30. From the north coast, go to the peddler camp north-northwest of Skaal Village and southeast of Haknir's Shoal. Read `Peddler's Journal` on the dead Dark Elf to start `More Than You Can Chew`.
31. Use a saved Potion of Waterwalking for the cold-water crossing to the Riekling island north of the peddler camp. Speak to the Riekling Tribe Leader, receive the Riekling Hunting Party Sketch, and inspect it.
32. Travel to the hunting party on the coast east of the Sun Stone. Help the hunting party kill the Giant Bull Netch, take the Giant Bull Netch Leather, and return to the Riekling Tribe Leader.
33. Give the Giant Bull Netch Leather to the Tribe Leader, take the Key to Riekling Chest, open the nearby chest, and take the full Painted Netch Leather armor set plus `Crafting with Netch Leather`. Read the crafting guide so the Fort Hraggstad shipment objective appears; leave that mainland shipment active for the later Fort Hraggstad route.
34. Travel to Kagrumez with the Nchardak gem and the Revus gem. Enter the ruin, kill the reavers in the main chamber, and take both Kagrumez Resonance Gems plus `Raleth Eldri's Notes on Kagrumez` from the reaver leader.
35. Use the central Kagrumez pedestal and copy the blue-dot pattern shown on the first door. Survive the first Dwemer trial, enter the opened chamber, and speak to the Steadfast Dwarven Spider to unlock it as a follower option; leave it in Kagrumez.
36. Copy the second door's three-gem pattern on the pedestal, survive the second trial, and speak to the Steadfast Dwarven Sphere to unlock it as a follower option; leave it in Kagrumez.
37. Copy the third door's four-gem pattern on the pedestal and survive the final trial. In the treasure chamber, take the Dwarven Black Bow of Fate and the Soul Stealer Arrows beside it. Preserve the bow and keep all four collected Kagrumez Resonance Gems together in safe storage; the fifth gem in Fahlbtharz waits for the Fahlbtharz route.
38. Return to Raven Rock. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-A-NEW-DEBT` before letting Mogrul finish the debt confrontation. When he confronts you about Drovas's debt, choose the nonpayment line once so the debt collectors are enabled; do not threaten him or try the Intimidate discount. Leave Raven Rock on foot on the normal exterior road until Mogrul's thugs approach, kill the debt collectors, loot and read `Mogrul's Orders`, then return to Raven Rock and pay Mogrul the full 1,000 gold. Complete `A New Debt`. If Mogrul or Slitter becomes hostile before the conversation/payment path is stable, reload the hard save. Store the Painted Netch Leather set, Dwarven Black Bow of Fate, Kagrumez Resonance Gems, Staff of Flames, spare Heart Stones, and Black Books at Severin Manor, update Vampire Lord perk progress from this section's hostile kills, and make a rotating manual save.
### Kolbjorn Excavation and Raven Rock West

1. Start from Severin Manor with the Ancient Nordic Pickaxe, hot food, cold gear, lockpicks, a torch or Candlelight, carry space for Ahzidal's relics, and at least 11,000 gold reserved for Ralis's excavation.
2. Speak to Cindiri Arano in Raven Rock and accept `First Edition`. Then travel southeast to Kolbjorn Barrow by Old Attius Farm.
3. At Kolbjorn, take and read `The Journal of Ralis Sedarys - Volume 19` from Ralis's hay pile. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-KOLBJORN-FUND-1`, speak to Ralis Sedarys, and fund the first excavation phase for 1,000 gold.
4. Use the first excavation wait for the southern coast loop. Travel southeast to Hrodulf's House, kill the ash spawn outside, and take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the bottom shelf in the ruined west side of the house.
5. Enter the cellar through the trapdoor. Kill the reavers, read the letter from Bjornolfr on the table, read Hrodulf's Journal on the crate, activate the moveable west bookcase, and follow the tunnel to the Dwemer chamber. Take the unique lit, weightless torch in the tunnel, find Bjornolfr's body and the Bloodstained Letter, then go south from the house to Hrodulf's boat and body on the coast to finish Hrodulf's House.
6. Continue southwest to the Wreck of the Strident Squall. Clear the reavers, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the shelves below deck, and take the `Lusty Argonian Maid Folio` from the chest behind the lower stairs near the hole in the hull.
7. Return to Raven Rock, give the folio to Cindiri to complete `First Edition`, sell the new East Empire Company Pendants to Fethis Alor, store the Hrodulf documents and southern-loop loot, sleep or wait in Raven Rock until the courier brings `Letter from Ralis Sedarys`, and read the letter.
8. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-KOLBJORN-RETURN-1` before entering the barrow after the first letter. At the camp, take and read `The Journal of Ralis Sedarys - Volume 20` from the bench outside the large tent, speak to Ralis, and enter Kolbjorn Barrow.
9. Clear the first draugr phase. In the first large room, take the unique engraved Skull from the altar pressure plate to open the rear rotating door. Go through, take Ahzidal's Boots of Waterwalking from the pedestal, return to Ralis, make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-KOLBJORN-FUND-2`, and fund the next phase for 2,000 gold.
10. Use the second excavation wait for the Raven Rock north loop. Travel northeast from Raven Rock to Brodir Grove, kill the reavers, take Stormfang from the reaver lord, and take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the crate north of the cooking spit under the hanging garlic.
11. Continue north to Damphall Mine. Before entering, go north from the mine to the snowberry patches and wooden platform, kill the rieklings that burst from the barrels, and take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the left-hand crate.
12. Enter Damphall Mine and clear it through the reaver boss and treasure room. Use the boss's key on the master-locked treasure-room gate, loot the boss chest, use the trapdoor shortcut back to the deep water near the entrance, and return to Raven Rock.
13. Store Stormfang and the Damphall loot, sell the Brodir and Damphall East Empire Company Pendants to Fethis, sleep or wait until the courier brings `Letter from Ralis Sedarys 2`, and read the letter.
14. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-KOLBJORN-RETURN-2` before entering the barrow after the second letter. At the camp, take and read `The Journal of Ralis Sedarys - Volume 21` from the bench, speak to Ralis, and enter Kolbjorn Barrow.
15. Clear the second draugr phase and find Bradyn and Mireli. At the four vertical handles near Mireli's route, leave the third handle from the top alone; pull the top handle, the second handle, the bottom handle, and the top handle again. On the back side of the shelf near Mireli's corpse, use the hidden handle to open the secret room and take Ahzidal's Ring of Necromancy. Take and read `Mireli's Letter to Mother` from Mireli's corpse, return to Ralis, make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-KOLBJORN-FUND-3`, and fund the next phase for 3,000 gold.
16. Use the third excavation wait for Coldcinder Cave. From Raven Rock, climb to the highest columnar basalt formation south of the Bulwark and north-northwest of Kolbjorn, enter the trapdoor to Coldcinder Cave, clear the ash spawn, netches, and skeevers, mine the three Heart Stone deposits, exit by the trapdoor ladder or Bulwark-side path, return to Raven Rock, and wait for `Letter from Ralis Sedarys 3`.
17. Read the third letter, then make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-KOLBJORN-RETURN-3` before entering the barrow. At the camp, take and read `The Journal of Ralis Sedarys - Volume 22` from the table inside the large tent, speak to Ralis, and enter Kolbjorn Barrow.
18. Clear the third draugr phase. Pull the chain beside the iron-door route, backtrack to the newly opened pedestal, and take Ahzidal's Gauntlets of Warding. Continue to the pressure-plate chamber, light every floor plate to open the flooded alcove, and take Ahzidal's Ring of Arcana from the pedestal; also check the water pit around the pedestal, since draugr shouts can knock the ring off before you reach it. Return to Ralis, make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-KOLBJORN-FUND-4`, and fund the final phase for 5,000 gold.
19. Sleep or wait in Raven Rock until the courier brings `Letter from Ralis Sedarys 4`, read the letter, and make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-KOLBJORN-RETURN-4` before the final Kolbjorn return.
20. At the final camp, take and read `The Journal of Ralis Sedarys - Volume 23` from the small table inside the large tent. Enter Kolbjorn Barrow, descend to the newly accessible word wall, and learn Cyclone: Gaar.
21. Continue to the throne room. Pull the side chains to reveal the hidden whale symbols, set both rune totems to whales, and pull the lever to open Ahzidal's Armor of Retribution. Take the armor, set both rune totems to eagles, and pull the lever again to open the trapdoor stairs.
22. At the bottom, set both rune totems to snakes and pull the chain to open Ahzidal's Helm of Vision. Take the helm, mine the stalhrim deposit opposite the stairs, continue to Ahzidal's chamber, defeat Ahzidal, and take the Ahzidal dragon priest mask.
23. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-DRAGONBORN-UNEARTHED-RALIS` before choosing Ralis's fate. Branch route first: tell Ralis you cannot let him go unpunished, kill him, and take Hoarfrost from his corpse. Reload `HS-DRAGONBORN-UNEARTHED-RALIS`.
24. Main route: spare Ralis by choosing the dialogue that lets him leave Kolbjorn. He will move to The Retching Netch as a recruitable follower.
25. Use the newly opened exit tunnel. Loot the boss chest, enter the circular Black Book chamber, read `Black Book: Filament and Filigree`, and enter Apocrypha.
26. In Apocrypha, use Candlelight or a torch in the dark stretches. At the first table to the right, read `Three Thieves`. Continue along the lit path, activate the scrye, defeat the seekers and lurkers, read the Black Book at the reward platform, and choose Secret of Arcana.
27. Return to Kolbjorn Barrow. In the room beyond the Black Book chamber, mine the stalhrim-filled sarcophagus, use the lever to raise the shortcut stairs, and leave the barrow.
28. Return to Raven Rock. With no active follower, recruit Ralis once at The Retching Netch, take Hoarfrost through follower trade, then dismiss him so his follower option remains available. Store Hoarfrost, Stormfang, Ahzidal's full relic set, the Ahzidal mask, the engraved Skull, the Ralis journals, Ralis's letters, Mireli's letter, Hrodulf documents, and Black Books at Severin Manor.
29. Sell any new East Empire Company Pendants to Fethis Alor. Update Vampire Lord perk progress from the hostile kills in this section and make a rotating manual save.

### Fahlbtharz, Deathbrand, and Karstaag

1. Start from Severin Manor with the Ancient Nordic Pickaxe, hot food, cold gear, lockpicks, a ranged option, waterbreathing support if you have it, and empty carry space for several unique armor and weapon sets. Before leaving town, go to the downstairs bedroom in Raven Rock Temple and read `Heretic Dossier: Blacksmith's Confessional`.
2. Travel northwest to Fahlbtharz and clear the ruin by the normal route. At the ten-button panel, press the second button from the right on the bottom row.
3. In the boilery, solve the steam puzzle by striking resonators 1, 2, and 5 from left to right. In the Grand Hall, use centurion dynamo cores to open the treasure room, then take the Kagrumez Resonance Gem and Visage of Mzund. Before leaving Fahlbtharz, enter Fahlbtharz Forge, kill Hand Kenro Hlan, and take `Hand Kenro's Note`, the Unenchanted Weapon, Kenro's Helmet, Kenro's Robes, the Fahlbtharz Forge Gem, and at least one Pyroil Tar. Read the note, then use the lift exit north of the Abandoned Lodge, return to Raven Rock, and store the fifth Kagrumez gem with the other four. Store the Kenro disguise, Unenchanted Weapon, Fahlbtharz Forge Gem, and Pyroil Tar for the Tribunal route.
4. Prepare for a long cold north-coast loop, then travel to Haknir's Shoal. Clear the pirate camp, take the Deathbrand Treasure Map from the pirate captain, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox at the back of the middle tent, and take Deathbrand Helm from the ancient chest northwest of the camp.
5. Swim or cross to the Riekling island north of Haknir's Shoal and northwest of Frossel. Clear the rieklings and take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox at the top of the larger Riekling structure, near the knapsack.
6. Follow the Deathbrand map circuit. Go to the river west of Tel Mithryn, near the netches and boulder pile, defeat the ash spawn and treasure hunters, and take Deathbrand Boots from the ancient chest. Then go to the small pine forest north-northwest of the Earth Stone, defeat the reavers, and take Deathbrand Armor. Finally, go to the tidal pool south of Bristleback Cave and near Broken Tusk Mine, clear the mudcrabs, and take Deathbrand Gauntlets. The fourth armor chest gives you the Gyldenhul Barrow Key. Treasure Maps tracking is now 12 of 13 resolved map-and-cache entries.
7. Travel to Horker Island. Clear the horkers, including Lord Tusk, take Horksbane from Saden's body inland from the eastern wreckage, and take the East Empire Company Pendant from the underwater strongbox beside the broken boat on the east coast.
8. Use the Gyldenhul Barrow Key at the barrow entrance on Horker Island. Take and read `Torn Note` from the dead adventurer, mine the western stalhrim deposit with the Ancient Nordic Pickaxe, continue into the treasure corridor, and take Bloodscythe from Haknir's skeleton. Defeat Haknir Death-Brand and his crew, then take Soulrender from Haknir's remains. Preserve the full Deathbrand set, Bloodscythe, Soulrender, and Horksbane.
9. Go to Frossel and clear the cave. Take the East Empire Company Pendant from the expert strongbox on the ground in the easternmost chamber after the first tripwire claw trap.
10. Go north of Frossel to the unmarked giant nirnroot island in the far northeast of Solstheim. Take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox in the boat on the island's eastern side, wedged between a chest and a tipped barrel.
11. Travel to Glacial Cave. Take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox outside, half-buried in the snow between crates just east of the entrance. Enter the cave, clear the rieklings, and take Karstaag's Skull from the back wall.
12. During this north-coast travel, keep `Filial Bonds` active. If Torkild appears naturally, speak to him, kill the werebear, take `Torkild's Letter to Wulf`, and give it to Wulf Wild-Blood at the next Skaal Village stop. If he does not appear here, leave the final search for the pre-Storn visit.
13. Enter Castle Karstaag Caverns from the waterfall-side cave entrance and clear the cavern route to Castle Karstaag Ruins. Loot Esmond Tyne in the first huge chamber for the Bonemold Formula before continuing upward through the caverns.
14. In Castle Karstaag Ruins, clear the rieklings, make a rotating manual save, place Karstaag's Skull on the throne, and defeat Karstaag. You gain the limited-use Summon Karstaag power; preserve all three uses for later unless the guide explicitly spends one.
15. Return to Raven Rock. Give the Bonemold Formula to Glover Mallory to complete `Paid in Full`. Use the house and basement key he gives you, enter Glover Mallory's House and basement, take the East Empire Company Pendant from the strongbox on the small shelf in the northwest corner of the first room, take and read `Glover's Letter`, and take the full Blackguard's Armor set from the basement. Keep `Glover's Letter` for the next Riften return; Sapphire's reward waits until the route is back on the mainland.
16. Sell the new East Empire Company Pendants to Fethis Alor. Keep `Pain in the Necklace` active; the Benkongerike pendant comes with the later Untold Legends route. Store Visage of Mzund, the Kagrumez Resonance Gem, the Deathbrand set, Bloodscythe, Soulrender, Horksbane, Blackguard's Armor, Glover's Letter, and any heavy northern-loop loot at Severin Manor. Update Vampire Lord perk progress from this section's hostile kills and make a rotating manual save.

### Ghosts of the Tribunal and Trueflame

1. Start from Severin Manor with Kenro's Helmet, Kenro's Robes, the Unenchanted Weapon, the first Fahlbtharz Forge Gem, one Pyroil Tar, food, healing, lockpicks, the Amulet of Articulation, and enough carry space for several masks, weapons, and journals. Leave pets outside Ashfall's Tear.
2. Travel north from Raven Rock to Ashfall's Tear. Equip Kenro's Helmet and Kenro's Robes before approaching the inner temple. In the entry puzzle, set the pillars to Almalexia, Sotha Sil, and Vivec, then open the route inward. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-AE-GHOSTS-TEMPLE` before committing to an outcome inside the temple.
3. Branch route: remove the disguise or openly attack the heretics, then clear the temple faithful in Ashfall's Tear. This branch is only to experience the destroyed-temple outcome; it cuts off the Tribunal Armory Key and Skull Crusher, so do not continue from this branch save.
4. Reload `HS-AE-GHOSTS-TEMPLE`. Main route: wear Kenro's disguise, enter peacefully, speak to Matriarch Drevlan, reveal that you want to join, use the Amulet of Articulation on the persuasion check, pray at the Shrine of Almalexia when she tells you to prove loyalty, and speak to her again.
5. Ask Matriarch Drevlan for the Goddess's command. Take and read `Masks of the Tribunal`, which starts `Buyer Beware`, `Careless Curation`, and `Her Word Against Theirs`.
6. While Ashfall's Tear is peaceful, collect the temple documents that are not tied to follower inventory: `Matriarch Drevlan's Journal` from Drevlan's room, `Sculptor Visthar's Journal` from the sculpture room, and `Priest Drureth's Journal` from the alchemy and enchanting room.
7. Speak to Priest Drureth in Ashfall's Tear and receive the `Propaganda Letter`. Return to Raven Rock, give the letter to Aphia Velothi for translation, wait two hours, and take the translated letters back from her.
8. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-AE-GHOSTS-PROPAGANDA` before choosing who receives the translated letters.
9. Branch route: give the translated letters to the Reclamation Priest in Raven Rock Temple. Complete `Buyer Beware`, `Careless Curation`, the ash-zombie return to Ashfall's Tear, and `Ashen Heart` by following the same site instructions in the main route below, but do not collect or store any branch loot as your kept copy. After `Ashen Heart`, return to Ashfall's Tear once more, defeat the Reclamation Priest and Redoran guards when they attack the heretics, take `Reclamation Priest's Journal`, then reload `HS-AE-GHOSTS-PROPAGANDA`.
10. Main route: distribute the translated letters to Raven Rock civilians, giving one to Geldis Sadri at The Retching Netch and the other three to Cindiri Arano, Fethis Alor, and Milore Ienth. Return to Ashfall's Tear, speak to Matriarch Drevlan, receive `Letter from Matriarch Drevlan`, and complete `Her Word Against Theirs`.
11. For `Buyer Beware`, go to the top of the Bulwark near Raven Rock's south gate before midnight, face toward Old Attius Farm, and shoot down the circling Felsaad Tern. Loot and read `Note on the Target`.
12. Follow the Redoran Guard to Old Attius Farm Cellar. Enter the cellar through the trapdoor in the ruined farmhouse, kill the Redoran Guard and the Buyer, and take the Mask of Vivec, the second Fahlbtharz Forge Gem, the Attius Farm Cellar Key, and `Redoran Guard's Note`.
13. For `Careless Curation`, speak to Aphia Velothi in Raven Rock about the missing curate. Take and read `Curate Melita's Plea`.
14. Take the ferry to Windhelm, use Candlehearth Hall or Gallows Hall as the mainland support stop, then travel to Stony Creek Cave. The cave was cleared during the Bards College route; go directly to the waterfall chamber at the back, rescue Curate Melita, and take the Mask of Sotha Sil.
15. Before returning to Solstheim, go to Riften. Find Sapphire in the Thieves Guild area, give her `Glover's Letter`, receive the Exquisite Sapphire, and preserve it with your other unique valuables.
16. While you are on the mainland with `Crafting with Netch Leather` already read, travel to Solitude and then to Fort Hraggstad. Use the cleared fort entrance, search the Netch Leather shipment chest just inside the fort, and take the Netch Leather armor pieces and the Boots of Blinding Speed. Preserve the boots and one copy of each Netch Leather piece.
17. Return by Windhelm ferry to Raven Rock, then go back to Ashfall's Tear. Clear the ash zombies attacking the temple, speak to Matriarch Drevlan, and receive the Tribunal Armory Key. This also unlocks Her Hand and Indoril crafting for the later Smithing block.
18. Enter the armory. Take the Mask of Almalexia, Light of Day, Magebane, Skull Crusher, and the third Fahlbtharz Forge Gem. Read `Priest Arthamis' Note`.
19. Start `Ashen Heart` by speaking to Matriarch Drevlan and reading `Matriarch Drevlan's Note`. In the armory puzzle, place the Mask of Sotha Sil, Mask of Almalexia, and Mask of Vivec on the mannequins from left to right. Open the rear chest, take Hopesfire and `Erden Relvel's Note`, and read the note.
20. Travel to the Tel Mithryn graveyard. Defeat Erden Relvel and the ash zombies, then take the Mask of Dagoth Ur, Robe of the Lich, and the fourth Fahlbtharz Forge Gem from Erden.
21. Return to Ashfall's Tear after `Ashen Heart`. Recruit Vesparth the Toe, trade for the Cleaver of St. Felms and `Vesparth's Journal`, then dismiss her. Recruit Hand Kydren Indobar, trade for `Hand Indobar's Journal`, then dismiss him. Speak with Hand Ethra Mavandas and Watchman Sindras so their follower availability is established without replacing your main follower plan. Curate Melita, Caretaker Ineril, the Ashfall blacksmith, and the restored Ashfall services are now available on the main save.
22. Return to Fahlbtharz Forge with the Unenchanted Weapon, the four Fahlbtharz Forge Gems, and Pyroil Tar. Place all four gems in the forge panel, apply Pyroil Tar to the Unenchanted Weapon, place the weapon in the sword slot, activate the forge, and take Trueflame.
23. Return to Severin Manor. Store the Tribunal masks, Robe of the Lich, Kenro's Helmet, Kenro's Robes, Trueflame, Hopesfire, Light of Day, Magebane, Skull Crusher, Cleaver of St. Felms, Exquisite Sapphire, Boots of Blinding Speed, the Netch Leather pieces, the Ghosts journals and notes, and any heavy Ashfall loot. Preserve one copy of any ordinary Ghosts equipment you acquired, including Common Robes, Extravagant Robes, Her Hand pieces, Indoril pieces, an Ordinator Shield, a Redoran Watchman's Helmet, an Ebony Mace, or an Ebony Scimitar. Tribunal Priest Robes and the separate Dwarven Forge Gem variants are not obtainable in normal play. Update Vampire Lord perk progress from the hostile kills in this section and make a rotating manual save.

### Bittercup, the Pit, and Fortunate Son

1. Start at Severin Manor. Store heavy Solstheim loot, buy or take one bottle of Sujamma in Raven Rock, and carry food, healing, lockpicks, stealth or pickpocket support, the Amulet of Articulation, and enough free carry space for the Bittercup reward set.
2. Before leaving Solstheim, go to Skaal Village and speak to Morwen. Accept Bera's Necklace for Runil.
3. Take the Raven Rock ferry to Windhelm, then use carriage and road travel to reach Falkreath. Deliver Bera's Necklace to Runil in Falkreath's Hall of the Dead, then rent a room at Dead Man's Drink and read and take `Mysterious Altar` from the rented room to start `A Dying Wish`.
4. Ride north of Falkreath to Falkreath Watchtower. Clear the necromancer from the tower, then read and take `Liminal Bridges` from the tent at the top.
5. Continue to Roadside Ruins. Clear the spriggan, read and take `Catalogue of Weapon Enchantments` from the chest in the center of the ruin, then stand at the Bittercup altar.
6. Make `HARD SAVE: HS-AE-BITTERCUP-ALTAR` before filling any bowl.
7. Branch route: choose the bowl of Power, sleep at the nearby bedroll, and complete `The Pit`. In the starting dungeon, take and read `Prisoner's Note` from the bucket in the cell. In the arena, defeat the Pit Fighter, wolf, sabre cats, and Neski Skull-Sabre. Loot Neski for the Bittercup, Grand Champion's Sword, Grand Champion's Helm, and `On the Bittercup`.
8. Still on the Power branch, drink this branch-copy of the Bittercup once, then continue into Watchtower Barracks. Kill the Pit Boss and bandits, take the Pit Key, read and take `Pit Fighter's Note` beside the belongings chest, recover your belongings, leave the fort, and reload `HS-AE-BITTERCUP-ALTAR`.
9. Branch route: choose the bowl of Nothing and return to Falkreath until the courier gives you `Note from the Temple of Kynareth`. Read it, travel to Dawnstar, speak to Harlaug at the docks, and take his ferry to Giant's Tooth.
10. On Giant's Tooth, reach Iron Tusk Cave, harvest Ironwood Fruit from the Iron Wood tree, defeat the spriggans in the cave, fight back through the ice wraiths outside, and return by Harlaug's ferry. Take Salt Pile from the island fish barrels if you need one for the soup.
11. In Dawnstar, use Silus Vesuius's House cooking station to cook Ironwood Soup from Ironwood Fruit, Charred Skeever Hide, and Salt Pile. Travel to Whiterun, give the soup to Acolyte Aldren in the Temple of Kynareth, wait one full day, then speak to Rulnik Wind-Strider. Receive Rulnik's Dagger, the Bittercup, and `On the Bittercup`, speak to Rulnik once so his follower availability is established, then reload `HS-AE-BITTERCUP-ALTAR`.
12. Main route: choose the bowl of Fortune, sleep at the nearby bedroll, then read and take `Rogue's Job Offer` and keep the Spiked Vial. `A Dying Wish` ends and `Fortunate Son` begins.
13. Travel to Markarth and enter the Silver-Blood Inn. Use one of the inn's cooking pots to combine the Sujamma with the Spiked Vial into Spiked Sujamma. Speak to Kleppr or Frabbi and give or sell them the Spiked Sujamma for Inimoro.
14. Wait in the Silver-Blood Inn until Inimoro drinks the Spiked Sujamma and becomes sick. Make a rotating manual save, then pickpocket Cuinanthil's Family Seal from Cuinanthil.
15. Go to the Warrens. Find the dead courier, read and take `Rogue's Instructions`, take the Courier's Clothes and Courier's Boots, create `Letter from Cuinanthil's Steward` with the family seal, equip the courier disguise, and return to Cuinanthil in the Silver-Blood Inn.
16. Use the courier lines `I've been looking for you...` and `Got something I'm supposed to deliver - your hands only.` After Cuinanthil accepts the fake letter, let the inn fight play out, then follow Cuinanthil back to the Warrens and into the hidden vault.
17. In the vault, let Ernanthil confront and kill Cuinanthil. Read and take `Cuinanthil's Journal`, take Key to Eslaf's Room from Cuinanthil, then speak to Ernanthil for the 1000 gold reward, the Bittercup, and `On the Bittercup`.
18. After the reward dialogue, kill Ernanthil in the vault and read and take `Ernanthil's Journal`. Take and read `Spell Tome: Master Transmute` from the large chest in Eslaf's room, loot the vault, and complete `Fortunate Son`.
19. Preserve the main-route Bittercup unconsumed. Return to a safe storage stop and store the Bittercup, `On the Bittercup`, Cuinanthil's Family Seal, Courier's Clothes, Courier's Boots, Key to Eslaf's Room, `Cuinanthil's Journal`, `Ernanthil's Journal`, `Mysterious Altar`, `Rogue's Job Offer`, `Rogue's Instructions`, and `Letter from Cuinanthil's Steward`. The Bittercup item-list entries named Clothes, Ferryman's Ring, Skull-Sabre Shield, and Healing Salts are not obtainable in normal play. Update Vampire Lord perk progress from this section's hostile kills and make a rotating manual save.

### Miraak Finale, Lost Knowledge, and Last Black Books

1. Begin this section only at level 60 or higher, with `Black Book: Epistolary Acumen`, `Black Book: Waking Dreams`, enough dragon souls to unlock the final Bend Will word, Scholar's Insight still active, hot food, healing, lockpicks, and room for Miraak's equipment and two more Black Book routes. Sleep at Severin Manor if a level-up is waiting.
2. Visit Skaal Village before speaking to Storn. Speak to Morwen, receive her Nordic carved helmet reward for Bera's Necklace, and unlock Morwen as a non-default spouse option.
3. Close `Filial Bonds` before advancing the Dragonborn finale. If `Torkild's Letter to Wulf` is already in your inventory, give it to Wulf Wild-Blood now.
